Motivation: 非侵入式神经记录信噪比低，难以精细重建音素或单词；神经科学证据表明高层语义表征分布于皮层区域且时间尺度较慢，因此假设语义内容比低层声学或词汇特征更适合作为非侵入式解码目标。

Method: 提出Brain2Semantics2Text模型，将句子级MEG响应映射到语义流形空间，再将预测的语义嵌入逆转为自然语言文本；通过语义瓶颈机制避免词级对齐，直接恢复高层语义含义。

Result: 该方法成功实现了从MEG信号到文本的语义级重建，在句子层面优于 prior 非侵入式Brain2Text方法，验证了语义中间表示的有效性。

Conclusion: 利用语义嵌入作为中间表示可有效克服非侵入式脑机接口中的信噪比限制，为高层语义解码提供新范式，显著提升句子级文本重建性能。

Abstract: Non-invasive speech decoding remains constrained by the low signal-to-noise ratio of neural recordings, which makes fine-grained reconstruction of phonemes or individual words difficult. Motivated by neuroscientific evidence that high-level semantic representations are distributed across cortical regions and evolve over slower temporal scales, we hypothesize that semantic content may provide a more suitable target for non-invasive decoding than low-level acoustic or lexical features. We introduce Brain2Semantics2Text, a method that reconstructs text through an intermediate semantic embedding space. Our model maps sentence-level MEG responses into a semantic manifold and then inverts the predicted embeddings into natural language. This semantic bottleneck enables recovery of high-level meaning without word-level alignment. We describe the core principles of the approach, its implementation, and the strategies used to mitigate the challenges of learning a reliable neural-to-semantic mapping. Finally, we compare against prior non-invasive Brain2Text methods and show improved sentence-level results.

</details>


### [140] [RiLM: Parameter-Efficient Language Modeling via Geodesic Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10305)
*Fang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出黎曼语言模型（RiLM），通过移除传统的输出矩阵，利用黎曼流形上的测地线距离进行解码，显著降低了小模型参数量。在双曲空间实例化（HypRiLM）后，其在WikiText-2上的困惑度（54.2）优于传统LSTM、Transformer及SSM基线约两倍，证明了基于几何的解码在小模型边缘部署中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对参数量低于一百万的语言模型在边缘部署、领域适应及可复现研究中的重要性，解决传统小模型（如两层LSTM或Transformer）中输出矩阵（$W_{out}$）占用约三分之一容量导致效率低下的问题。

Method: 提出黎曼语言模型（RiLM）框架，完全移除输出层。将上下文展开为黎曼流形上的轨迹，通过当前状态与词汇嵌入之间的平方测地线距离计算下一个token的概率，实现输入输出嵌入映射共享及“解码即几何”。具体实例化为平坦空间（Flat RiLM）和庞加莱球双曲空间（HypRiLM），并引入Mobius稳定化技术解决朴素双曲递归中的边界坍塌问题。

Result: 在WikiText-2数据集上（d=128, |V|=2000），HypRiLM达到54.2 +/- 0.2的验证困惑度，显著优于Flat RiLM（87.6）及匹配的LSTM、Transformer和SSM基线（113-147）。HypRiLM表现比最强的绑定循环基线（SSM）高出约两倍。在Penn Treebank及10k词汇量压力测试中，验证了测地线解码的迁移能力及双曲曲率的选择性优势。

Conclusion: 基于黎曼几何（特别是双曲空间）的解码机制能有效提升小参数语言模型的性能，解决了输出矩阵冗余问题并恢复了双曲空间的可训练性。该方法在受控的小模型对比中表现卓越，虽未追求全词汇量的最先进水平，但为边缘计算和特定领域的应用提供了高效的新范式。

Abstract: Language models under one million parameters matter for edge deployment, domain adaptation, and reproducible research, yet a two-layer LSTM or Transformer at embedding width d = 128 still spends roughly one third of its capacity on the output matrix W_out in R^(d x |V|). We propose Riemannian Language Models (RiLM), which remove that layer entirely: context unfolds as a trajectory on a Riemannian manifold, and next-token probabilities arise from squared geodesic distance between the current state and vocabulary embeddings. The same embedding map serves input and output -- decoding is geometry. We instantiate the framework on flat R^d (Flat RiLM) and the Poincare ball H^d (HypRiLM) with a shared MLP composition map phi (~290k parameters, d = 128, |V| = 2000). Across five seeds on WikiText-2, HypRiLM reaches 54.2 +/- 0.2 validation perplexity versus 87.6 +/- 0.6 for Flat RiLM; tied and matched LSTM, Transformer, and SSM controls remain at 113-147 PPL on WT-2 -- HypRiLM leads by roughly 2x over the strongest tied recurrent baseline (SSM, 113.0 +/- 3.8). Penn Treebank and a 10k-vocabulary stress test confirm that geodesic decoding transfers across corpora and larger |V|, while hyperbolic curvature helps selectively. We also characterize boundary collapse in naive hyperbolic recurrence and show how Mobius stabilization restores trainability. Claims are scoped to controlled small-model comparisons, not full-vocabulary state of the art.

</details>


### [141] [On-Policy Distillation for Vision-Language Model Adaptation, an Effective Paradigm on Low-Quality Multimodal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10321)
*Hongyuan Zhang,Xianda Guo,Yanlun Peng,Qianlong Yang,Yubin Guo,Pinhan Fu,Mulin Chen,Xiaozhen Qiao,Ping Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出OnPoKD，首个将“在策略”（on-policy）蒸馏应用于视觉语言模型适应的框架，通过轻量级控制器动态构建样本级自适应蒸馏目标，以解决现有方法在类别和域偏移下目标不可靠的问题，显著提升了泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉语言蒸馏方法通常使用固定的教师预测作为统一的训练目标，这种方法在面对类别偏移和域偏移时可靠性不足，无法适应不同样本的可靠性和训练阶段的变化。

Method: 提出OnPoKD框架，引入一个轻量级策略控制器。该控制器利用教师模型、学生模型和零-shot先验提供的可靠性和分歧线索，动态平衡教师监督、零-shot先验引导和硬标签锚定，从而为每个样本构建自适应的蒸馏目标。控制器通过验证反馈进行更新，以优化迁移能力而非仅仅拟合训练分布。

Result: 在Base-to-novel泛化和跨数据集迁移基准上的大量实验表明，OnPoKD consistently优于现有的强视觉语言蒸馏基线方法。

Conclusion: OnPoKD通过将蒸馏目标构建视为动态策略决策，成功解决了固定目标在分布偏移下的局限。该框架可无缝集成到现有流程中，不改变推理架构或增加测试成本，显著提升了视觉语言模型的适应性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation offers an efficient route to transfer a task-adapted vision-language teacher to a compact student. The training target in current vision-language distillation methods is typically constructed from the teacher prediction and applied uniformly to all training samples, making it unreliable under class and domain shifts. In this paper, we argue that distillation target construction should be treated as a dynamic training decision rather than a fixed recipe. To this end, we propose OnPoKD, an on-policy distillation framework for vision-language model adaptation. To the best of our knowledge, OnPoKD is the first framework that applies on-policy distillation to vision-language model adaptation by learning target construction as a policy decision. OnPoKD learns a lightweight controller that constructs sample-wise adaptive targets using reliability and disagreement cues from the teacher model, student model, and zero-shot prior. Instead of relying on a fixed teacher prediction, the controller dynamically balances teacher supervision, zero-shot prior guidance, and hard-label anchoring through bounded policy actions, allowing the distillation target to adapt to varying sample reliability and training stages. The policy controller is updated with validation feedback, encouraging target construction to optimize transferability rather than merely fitting the training distribution. Since the controller is only used during training, OnPoKD can be seamlessly integrated into existing vision-language distillation pipelines while preserving the original inference architecture and test-time cost. Extensive experiments on Base-to-novel generalization and Cross-dataset transfer benchmarks show that OnPoKD consistently improves over strong vision-language distillation baselines.

</details>


### [142] [Rosetta at AlexandriaX-2026: LoRA-Adapted NileChat for Context-Aware Dialectal Arabic Dialogue Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10395)
*Nada Esmaeil,Fathima Rena,Sibi Subhash,Osama Elgendy,Mina Naguib,Salma Omar,Muhammad Arif*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Rosetta系统，该系统通过微调NileChat-3B的LoRA适配器，利用结构化提示进行上下文感知的英语到阿拉伯方言对话翻译；实验表明外部预训练仅在少数方言中有效，且因负迁移导致整体性能略有下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决亚历山大X共享任务（AlexandriaX）中子任务1的挑战，即实现上下文感知的英语到阿拉伯方言对话翻译，并探索在受限与不受限轨道下的模型优化策略。

Method: 基于NileChat-3B模型，微调LoRA适配器，使用结构化系统/用户提示以根据方言和对话上下文调节生成；在不受限轨道中，额外在MADAR和PADIC数据集上进行预训练。

Result: Rosetta在受限轨道中排名第4（spBLEU 26.10），在不受限轨道中排名第5（spBLEU 25.09）；外部预训练仅对13种方言中的2种有帮助，却略微降低了整体性能。

Conclusion: 外部预训练在该任务中引发了负迁移现象，表明并非所有额外的预训练数据都能提升多方言翻译性能，需谨慎选择预训练策略。

Abstract: This paper describes the Rosetta system for Subtask 1 (Context-Aware English-to-Dialectal Arabic Dialogue Translation) of the AlexandriaX shared task, participating in both constrained and unconstrained tracks. The approach fine-tunes a LoRA adapter on NileChat-3B using structured system/user prompts that condition generation on dialect and dialogue context. For the unconstrained track, the adapter is additionally pretrained on MADAR and PADIC. Rosetta ranked 4th in the constrained track (spBLEU 26.10) and 5th in the unconstrained track (spBLEU 25.09). The experimental results demonstrate that external pretraining helps only two of thirteen dialects while slightly hurting overall performance, suggesting negative transfer.

</details>


### [143] [Can Foundation Models Moderate Online Content? Evaluating Instruction- vs. Example-Driven Policy Operationalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10410)
*Ayan Majumdar,Shounak Paul,Pushpdeep Singh,Ines Abdelaziz,Sayeh Jarollahi,Seungeon Lee,Krishna P. Gummadi,Ingmar Weber,Abhisek Dash*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过新基准 ModerationBench 对比了指令驱动和示例驱动两种范式，发现基础视觉语言模型（VLM）在内容审核任务上显著优于现有系统，F1 分数提升近三倍，且两种范式效果相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内容审核政策日益复杂，难以一致地执行；虽然基础模型具备相应能力，但其在实际在线内容审核中的可靠性尚未经过系统验证。

Method: 构建了包含 4000 条 Bluesky 平台人工标注真实帖子的新基准 ModerationBench，系统对比了指令驱动（基于政策原则推理）和示例驱动（基于先例泛化）两种视觉语言模型（VLM）引导范式。

Result: 基础模型在随机帖子上的 F1 分数达到 0.60，远超 Bluesky 现有系统的 0.22（提升近三倍）；指令驱动与示例驱动两种范式均能达到相近的峰值效果。

Conclusion: 基础模型为实现大规模、可靠且灵活的政策执行提供了可行路径，两种引导范式均具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: The growing complexity of content moderation policies presents a critical challenge for their consistent operationalization. While foundation models possess the basic capabilities needed to confront this challenge, whether they can reliably moderate online content remains an unanswered question. In this paper, we systematically compare two competing paradigms for Vision-Language Model (VLM) guidance: an instruction-driven approach where models reason from policy precepts, and an example-driven approach where they generalize from prior precedents. We ground this investigation in ModerationBench, a new benchmark of 4,000 manually annotated, in-the-wild posts from the Bluesky platform. Our experiments reveal that foundation models can substantially outperform Bluesky's deployed moderation system, nearly tripling its $F_1$ score (0.60 vs. 0.22) on Random Posts in the benchmark, with both instruction- and example-driven paradigms achieving comparable peak effectiveness. Our findings thus chart a path toward reliable and adaptable policy operationalization at scale.

</details>


### [144] [Do speech foundation models really learn words?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10434)
*Robin Huo,Ewan Dunbar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过残差化去除音素信息，证明HuBERT和wav2vec 2.0等自监督语音基础模型在深层确实学习到了独立于局部语音内容的词表征，且该解耦方法能提升词发现任务中的高阶语言信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注模型对音素和词的判别能力，但良好的词判别可能仅源于对词形（音素）的编码，而非真正独立于语音形式的、包含身份或句法语义属性的词表征。需验证模型是否真正学习了形式独立的词表示。

Method: 采用残差化（residualization）技术从模型表征中剔除音素信息，从而分离并分析剩余表征中是否包含独立于局部语音内容的词级信息，并在词发现任务中评估该解耦表征的效果。

Result: 在HuBERT和wav2vec 2.0的深层网络中，去除音素信息后仍存在能高保真编码词身份的表征；这种简单的解耦方法显著增强了词发现任务中的高阶语言信息提取能力。

Conclusion: 自监督语音基础模型不仅编码语音形式，还在深层学习了独立于音素的词级语义/句法表征；通过残差化解耦可有效提取并利用此类高阶语言信息，推动语音理解任务的发展。

Abstract: Self-supervised speech foundation models are now used in a wide array of downstream applications, including traditional speech recognition and as the basis for tokens in speech-aware language models. Attempts to understand their usefulness have largely focused on probing their representations' ability to discriminate phonemes and words. However, discriminative ability for words need not imply specialized representation of words per se. Good discrimination of words may be explained by good encoding of word form (phonemes) rather than form-independent word representations encoding identity or syntactic/semantic properties. By partialling out phoneme information using residualization, we show that, in later layers, HuBERT and wav2vec 2.0 do in general learn representations which encode words with reasonable fidelity independently of local phonetic content. We show that this simple approach to disentanglement can enhance higher-order linguistic information in word discovery tasks.

</details>


### [145] [Building Multilingual Bridges: Data Mixing as the Pillar of Generalization for In-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10445)
*Mehrnaz Mofakhami,Ananya Sahu,Alejandro R. Salamanca,Daniel D'souza,Alexandre Berard,Thomas Euyang,Marzieh Fadaee,Julia Kreutzer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了L2推理概念，即模型能够使用用户提示的语言进行推理。通过数据中心的优化方法（数据组成和调度），构建了3.35B的Tiny Aya L2-Thinker模型，在60种语言的6个基准测试中实现了超过93%的L2推理率，证明了推理能力可以通过数据混合跨语言迁移，无需每种目标语言都有推理监督数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理语言模型严重偏向英语，无论提示语言如何主要用英语推理。这不仅对非英语用户不友好，还可能导致原始问题意图丢失，并忽略了更适合用目标语言表达的知识。

Method: 采用数据中心的方法，研究如何优化监督微调（SFT）中的数据组成和调度以实现推理泛化。构建了Tiny Aya L2-Thinker模型（3.35B规模），利用广泛的语言覆盖、多语言非推理数据和充足的英语推理骨干进行训练。

Result: 在涵盖数学、常识推理、指令遵循、开放生成和文化推理的6个基准测试中，该模型在60种语言上的L2推理率超过93%，同时保持了强劲的整体性能。

Conclusion: 推理是一种与语言无关的行为，可以通过精心设计的数据混合在类型学多样的语言间迁移，无需在每种目标语言中提供推理监督数据。研究路径表明，更广泛的语言覆盖、现成的多语言非推理数据和坚实的英语推理基础是实现泛化的关键。

Abstract: Reasoning language models have made substantial advances on a variety of complex tasks, yet their capabilities remain overwhelmingly English-centric: models primarily reason in English regardless of the language they are prompted in. This is inaccessible for non-English-speaking users, risks losing the intent of the original question, and forgoes knowledge more readily expressed in the target language. In this work, we advance L2 reasoning, the ability of a model to reason consistently in the language of the user's prompt, thus building an in-language bridge between the prompt and the answer. We approach this problem from a data-centric angle, investigating how to optimize data composition and scheduling in SFT for reasoning generalization. Building Tiny Aya L2-Thinker at 3.35B scale, we achieve an L2 reasoning rate above 93% across 60 languages on 6 benchmarks spanning math, commonsense reasoning, instruction following, open-ended generation, and cultural reasoning while keeping performance strong. We show the path to generalizing L2 reasoning to held-out languages goes through broader language coverage, readily available multilingual non-reasoning data, and a sufficient English reasoning backbone. These findings indicate that reasoning is a language-agnostic behavior that can be transferred across typologically diverse languages through careful data mixing and without requiring reasoning supervision in every target language. We release our model weights and multilingual reasoning data to support further research on accessible, in-language reasoning.

</details>


### [146] [IBIB: A Protocol for Measuring Enterprise AI Systems by Serving Route, Not Model Identifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10494)
*Blake Stenstrom,Charangan Vasantharajan,Brian Sathianathan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出当前大模型基准测试存在测量误差，即仅通过模型标识符评分而忽略了实际部署系统的权重、服务路由、精度等关键因素。为此，作者提出了IB2协议，包含能力绑定预检、包含可靠性的评分规则及盲审裁决机制。实验表明，能力可用性是可测量的，服务臂的选择显著影响精度，且排除失败响应会改变结论排序，因此基准测试应报告区间分辨率组而非单一排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有18个审计基准测试中普遍存在的“测量误差”问题：这些测试仅针对广告中的模型标识符打分，而忽略了企业实际部署系统中权重、服务路由、精度、输出契约及Harness（测试框架）等共同决定可用能力的因素，导致评估结果无法反映真实系统的表现。

Method: 提出名为IB2的协议，包含三个核心部分：1) 金盲能力绑定预检（gold-blind capability-binding preflight），在任务执行前验证路由能否执行评估契约；2) 包含可靠性的首过评分规则（reliability-inclusive first-pass scoring rule），将失败计入分数同时排除不支持的能力；3) 结构上对分数盲视的裁决机制（structurally score-blind adjudication）。该协议包含算法、分类表、请求契约及清单模式，并通过128个锁定任务和987个断言进行实例化验证。

Result: 在11个系统上的实验得出四个主要结果：1) 能力可用性是可测量的，相同权重的运行可能因绑定门限不同而产生不同结果；2) 区分度不均匀，部分测试套件在六系统范围内饱和，差异主要源于受管的数据库工作和多表连接；3) 服务臂的选择显著影响精度（从77.38%提升至82.54%）；4) 从分母中排除失败响应会改变点排序，证明包含可靠性会改变结论而非仅仅是措辞。

Conclusion: 现有的基准测试方法存在缺陷，广告标识符无法暴露系统极限。评估应报告基于区间的分辨率组而非单一排名，且必须将可靠性纳入评分体系，因为服务架构和测试框架的属性（如Harness生成）会显著影响最终评估结果，程序本身即为核心产物而非测试语料。

Abstract: Enterprises deploy systems, not checkpoints. Usable capability depends jointly on weights, serving route, precision, output contract, and harness, yet all 18 audited benchmarks score advertised model identifiers. We treat this as measurement error and give a protocol that makes it reportable. It has three parts. A gold-blind capability-binding preflight verifies that a route can execute the evaluation contract before any task reaches it; a reliability-inclusive first-pass scoring rule keeps failure in the score while keeping unsupported capability out; and adjudication is structurally score-blind. We call the protocol IB2 and release its algorithms, classification tables, request contract, and manifest schemas. Its reference instantiation, 128 locked tasks and 987 assertions over document, spreadsheet, chart, tool and database work, stays sealed: the procedure is the artifact, not the corpus. Across eleven systems, four results. Capability availability is measurable: two complete single-route runs on identical weights later failed distinct predicates of the finalized binding gate, while a third passed that gate before a fresh run. The advertised identifier exposed neither limit. Discrimination is not uniform: four of seven suites saturate under a six-system band, with the spread almost entirely from governed database work and multi-tab joins, so we report interval-backed resolution groups, not ranks; two of the nominal five-label output's four cuts fail multiplicity adjustment. Serving-arm choice moved one declared revision and precision from 77.38 to 82.54, paired interval [0.11,10.60], though the arms differ in access mode, harness generation, and the serving tool-call parser, and harness generation is a property of our evaluator, not any endpoint. Excluding failed responses from denominators changes the point ordering, so reliability inclusion changes a conclusion, not its wording.

</details>


### [147] [IdeaAMBIG: Benchmarking Implementation-Critical Gaps in Research-Idea Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10539)
*Yiling Ma,Yilun Zhao,Sihong Wu,Manasi Patwardhan,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出IdeaAMBIG基准，评估大模型在研究方法规格说明中的“编码就绪性”，发现缺陷定位是主要瓶颈，而一旦明确缺陷，模型生成澄清行动的能力显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多研究想法虽新颖合理，但其方法描述往往缺乏足够细节，导致无法被准确复现或编码实现；现有工作缺乏对“编码就绪性”的系统评估基准。

Method: 构建IdeaAMBIG基准，包含660个证据支持的实例（163个来自真实复现报告与GitHub问题的现实缺口，497个在编码就绪参考中注入的合成缺口），评估13个大模型在三项任务上的表现：编码就绪性评估、缺陷定位、澄清行动生成。

Result: 最佳模型在真实实例上的宏观缺陷恢复率仅为9.6%，但在已知缺陷前提下，宏观澄清行动成功率高达80.6%；若提供黄金解决方案，下游编码就绪率可从14%提升至98%。

Conclusion: 当前大模型在研究方法规格说明中的主要瓶颈在于缺陷定位能力不足，而非澄清行动生成；提升缺陷识别精度是改善科研方法可编码性的关键。

Abstract: A research idea may be novel, coherent, and scientifically plausible, yet its proposed method may remain insufficiently specified for faithful implementation. We study the codification readiness of implementation-facing research-method specifications, defined by whether they provide sufficient methodological information for a competent implementer or coding agent to construct the intended method without unsupported assumptions. We construct evidence-grounded specifications and their supported resolutions from papers, codebases, issue threads, and reproduction artifacts. We introduce IdeaAMBIG, a benchmark of 660 evidence-grounded instances: 163 real-world gaps from reproducibility reports and GitHub issues, and 497 controlled synthetic gaps injected into codification-ready references. IdeaAMBIG evaluates three capabilities: codification-readiness assessment, defect localization, and clarification action generation. Defect localization receives only the specification, whereas clarification additionally receives the annotated defect. Across 13 LLMs, the best model achieves 9.6% Macro Defect Recovery Rate on real-world instances but 80.6% Macro Clarification Action Success Rate when given the defect. In an oracle study, supplying the gold resolution raises the downstream codification-ready rate from 14% to 98%. Across all evaluated models, defect localization is the main bottleneck, with stronger clarification given the defect.

</details>


### [148] [Every Activation Boosted: Scaling General Reasoner to 1 Trillion Open Language Foundation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.22115)
*Ling Team,Ang Li,Ben Liu,Binbin Hu,Bing Li,Bingwei Zeng,Borui Ye,Caizhi Tang,Changxin Tian,Chao Huang,Chao Zhang,Chen Qian,Chenchen Ju,Chenchen Li,Chengfu Tang,Chilin Fu,Chunshao Ren,Chunwei Wu,Cong Zhang,Cunyin Peng,Dafeng Xu,Daixin Wang,Dalong Zhang,Dingnan Jin,Dingyuan Zhu,Dongke Hu,Fangzheng Zhao,Feifan Wu,Feng Zhu,Gangshan Wang,Haitao Zhang,Hailin Zhao,Hanxiao Zhang,Hanzi Wang,Hao Qian,Haoyi Yu,Heng Zhang,Hongliang Zhang,Hongzhi Luan,Huirong Dong,Huizhong Li,Jia Li,Jia Liu,Jialong Zhu,Jian Sha,Jianping Wei,Jiaolong Yang,Jieyue Ma,Jiewei Wu,Jinjing Huang,Jingyun Tian,Jingyuan Zhang,Jinquan Sun,Juanhui Tu,Jun Liu,Jun Xu,Jun Zhou,Junjie Ou,Junpeng Fang,Kaihong Zhang,Kaiqin Hu,Ke Shi,Kun Tang,Kunlong Chen,Lanyin Mei,Lei Liang,Lei Xu,Libo Zhang,Lin Ju,Lin Yuan,Ling Zhong,Lintao Ma,Lu Liu,Lu Yu,Lun Cai,Meiqi Zhu,Mengying Li,Min Chen,Minghao Xue,Minghong Cai,Mingming Yin,Peijie Jiang,Peilong Zhao,Pingping Liu,Qian Zhao,Qing Cui,Qingxiang Huang,Qingyuan Yang,Quankun Yu,Shaowei Wei,Shijie Lian,Shoujian Zheng,Shun Song,Shungen Zhang,Shuo Zhang,Siyuan Li,Song Liu,Ting Guo,Tong Zhao,Wanli Gu,Weichang Wu,Weiguang Han,Wenjing Fang,Wubin Wang,Xiang Shu,Xiao Shi,Xiaoshun Lan,Xiaolu Zhang,Xiaqing Sun,Xin Zhao,Xingyu Lu,Xiong Xu,Xudong Wang,Xudong Wang,Xuemin Yang,Yajie Yang,Yang Xiang,Yanzhe Li,Yi Zhang,Yilong Wang,Yingxue Li,Yongzhen Guo,Yuzhuo Fu,Yuanyuan Wang,Yue Yang,Yue Yu,Yufeng Deng,Yun Zhang,Yunfei Yu,Yuqi Zhang,Yuxiao He,Zengke Gui,Zhaoxin Huan,Zhaoyang Wang,Zhibo Zhu,Zhihao Wang,Zhiqiang Zhang,Zhoufei Wang,Zihang Zeng,Ziqi Liu,Zitao Xuan,Zuoli Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Ling 2.0 是一个基于混合专家（MoE）架构的推理导向语言基础系列，参数规模从 160 亿到 1 万亿，通过高稀疏性和协同创新实现了高达 7 倍的计算效率提升，并在万亿参数规模下确立了推理精度与计算效率的新帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决大规模语言模型在推理任务中计算成本高昂的问题，通过验证“每次激活都能提升推理能力”的原则，探索如何在保持高推理精度的同时，利用稀疏激活实现从数百亿到万亿参数的高效扩展。

Method: 采用统一的混合专家（MoE）范式，结合高稀疏性架构、多令牌预测（MTP）、推理导向数据与思维链（CoT）激活的中期训练、基于强化的微调（DFT, Evo-CoT），以及全规模 FP8 训练和细粒度异构流水线技术。

Result: 推出了包括 Ling-mini-2.0、Ling-flash-2.0 和 Ling-1T 在内的三个非思维（指令）模型，相比稠密模型实现了高达 7 倍的主动计算效率；其中 Ling-1T 在万亿参数规模下展示了稀疏激活与推理目标对齐后可扩展且高效的智能表现。

Conclusion: Ling 2.0 提供了一个连贯、开放且高效的基础设施，证明了正确对齐的稀疏激活是实现可扩展推理智能的关键，为未来包括 Ring 系列在内的推理和思维模型的发展奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: We introduce Ling 2.0, a series reasoning-oriented language foundation built upon the principle that every activation boosts reasoning capability. Designed to scale from tens of billions to one trillion parameters under a unified Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) paradigm, Ling 2.0 emphasizes high sparsity, cross-scale consistency, and efficiency guided by empirical scaling laws. The series includes three non-thinking (instruct) models - Ling-mini-2.0, Ling-flash-2.0, and Ling-1T - ranging from 16B to 1T total parameters and achieving up to 7-fold active-compute efficiency compared with dense counterparts. Ling 2.0 integrates coordinated innovations across model architecture, pre-training, post-training, and infrastructure: a high-sparsity MoE with MTP for efficient reasoning, reasoning-oriented data and mid-training CoT activation, reinforcement-based fine-tuning (DFT, Evo-CoT), and full-scale FP8 training with fine-grained heterogeneous pipelines. At the trillion scale, Ling-1T establishes a new Pareto frontier of reasoning accuracy versus computational efficiency, demonstrating that sparse activation, when properly aligned with reasoning objectives, enables scalable and efficient intelligence. Collectively, Ling 2.0 provides a coherent, open, and efficient foundation for advancing future reasoning and thinking models, including the Ring series built upon the same base.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [A Subsampled Davis-Kahan Bound for Large-Scale Eigenspace Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09211)
*Huan Qing*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种独立伯努利采样方案，证明了子采样矩阵的左奇异向量能逼近低秩对称矩阵的目标子空间，并推导了依赖于采样概率的子采样Davis-Kahan界，揭示了计算成本与统计误差之间的权衡，从而实现了大规模对称矩阵的可扩展谱分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Davis-Kahan定理虽是谱分析的核心工具，但在矩阵维度巨大时，计算主导特征向量成本过高，限制了谱方法在现代大规模应用中的实用性。

Method: 提出一种独立伯努利采样方案（independent Bernoulli sampling scheme），对低秩对称矩阵进行子采样，并证明子采样矩阵的主导左奇异向量能忠实逼近目标子空间。

Result: 推导出了显式的子采样Davis-Kahan界（subsampled Davis-Kahan bound），该误差界直接依赖于采样概率；揭示了计算成本随采样概率线性扩展，而统计误差随采样概率的平方根倒数缩放。

Conclusion: 该结果将Davis-Kahan定理扩展至子采样场景，解决了大规模对称矩阵谱分析的可扩展性问题，平衡了计算效率与统计精度。

Abstract: The Davis-Kahan theorem is a fundamental tool in spectral analysis, providing quantitative control over the distance between the eigenspaces of a symmetric matrix and its perturbation. However, when the matrix dimension is large, computing leading eigenvectors is computationally expensive, limiting the practical use of spectral methods in modern large-scale applications. This paper addresses this problem by proposing an independent Bernoulli sampling scheme and proves that the leading left singular vectors of the subsampled matrix faithfully approximate the target subspace of a low-rank symmetric matrix. Our main result is a subsampled Davis-Kahan bound that gives an explicit error bound depending directly on the sampling probability. The bound reveals the trade-off: the computational cost scales linearly with the sampling probability, while the statistical error scales as the inverse square root of the sampling probability. Our result thus extends the Davis-Kahan theorem to the subsampled setting, enabling scalable spectral analysis of large-scale symmetric matrices.

</details>


### [150] [Critical initialization destabilizes higher input derivatives in wide scalar-input networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09244)
*Prashant Singh,Pranav Singh*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文推导了宽随机初始化神经网络中直到三阶导数的均值场递归关系，证明了在临界点处一阶导数方差不变而二阶导数方差线性增长，并证实了特定缩放的残差网络中任意有限阶导数方差均有界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决边缘混沌（edge-of-chaos）条件虽能保持一阶输入扰动，但基于物理信息的损失、分数匹配和导数正则化等方法依赖于更高阶输入导数，而现有理论缺乏对这些高阶导数行为的精确描述。

Method: 利用无限宽极限下固定深度处有限导数jet的联合高斯性，推导直到三阶的均值场递归；分析临界点处的方差增长规律；对分支尺度为$L^{-1/2}$的残差网络进行理论证明；并通过数值模拟验证临界增长律、残差界及闭合递归。

Result: 在临界点，一阶导数方差具有深度不变性，而当激活函数曲率非零时，二阶导数方差随深度线性增长；导出的三阶系统在均值场磁化率上闭合；对于特定缩放的残差网络，证明了在明确正则性假设下，任意固定有限阶导数的方差一致有界。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了宽随机初始化网络中高阶导数的精确均值场理论，揭示了临界点处的高阶导数增长机制及残差网络的稳定性边界，为依赖高阶导数的学习方法提供了初始化理论依据（注：结果仅针对初始化状态，不涉及训练后性能）。

Abstract: The edge-of-chaos condition preserves first-order input perturbations in wide randomly initialized networks, but physics-informed losses, score matching and derivative regularization depend on higher input derivatives. For smooth scalar-input fully connected networks, using a joint Gaussianity of the finite derivative jet that holds in the infinite-width limit at each fixed depth, we derive mean-field recursions through third order that are exact at the variance fixed point, with finite-depth corrections that decay geometrically. At criticality, the first-derivative variance is depth-invariant, whereas the second-derivative variance grows linearly whenever the activation has nonzero curvature. The resulting third-order system closes on mean-field susceptibilities. For residual networks with branch scale L^{-1/2}, we prove that every fixed finite derivative order has uniformly bounded variance under explicit regularity assumptions. Simulations verify the critical growth laws, the residual bound, and the closed recursion. The results concern initialization, not trained-network performance.

</details>


### [151] [What Fixed-Rollout pass@k Evaluations Can Identify](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09245)
*Pranav Singh,Prashant Singh*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明了在固定采样数 $n$ 下，无法从统计上识别 $k>n$ 的外推 pass@k 指标，即使任务数量无限；并通过实证展示了在 $k=1000$ 时失败率估计存在巨大不确定性，提出了非参数基准和报告标准以区分直接估计与模型外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前大模型评估中普遍存在的将 pass@k 指标从实际采样数 $n$ 外推至远大于 $n$ 的 $k$ 值时的统计有效性问题，揭示现有方法在信息论层面的根本缺陷。

Method: 基于混合/随机任务条件二项式模型，利用矩问题理论证明固定 $n$ 的成功计数仅能识别潜在任务成功分布的前 $n$ 个矩；构造保持计数律但外推结果不兼容的反例；利用 Hausdorff 主表示计算精确的总体识别区间；在 Brown 等人发布的 10,000 次 rollout 数据集上进行反事实评估验证。

Result: 理论上证明了对于 $k>n$，通用的外推 pass@k、尾部指数及尾部常数均不可识别；实证显示在 $n=16$ 的设定下，$k=1000$ 时的失败率估计在不同配置下存在 1.5 倍至 2600 倍的巨大歧义；中间规模的失败份额无法单独决定识别区间的宽度。

Conclusion: 该研究并未否定参数化的推理时缩放律，而是提供了评估其假设所需的非参数基准；提出了精确的有限任务置信度证书及报告标准，明确区分直接估计值、识别集和基于模型条件的预测，以规范评估报告。

Abstract: Repeated-sampling evaluations increasingly extrapolate pass@k far beyond the number n of samples collected per problem. We show that, in the pooled/random-task conditional-Binomial model, fixed-n success counts identify only the n free moments of the latent per-task success distribution. Consequently, direct pass@k is identified for k <= n, but generic extrapolated pass@k, tail exponents, and tail constants are not identified for k > n, even with arbitrarily many exchangeable tasks at the same rollout budget. This is stronger than the observation that the usual estimator is undefined beyond n: it characterizes the information missing from the fixed-depth count-law experiment. We give exact count-law-preserving constructions with incompatible extrapolations, state the exceptional unique-extension case, and compute sharp population identified intervals through Hausdorff principal representations. On the public 10,000-rollout-per-problem release of Brown et al., counterfactual n = 16 evaluations leave failure at k = 1000 ambiguous by factors from 1.5 to over 2,600 across four MATH/GSM8K/CodeContests configurations. The calibration shows that intermediate-scale failure share alone does not determine width. Our result does not reject parametric inference-time scaling laws; it supplies the nonparametric baseline against which their assumptions can be evaluated. We give an exact, conservative one-coordinate finite-task confidence certificate and a reporting standard separating direct estimates, identified sets, and model-conditioned forecasts.

</details>


### [152] [CAST: Canonical Approximate Schur Tree for Approximate Cholesky on Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09255)
*Meher Chaitanya,Cameron Musco,Aristides Gionis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出CAST及其变体CAST-ρ，通过用加权随机生成树替代近似Cholesky分解中产生的稠密Schur补团，实现了无偏且高效的图数据预条件子构建，显著降低了计算成本并控制了采样方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决图数据工作负载中多次求解相同系数矩阵的Laplacian或SDDM系统时，传统近似Cholesky预条件子在消除顶点（pivot）时会产生稠密的Schur补团，导致存储和计算开销巨大的问题。

Method: 引入CAST（规范近似Schur树），将稠密团替换为从该团中直接采样的加权随机生成树，并通过按树包含概率的倒数重加权边来保证更新无偏；进一步提出CAST-ρ，通过将每个邻居复制ρ份并采样后收缩，以$O(ρd)$时间实现采样，利用增加ρ来降低归一化局部Schur误差的二阶矩界限。

Result: 证明了CAST分布独立于邻居排序，其杠杆分数边缘分布最小化了无偏单树估计器中最大的归一化重加权边贡献；CAST-ρ实现了$1/ρ$的误差二阶矩界限；实验表明CAST-1是更快的默认选择，而当额外边贡献成本较低时，CAST-2表现更优。

Conclusion: CAST系列方法通过随机生成树近似有效平衡了预条件子的构建成本、稀疏性与数值稳定性，为大规模图系统的迭代求解提供了高效且理论可证的优化方案。

Abstract: Graph-data workloads such as diffusion estimation, ranking, semi-supervised learning, and network optimization often solve many Laplacian or symmetric diagonally dominant M-matrix (SDDM) systems with the same coefficient matrix. Approximate Cholesky preconditioners eliminate vertices one at a time and store the resulting sparse approximate factorization, the \emph{factor}, whose construction cost is amortized across these solves. But eliminating a vertex, the \emph{pivot}, creates a dense Schur-complement clique among its $d$ active neighbors. We introduce CAST (Canonical Approximate Schur Tree), which replaces this clique with a weighted random spanning tree sampled directly from it. Every realization is connected and contains exactly d-1 edges, while reweighting each selected edge by the reciprocal of its tree-inclusion probability makes the update unbiased. The distribution is independent of the ordering of the pivot neighbors, and we prove that its leverage-score marginals minimize the largest normalized reweighted-edge contribution among unbiased inverse-marginal one-tree estimators. We also introduce CAST-$ρ$, which replaces each pivot neighbor with $ρ$ copies, each carrying a $1/ρ$ share of that neighbor's incident weight, samples a weighted random spanning tree on the expanded clique, and contracts the copies back to the original neighborhood. The resulting update remains unbiased and connected, can be sampled exactly in $O(ρd)$ time, and satisfies a $1/ρ$ bound on the second moment of the normalized local Schur error. Increasing $ρ$ therefore reduces certified local sampling variability, but may increase construction cost and downstream fill. Empirically, we observe that CAST-1 is the faster default, whereas CAST-2 is preferable when its additional edge contributions remain inexpensive.

</details>


### [153] [Tensor Network Moral Graph Recovery of Discrete Probability Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09258)
*Á. Troyano Olivas,Chi-Hang Fred Fung,Hans H. Brunner,Momtchil Peev,Vicente Martin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用全连接张量网络（FCTNs）结合核范数正则化，从离散变量的概率分布中恢复因果有向无环图（DAG）的道德图（moral graph）的方法，并证明了在零重构误差下该方法能精确恢复道德图，同时给出了近似误差下的恢复界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决从离散变量概率分布中准确推断因果结构（具体为道德图）的难题，利用张量网络的表达能力和正则化技术来自动识别并剔除不必要的连接，从而实现高效的图结构学习。

Method: 采用全连接张量网络（FCTNs），将每个连接矩阵参数化为全1基准矩阵加上低秩修正项（$C_{ij} = U_{ij}V_{ij}^\top$）。通过对因子施加变分Frobenius范数惩罚来实现修正项的核范数正则化，从而驱动不必要的连接归零。该方法基于忠实性、正性及局部张量架构的无隐式重路由假设。

Result: 理论证明了在重构误差$\varepsilon = 0$时，任何最优FCTN的有效图精确等于道德图；在近似 regime ($\varepsilon > 0$) 下，利用Fannes-Audenaert条件互信息连续性导出了明确的恢复界限及正则化参数$\beta$的充分条件。有效图可直接从优化后的连接矩阵中读取。

Conclusion: 该方法通过核范数正则化的低秩修正机制，能够在理论上保证从离散数据中精确或近似地恢复因果DAG的道德图，为基于张量网络的因果结构学习提供了严格的理论依据和可行的算法路径。

Abstract: We present a method for recovering the moral graph of a causal DAG from a probability distribution over discrete variables, using fully connected tensor networks (FCTNs) with nuclear-norm-regularized bond corrections. Each bond matrix is parameterized as a baseline all-ones matrix plus a low-rank correction $C_{ij} = U_{ij}V_{ij}^\top$, and the nuclear norm of the correction implemented via the variational Frobenius norm penalty on the factors drives unnecessary bonds to zero. We prove that under faithfulness, positivity, and a no-implicit-rerouting assumption on the local tensor architecture, \textbf{every} optimal FCTN with zero reconstruction error $\varepsilon = 0$ has effective graph exactly equal to the moral graph. For the approximate regime ($\varepsilon > 0$), we provide explicit recovery bounds using the Fannes-Audenaert continuity of conditional mutual information, and derive a sufficient condition on the regularization parameter $β$. The effective graph is read directly from the optimized bond matrices.

</details>


### [154] [Mode Coverage in Normalizing Flow Boltzmann Generators via Log-Ratio Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09473)
*Qi Feng,Rongjie Lai,Di Qi,Xuda Ye*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为KLXX的新损失函数，通过引入两个对数比率变分（log-ratio variations）来改进归一化流玻尔兹曼生成器（Normalizing Flow Boltzmann Generators），解决了传统前向KL训练因依赖有偏目标样本而遗漏目标模式（modes）的问题，显著提升了模式覆盖率和采样准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的归一化流玻尔兹曼生成器在使用前向KL散度训练时，依赖于可能带有偏差或遗漏某些模式的目标样本，导致生成的流可能错过目标质量分布，尽管其观测到的重要性权重显示出较高的有效样本量（ESS），从而产生误导性的训练效果。

Method: 提出对数比率变分$\X_ω$（目标与推前对数密度比率在加权测度$ω$下的平均绝对成对差），并据此构建KLXX损失函数。该函数在前向KL基础上增加两项变分：一项由目标分布加权以提高精度，另一项由淬火（quench）、回火（temper）及推前样本的混合分布加权以搜索候选模式。推导了KLXX的Fisher-Rao梯度流及固定代理误差界，并将其应用于具有逐阶段重要性重加权的自适应分期玻尔兹曼生成器中，证明了该方案在样本量趋于无穷时的渐近无偏性。

Result: 数值测试表明，相比传统的前向KL损失，KLXX显著改善了模式覆盖率；增强了生成器针对构建调度（schedule）的损失函数的逐阶段诊断能力；生成器恢复的可观测量与独立参考值高度接近。

Conclusion: 对数比率变分提供了前向KL损失通常遗漏的关键信息，通过KLXX损失函数有效解决了模式遗漏问题，为玻尔兹曼生成器的训练和采样提供了更可靠的理论保证和实证性能。

Abstract: Normalizing flow Boltzmann generators retain a tractable pushforward density, but training with forward KL depends on target samples that may be biased or omit modes. As a result, a flow can miss target mass while its observed importance weights give a high effective sample size. We introduce the log-ratio variation $\X_ω$, the mean absolute pairwise difference of the target-to-pushforward log-density ratio under a weighting measure $ω$, and use it to define KLXX, a new loss function. Two log-ratio variations are added to the forward KL (denoted by the two X's): one weighted by the target to improve accuracy, the other by a mixture of quench and temper samples with pushforward samples to search candidate modes. We derive the Fisher--Rao gradient flow of KLXX, where both variations contribute nonpositive dissipation, and a fixed-surrogate error bound for KLXX. We use KLXX in an adaptive-staging Boltzmann generator, with importance reweighting at every stage. We bound the sampling error of its inference scheme when the stage weights are essentially bounded, and prove it asymptotically unbiased in the sample size. In the numerical tests, KLXX improves mode coverage over forward KL. It also improves the generator's per-stage diagnostics against the loss that built the schedule. The observables the generator recovers are close to independent references. The log-ratio variations thus supply information that the forward KL loss usually omits.

</details>


### [155] [Recovery Theory for Projected Power Iterations in Permutation Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09502)
*Vahan Huroyan,Gilad Lerman*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了投影幂法（PPM）在稀疏均匀腐蚀模型下同步多个排列的问题，证明了在特定条件下可实现单步精确恢复或误差收缩，并最终达到几乎精确或完全精确的恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在存在噪声和部分观测（稀疏性）的情况下，如何从成对测量中准确同步多个未知排列的问题，特别是在腐蚀模型较为复杂（均匀随机排列腐蚀）时的理论保证。

Method: 采用投影幂法（PPM），结合谱初始化（reference-block spectral initializer），在$\log m=o(np\pi_0^2)$和$np \ge C_0\log n$等条件下，通过理论分析证明单步恢复能力和迭代过程中的误差收缩性质，并扩展至非独立同分布腐蚀及部分排列情形。

Result: 1. 在固定正多数正确块的初始估计下，以高概率实现单步精确恢复；2. 在误差小于$0.5-\varepsilon$的 basin 内，一次更新即可将误差收缩至$O(m/(np\pi_0^2))$，且后续迭代保持几乎精确；3. 谱初始化可提供$O_{\mathbb P}(m/(np\pi_0^2))$的对齐块误差，实现端到端几乎精确恢复；4. 在更强信号条件下，有限次迭代后可达精确恢复；5. 理论可推广至部分排列及变支持集情形。

Conclusion: 投影幂法（PPM）在稀疏均匀腐蚀模型下具有强大的理论保证，不仅能实现单步精确恢复，还能通过误差收缩机制确保迭代轨迹的稳定性，为排列同步问题提供了鲁棒且高效的解决方案，其理论结果广泛适用于多种腐蚀模型和部分观测场景。

Abstract: We study the projected power method (PPM) for synchronizing \(n\) unknown permutations of \(m\) objects under a possibly sparse uniform corruption model. Each pair is observed with probability \(p\), and an observed measurement is uncorrupted with probability \(π_0\) and is otherwise an independent uniform permutation. Under \(\log m=o(npπ_0^2)\), we prove exact one-step recovery (with high probability) of each prescribed block for an independent estimate with a fixed positive majority of correct blocks. When \(np\ge C_0\log n\) and \(m=o(npπ_0^2)\), we prove that one high-probability event yields a block-error contraction simultaneously for every estimate whose optimally aligned error is at most \(0.5-ε\). The contraction factor is \(O(m/(npπ_0^2))\) and the error floor is \(O(e^{-cnpπ_0}+e^{-cnpπ_0^2}+{\log n}/{n})\). Consequently, one update maps every possibly data-dependent estimate in this basin to vanishing block error, and all subsequent iterates remain almost exact uniformly over the iteration index. The one-step and trajectory results extend to independent, non-identically distributed, permutation-valued corruptions with mean \(m^{-1} \mathbf{1}\mathbf{1}^{\top}\). Under the uniform model, a reference-block spectral initializer has aligned block error \(O_{\mathbb P}(m/(npπ_0^2))\), yielding an end-to-end almost-exact recovery guarantee. Under a stronger all-block signal condition, PPM reaches exact recovery after finitely many iterations. The theory transfers exactly to partial permutations with common support; for varying supports, we establish deterministic and probabilistic co-visibility margins.

</details>


### [156] [High-probability guarantees for linear accessibility in feature superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09556)
*Enrico Vompa*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文通过将线性可及性构建为压缩感知问题，证明了在亚高斯噪声下，特征叠加所需的维度呈线性增长（$d=O_{\varepsilon}(k \log m)$），而非此前认为的最坏情况下的二次方增长，并量化了线性表示假设的几何约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决神经网络中特征叠加（feature superposition）导致的跨特征干扰问题，该干扰限制了同时激活特征的线性可及性，且此前关于所需维度的理论界限仅为最坏情况下的二次方估计，缺乏紧致的概率界限。

Method: 将线性可及性问题形式化为压缩感知（compressed sensing）问题，推导固定支撑集在亚高斯噪声下的高概率界限，并通过高斯尾近似（Gaussian-tail approximations） Across 系统参数验证这些界限。

Result: 证明了充分维度随特征数量呈线性缩放（$d=O_{\varepsilon}(k \log m)$），推翻了 prior 的二次方限制假设，并通过实验验证了理论界限在不同系统参数下的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究结果量化了线性表示假设的几何约束，为评估稀疏自编码器、组合泛化能力及神经可解释性提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Neural networks can leverage feature superposition to encode more concepts than dimensions, but cross-feature interference constrains the linear accessibility of simultaneously active features. By framing linear accessibility as a compressed sensing problem, we derive high-probability bounds for fixed supports under subgaussian noise, proving the sufficient dimension scales linearly ($d=O_{\varepsilon}(k \log m)$) rather than prior worst-case quadratic limits. We then validate these bounds across system parameters through Gaussian-tail approximations. These results quantify the geometric constraints of the linear representation hypothesis, providing a framework for evaluating sparse autoencoders, compositional generalization, and neural interpretability.

</details>


### [157] [Learning with Synthetic Data via SGD in High-Dimensional Linear Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09572)
*Jichu li,Difan Zou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: ...


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ...

Method: ...

Result: ...

Conclusion: ...

Abstract: Synthetic data has become a promising way to scale model training beyond limited human-generated data but it may also induce strong model collapse (Dohmatob et al., 2024), where any fixed fraction of synthetic data prevents model performance from improving under data scaling, leaving a non-vanishing excess risk floor. In this paper, we study how synthetic data affects the generalization of one-pass SGD in high-dimensional linear regression with model shift. We establish finite-sample risk bounds for mixed and two-stage training, separating standard bias and variance from source-mismatch effects, namely fluctuation and persistent drift under mixing and filtered initialization bias under two-stage. These bounds reveal a sharp contrast: mixed training induces strong model collapse, while two-stage training avoids the floor by using synthetic data only in the first stage, showing that collapse is not inevitable under a simple data curriculum. Under a random sketch model, we further obtain scaling laws for both protocols, with tight results for mixed training in the optimization-saturated regime. These laws show that larger models may amplify synthetic-induced degradation under mixing, and quantify how high-quality synthetic pretraining may reduce bias in two-stage training. Finally, we establish an exact finite-sample necessary-and-sufficient condition for two-stage training to strictly outperform real-only training under the same real-data budget and identical real-stage updates. Overall, our results highlight that synthetic data is neither inherently harmful nor beneficial; its effect depends critically on both its quality and the training protocol used to incorporate it.

</details>


### [158] [Distillation of Synthetic Data for Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09586)
*Niloy Biswas,Noureddine El Karoui*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出“合成数据蒸馏”（SDD）方法，通过让时间序列基础模型（TSFM）的输出与轨迹的条件预测分布而非单一实现值进行对比，显著降低了训练梯度的方差，从而在多种模型规模下实现了比传统方法快10%-40%的收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前TSFM在合成数据上的预训练通常基于损失函数，该函数将模型输出与轨迹的未来实现值进行点对点比较。这种方法忽略了数据生成过程的已知统计特性，导致训练效率受限，作者旨在利用已知的条件分布信息来优化训练目标。

Method: 提出合成数据蒸馏（SDD）策略，将损失目标从比较“实现值”改为比较“条件预测分布”。从理论上看，这是一种Rao-Blackwellization过程，它在保持随机梯度期望不变的同时，根据Loewner偏序证明了其能显著降低随机梯度的协方差。

Result: 在参数量从4M到2.5B的TSFM模型族上进行了实证验证。结果显示，SDD在所有模型规模下均实现了更快的验证损失收敛；特别是在高斯过程数据上，SDD在达到或优于现有基准损失性能的同时，减少了10%-40%的训练迭代次数。

Conclusion: 利用合成数据中已知的数据生成过程进行分布层面的蒸馏（SDD），是一种理论上严谨且实证有效的优化手段，能显著提升时间序列基础模型的训练效率和收敛速度。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) are increasingly pre-trained on synthetically generated time series trajectories, where the data generating process is known. Current pre-training recipes are based on loss objectives which compare TSFM outputs to realized future values of each trajectory. We instead propose loss objectives which compare TSFM outputs to the conditional forecast distribution of each trajectory, a procedure we call synthetic data distillation (SDD). SDD corresponds to a Rao-Blackwellization of the training objective, in that it leaves the expectation of stochastic gradients unchanged while provably reducing the covariance of the stochastic gradient under the Loewner partial ordering. We empirically validate SDD on a TSFM model family of sizes from $4$M to $2.5$B parameters, and observe faster convergence of validation loss at every model size: on Gaussian Process data, SDD attains or improves upon the Status Quo loss whilst requiring $10\%-40\%$ less training iterations.

</details>


### [159] [Why Learning Rediscovers the Closed-Form Diagonal Regularizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09656)
*Jeahn Han,Pyojin Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文揭示了模态逆问题中的“对角饱和原理”：在各向同性截断噪声下，贝叶斯最优的正则化形状是由先验决定的闭式幂律，与具体域无关；实验表明，简单的闭式解在性能上几乎等同于复杂的深度学习架构，除非利用跨模态耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决模态逆问题中正则化策略的选择难题，探究是否存在一种通用的、由先验主导的最优正则化形式，并评估复杂的学习型架构相对于简单闭式解的实际增益空间。

Method: 结合Berry随机波猜想（用于去相关截断噪声）和Weyl特征值计数定律（提供足够模态），理论推导出贝叶斯最优Tikhonov形状为闭式幂律 $\Gamma_k \propto \lambda_k^{|s|}$。通过在有限元模拟的声学房间和热扩散问题上进行实证，对比该闭式解与三种对角架构及利用跨模态耦合的Learned Iterative Ridge方法的性能。

Result: 在声学房间模拟中，闭式幂律解相对于每房间的神谕调优接近最优；三种对角架构虽学习到不同的频谱，但其重建误差与闭式解仅相差1 pp。该框架无需新参数即可扩展至热扩散问题。只有利用跨模态耦合的Learned Iterative Ridge方法才能突破对角族的性能饱和边界。

Conclusion: 在对角正则化族中存在性能饱和现象，简单的闭式幂律解在鲁棒性和最优性上极具竞争力，极大地压缩了对角正则化器的学习空间；真正的性能提升始于利用跨模态耦合，这标志着学习开始发挥实质性作用的边界。

Abstract: We identify a diagonal saturation principle in modal inverse problems: when truncation noise is isotropic, the Bayes-optimal Tikhonov shape is a closed-form power law Gamma_k proportional to lambda_k^|s| set by the prior alone, independent of the domain. Berry's random-wave conjecture decorrelates the truncation noise across modes, and Weyl's eigenvalue counting law supplies enough modes for the conclusion to survive empirical Berry violations. Together they predict an approximately flat loss landscape across the per-mode family, leaving narrow scope for a diagonal regularizer to robustly beat the closed form. On FEM-simulated acoustic rooms, the closed form is near-optimal relative to per-room oracle tuning across observation windows, and three diagonal architectures trained on the same data match its reconstruction error within 1 pp despite learning qualitatively different spectra. The framework extends to heat diffusion via a known exponential Green's function correction with no new free parameters. Saturation is restricted to the diagonal family: Learned Iterative Ridge crosses the boundary by exploiting cross-mode coupling, locating where learning starts to help.

</details>


### [160] [A Unifying Perspective on Probabilities as Model Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09855)
*Benedikt Höltgen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出“预测方法”框架，统一贝叶斯与频率主义概率观，证明所有概率均依赖模型，并指出在满足有限校准准则时可指导有效决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决概率理解上的基础分歧（如贝叶斯派与频率派之争），并澄清在何种条件下依据概率行动能产生理想结果。

Method: 构建“预测方法”概念，将概率定义为抽象构建与预测转换的输出；结合归纳论证与概率演算，分析有限校准准则的可行性。

Result: 揭示了即使所谓“客观”概率也具有模型依赖性；证明在满足有限校准准则时，可预测给定策略的效用分布，从而在有限事件集上实现成功决策。

Conclusion: 建立了关于概率及其应用的连贯视角，整合了不同解释背后的关键直觉，为概率的理论与实践提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Although probabilistic statements are ubiquitous, foundational disagreements persist about their understanding, as exemplified by debates between Bayesians and frequentists; moreover, it is unclear when and why acting on them actually leads to desirable outcomes. Here, we argue that every probability is the output of a \emph{prediction method}, that is, it depends on both a particular way of constructing abstractions and a way of transforming them into predictions. Through this, we provide a unifying perspective on supposedly different kinds of probabilities and show that even supposedly objective ones are model-dependent. We demonstrate that when a finite calibration criterion is met, one can anticipate the distribution of utilities for a given policy and inform successful decision-making on finite sets of events. Based on the notion of prediction methods, inductive arguments, and the probability calculus, we explain the feasibility of the calibration criterion in many settings. Overall, we develop a coherent perspective on probabilities and their use, connecting key intuitions behind other interpretations along the way.

</details>


### [161] [FlowCPO: A Unified Divergence View of Preference Alignment for Flow Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09905)
*Yansen Han,Shengyi Liao,Peng Sun,Deyuan Liu,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Tao Lin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: ...


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ...

Method: ...

Result: ...

Conclusion: ...

Abstract: Preference alignment for flow and diffusion models now spans online reinforcement learning and offline preference optimization, but the relation between these methods remains unclear. In particular, existing forward-process alignment methods require fresh samples from the current model, while offline methods based on fixed preference pairs rely primarily on positive-only fine-tuning or DPO-style likelihood-ratio surrogates. We organize these approaches through a divergence-based framework and introduce FlowCPO, an offline forward-KL objective that uses both preferred and dispreferred samples without online rollouts. For linear interpolation, we show under explicit regularity conditions that the forward-KL objective is bounded by a contrastive flow matching loss, yielding a tractable surrogate on fixed data. We further show that this loss is nonnegative, whereas the signed regression loss of simplified FlowDPO can be unbounded below. In the in-domain setting, FlowCPO achieves higher mean GenEval and OCR scores than the evaluated baselines, reaching 0.84 and 0.87 versus 0.81 and 0.74 for FlowDPO at CFG 3.0. In the out-of-domain setting, the results are mixed, with the best GenEval result but lower reward scores than RFT on several metrics.

</details>


### [162] [Optimal Value Inference for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09981)
*Nan Lu,Ethan Lee,James M. Robins,David Simchi-Levi,Junwei Lu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对强化学习最优价值离线推断的去偏估计量，利用Neyman正交性在行为策略随时间变化时仍能保证渐近正态性，并通过合成实验及自行车调度、AI智能体工具使用等实际场景验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决强化学习中离线推断最优价值时的统计挑战，特别是在行为策略随时间变化（time-varying behavior policy）且存在由自诱导Bellman方程定义的新的干扰参数（nuisances）的情况下，实现有效的统计推断。

Method: 1. 通过Softmax对应近似最大Bellman算子，推导两个新的干扰参数作为自诱导Bellman方程的不动点；2. 利用Neyman正交性构建去偏估计量（debiased estimator）；3. 建立该估计量在发散视界（diverging horizons）下的渐近正态性理论；4. 提供具体的干扰参数估计过程。

Result: 理论上证明了只要干扰参数达到许多机器学习方法可实现的统计速率，该估计量即具有渐近正态性；合成实验验证了数值性能；在自行车重定位和AI智能体工具使用等现实决策问题中成功实施了该推断方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的推断方法在处理动态行为策略的离线强化学习最优价值评估中是有效且稳健的，能够支持现实世界复杂决策场景中的统计推断。

Abstract: We study offline inference for the optimal value in reinforcement learning. Two new nuisances are derived as fixed points of a self-induced Bellman equation, in which we approximate the maximum Bellman operator by its softmax correspondence. We propose a debiased estimator through the Neyman orthogonality and establish its asymptotic normality under diverging horizons even when the behavior policy changes with time, as long as the nuisances have the statistical rates that can be achieved by many machine learning methods. We provide a concrete estimating procedure for these nuisances and show they can lead to valid inference. Synthetic experiments validate the numerical performance of our inference method, and we implement it in real-life decision-making problems, including bike repositioning and AI agentic tool use.

</details>


### [163] [A statistical approach to bias in zero-shot learning: the lens of handwriting recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10084)
*Clarence Chew,Gim Siang Chia,Sukalpa Chanda,Subhroshekhar Ghosh,Soumendu Sundar Mukherjee*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统计偏差校正方法，通过两阶段分层架构（经典GZSL黑盒+轻量级蒙特卡洛校正器集合），解决了广义零样本学习（GZSL）在超大词汇量手写单词识别中偏向已见类别的问题，实现了未见单词分类准确率相对提升超过20%，并证明了低维表示（约15维）的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GZSL技术受限于可处理的未见类别数量较少，且存在向训练期间已见类别严重偏斜的误分类偏差，难以扩展到超大词汇量场景（如手写单词识别）。

Method: 提出一种统计偏差校正方法：1）将经典GZSL特征学习器视为黑盒；2）采用两阶段分层架构，第一阶段使用黑盒，第二阶段利用轻量级蒙特卡洛偏差校正器集合；3）基于预测的训练状态（已见/未见），仅使用统计方法（如最近邻、逻辑回归、随机森林）对测试数据进行分类。

Result: 在未见单词分类任务中，相比现有技术实现了超过20%的相对准确率提升；发现大规模词汇量的单词识别可适配更低维度的表示（约15维）。

Conclusion: 该偏差校正方法具有数学理论支撑，可作为即插即用模块与任意经典GZSL学习器结合，展现出在多种GZSL应用场景中的广泛适用性。

Abstract: Generalized zero-shot learning (GZSL) has emerged as an important paradigm for visual recognition systems that must generalize to classes that were not observed during training. Traditional GZSL techniques are limited by their applicability to a relatively small number of such unseen classes, scalability beyond which is challenging due to its well-known misclassification bias towards classes observed during training. In this work, we investigate the GZSL paradigm through the lens of zero-shot handwritten word recognition over extremely large vocabularies. We propose a statistical approach to rectifying this bias, which views any classical GZSL feature learner as a black box mechanism whose intrinsic bias in identifying the training status (seen vs. unseen) of a typical data point we aim to correct, similar to an out of distribution inferential problem. Our method leverages a simple two-stage hierarchical architecture, combining a classical GZSL blackbox in the first stage and an ensemble of lightweight Monte Carlo bias-correctors in the second. Once debiased, the classification of test data is undertaken only restricted to its predicted training status via well-founded statistical methods (eg nearest neighbour, logistic regression and random forests). We achieve relative accuracy improvements of over 20% in the classification of unseen words compared to established techniques. A key outcome is that word recognition over large scale vocabularies is amenable to a much lower dimensional representation (~15 dimensions). Our approach is underpinned by mathematical analysis that captures the essence of the statistical approach to bias correction. Our approach to bias rectification can be combined in a turn-key fashion with any classical GZSL learner as a blackbox, thereby suggesting a wide scope of applicability of this method for a wide variety of GZSL implementations in different domains.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [Approximate Nearest Neighbor in Ultra-High Dimensional $\ell_\infty$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09427)
*Nathan White,Tian Zhang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对超高维空间（$d \gg n$）下的$\ell_\infty$范数近似最近邻（ANN）问题，提出了“子集嵌入”技术，实现了与维度$d$无关的查询时间，并提供了两种具有不同近似比和查询时间权衡的数据结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决超高维设定下$\ell_\infty$范数ANN问题的效率瓶颈。现有研究（SoCG 2025）在$\ell_1, \ell_2$范数下表现优异，但在$\ell_\infty$范数下查询时间为$Ω(nd)$，效率低下。由于理想的数据结构查询时间应不依赖于维度$d$，因此亟需针对$\ell_\infty$的高效算法。

Method: 引入“子集嵌入”（subset embeddings）概念，即通过仅选择维度的子集来嵌入点。论文证明了通过计算$n^{1+1/c}$个坐标的距离，可以在$O(c)$因子内保持$n$个点数据集中的所有成对距离，并给出了相应的下界证明。基于此技术构建了两种ANN数据结构。

Result: 1. 建立了子集嵌入的理论界限：保持$O(c)$近似比需$n^{1+1/c}$个坐标，且存在$n^{1+Ω(1/c)}$的下界。
2. 提出第一种数据结构：空间$O(n^2\log d)$，查询时间$\tilde{O}(n^{1+1/c})$，近似比$O(c\log\log n)$。
3. 提出第二种数据结构：空间与查询时间同上，但近似比优化至$O(c^{\log_2 3}) \approx O(c^{1.58})$，实现了例如$n^{1.01}$查询时间下的$O(1)$近似。

Conclusion: 通过子集嵌入技术，成功克服了$\ell_\infty$范数在超高维空间下的计算挑战，构建了查询时间与维度$d$无关的高效ANN数据结构，并在近似比与查询时间之间提供了灵活的权衡，显著优于现有结果。

Abstract: We study the approximate nearest neighbor problem under $\ell_\infty$ in the ultra-high dimensional setting where the dimension $d$ is significantly larger than the number of points $n$. Thus, we desire data structures with no dependence on $d$ in the query time. [Herold-Nanongkai-Spoerhase-Varma-Wu, SoCG 2025] introduce this problem and give data structures in $\ell_p$: for $p=1,2$, they give $(1+\varepsilon)$-approximation data structures with space $\tilde{O}(n\log d/\text{poly}(\varepsilon))$ and query time $\tilde{O}(n/\text{poly}(\varepsilon))$. Since any data structure must have query time $Ω(\min \{n,d\})$, this query time is nearly tight. However, their results are inefficient for $\ell_\infty$, with query time $Ω(nd)$.
  In order to handle the challenges of $\ell_\infty$, we introduce a notion of subset embeddings, which embed points by simply selecting a subset of dimensions. In particular, we show one may preserve all pairwise distances of an $n$ point dataset up to a factor of $O(c)$ by computing distances on only $n^{1+1/c}$ coordinates. We also show a matching lower bound: for any $c > 1$, there exists a set of $n$ points in $\mathbb{R}^{d}$ such that any subset embedding for the set with approximation $c$ must have at least $n^{1+Ω(1/c)}$ coordinates.
  Using our subset embeddings, we give data structures for approximate nearest neighbor in $\ell_\infty$ with space $O(n^2\log d)$, query time $\tilde{O}(n^{1+1/c})$, and approximation $O(c\log\log n)$ for any $c \geq 1$.
  Finally, we give another data structure for the approximate nearest neighbor under $\ell_\infty$ with the same space and query time as our subset embedding approach, but with approximation $O(c^{\log_2 3}) \approx O(c^{1.58})$. This allows us to achieve $O(1)$-approximation with query time e.g.~$n^{1.01}$

</details>


### [165] [Streaming Algorithms for Gaussian Kernel Density Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09622)
*Qin Zhang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对生成式数据，提出利用高斯核的几何结构，在固定维欧几里得向量流中实现亚线性空间复杂度的单遍相似性感知的统计算法（如去重计数和频率矩），突破了通用相似性函数下的线性空间限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式系统产生的数据需要基于相似性而非严格相等的统计框架，但现有的通用相似性函数框架即使在粗略的单遍近似下也需要线性空间，无法高效处理大规模流数据。

Method: 研究固定维欧几里得向量流中基于高斯核的相似性感知统计量（多样性指数和高斯密度矩），利用高斯核的几何与分析性质设计单遍亚线性空间近似算法，并辅以理论下界证明。

Result: 成功构建了针对高斯核的单遍亚线性空间近似算法，并证明了其下界，表明利用特定的几何结构可以根本性地改变相似性感知统计分析的流计算复杂度。

Conclusion: 自然向量相似性中存在的几何结构（特别是高斯核）能够克服通用相似性函数的空间复杂度障碍，使得高效的大规模相似性感知流统计分析成为可能。

Abstract: Motivated by data produced by generative systems, \cite{LZ26b} formulates similarity-aware statistics via a weighted similarity graph, replacing equality with similarity in classical frequency-based statistics. Although this framework captures semantic relationships between nonidentical items, under general similarity functions even coarse one-pass approximation can require linear space. We therefore ask whether the geometric structure present in natural vector similarities can overcome this barrier. We answer this question affirmatively for the Gaussian kernel. For fixed-dimensional Euclidean vector streams, we study similarity-aware analogues of classical frequency statistics, including the number of distinct elements and frequency moments, through the diversity index and Gaussian density moments. We give one-pass sublinear-space approximation algorithms that exploit the geometric and analytic properties of the Gaussian kernel, and complement them with lower bounds. Our results show that geometric structure can fundamentally change the streaming complexity of similarity-aware statistical analysis.

</details>


### [166] [Subexponential Approximation of the Permanent in Deterministic Polynomial Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10516)
*Sergei Kudria,Jason Luo,Mahbod Majid*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 首提确定性多项式时间算法，以亚指数因子近似非负有理矩阵永久值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对无限制输入的确定性多项式时间算法，其近似因子均为指数级 $\exp(Ω(n))$，需突破此限制。

Method: 利用凸优化收紧永久值上界，该界基于二分图匹配加权和及未匹配顶点相关性控制误差；结合相关性衰减与顶点删除效应 bound 进行确定性近似。

Result: 对于 $n$ 阶矩阵，近似因子降至 $\exp(O(\frac{n(\log\log n)^2}{\log n})) = \exp(o(n))$，优于 prior $\exp(Ω(n))$。

Conclusion: 该算法在确定性多项式时间内实现了矩阵永久值的亚指数因子近似，显著改进了已有理论保证。

Abstract: We give the first deterministic polynomial time algorithm that approximates the permanent of arbitrary nonnegative rational matrices within a subexponential factor. For a matrix of order $n$, the approximation factor is \[
  \exp\!\left(O\!\left(\frac{n(\log\log n)^2}{\log n}\right)\right)=\exp(o(n)). \] All previously known deterministic polynomial time guarantees for unrestricted inputs had approximation factors $\exp(Ω(n))$.
  Our proof uses convex optimization to tighten an upper bound on the permanent. The bound is based on weighted sums over all matchings in a bipartite graph representing the matrix, and correlations between unmatched vertices control its error. We approximate these sums deterministically using correlation decay and a bound on the effect of vertex deletion.

</details>


### [167] [Fast Algorithms for Sparse PCA and Robust Sparse Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09701)
*Giannis Iakovidis,Ankit Pensia*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于稀疏主成分分析（Sparse-PCA）认证的双准则快速算法，将运行时间从通用的$O(d^4)$降低至$O(d^2)$甚至亚二次时间，并首次实现了鲁棒稀疏估计的二次及亚二次时间算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的稀疏PCA认证标准半定松弛（SDR）方法虽然有效，但通用求解器需要$Ω(d^4)$的时间，计算成本过高，亟需更高效的算法来处理高维数据。

Method: 提出一种双准则算法：将高值稀疏方向归约为大相关性图中的有界半径集合，并搜索生成的候选支持集；在样本访问模型中，利用快速相关性检测构建图，无需形成经验协方差矩阵即可实现亚二次时间复杂度。

Result: 算法运行时间为$O(d^2+d k^{O(\log k)})$，当$k\leq\exp(O(\sqrt{\log d}))$时为$O(d^2)$；在样本访问模型下，对于$k=\mathrm{polylog}(d)$可实现$d^{2 - Ω(1)}$的亚二次时间；成功应用于鲁棒稀疏估计，提供了该类问题的首个二次及亚二次时间算法。

Conclusion: 该研究突破了稀疏PCA认证的计算瓶颈，通过创新的图论方法和快速相关性检测技术，显著提升了算法效率，为高维统计估计提供了高效的计算工具。

Abstract: We study fast algorithms for sparse-PCA certification. Given a positive semidefinite matrix $M$, the problem asks either to rule out a large $k$-sparse quadratic form or to return a high-value (relaxed) witness. The standard semidefinite relaxation provides such certificates, but existing general-purpose solvers require $Ω(d^4)$ time. We give a bicriteria algorithm running in $O(d^2+d k^{O(\log k)})$ time: if some $k$-sparse unit vector has quadratic form greater than $2$, it returns either an $O(k^2)$-sparse unit vector or an SDP-feasible matrix of value at least $1$. For $k\leq\exp(O(\sqrt{\log d}))$, this running time is $O(d^2)$. We also go below the quadratic barrier in the sample-access model: Given $n=d^{o(1)}$ samples, our algorithm obtains a related one-sided certificate in $d^{2 - Ω(1)}$ time for $k=\mathrm{polylog}(d)$, without forming the empirical covariance matrix. As an application, these certificate routines yield the first quadratic and subquadratic-time algorithms for robust sparse estimation for broad families of distributions. Our sparse-PCA algorithm reduces a high-value sparse direction to a bounded-radius set in the graph of large correlations and searches the resulting candidate supports. The subquadratic implementation constructs this graph using fast correlation detection.

</details>


### [168] [A Sharp Barrier for Consistent Submodular Maximization: Any Improvement over $2-\sqrt{2}$ Entails Exponential Queries or Linear Recourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09986)
*Shi Fu,Qixin Zhang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文解决了动态子模化最大化中一致性（稳定性）与近似比的权衡问题，证明了在多项式时间查询和最坏情况常数调整次数下，最优近似比为 $2-\sqrt{2} \approx 0.5858$，严格小于离线最优的 $1-1/e$，并给出了相应的随机算法及下界证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dütting 等人 (2025) 提出了在元素随时间到达时，如何在保持解的稳定性（每次插入仅调整 $O(1)$ 个元素）的同时最大化单调子模目标函数的问题。他们留下了一个开放性问题：是否存在高效算法能达到离线场景下的 $1-1/e$ 近似保证。本文旨在解决这一开放问题，量化“一致性”带来的计算成本。

Method: 作者通过理论证明确立了多项式次值查询和最坏情况常数回溯（recourse）下的上界 $\beta = 2-\sqrt{2}$。提出了一种随机算法，对于任意 $\varepsilon>0$，能以 $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ 的每次插入调整次数达到 $\beta-\varepsilon$ 的近似比。同时，通过构造下界证明，任何固定的性能提升都需要指数级查询或线性级的调整次数。此外，还分析了曲率依赖阈值及加权覆盖问题的特殊情况。

Result: 1. 确定了多项式时间及常数回溯下的最优近似比上界为 $2-\sqrt{2} \approx 0.5858$，证明了其严格小于 $1-1/e$。
2. 设计了随机算法，在 $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ 的回溯代价下实现 $\beta-\varepsilon$ 近似。
3. 证明了若要超越该界限，需付出指数级查询成本或 $\Omega(k)$ 的线性回溯代价。
4. 确定了精确的曲率依赖阈值 $1-(\sqrt{2}-1)\vartheta$，并在加权覆盖问题上以 $O(\varepsilon^{-1})$ 回溯达到了 $1-1/e-\varepsilon$。
5. 分离了通用未来价格证书的存在性与其高效计算的可能性。

Conclusion: 一致性约束（即限制解的变动次数）在动态子模化最大化中引入了不可消除的性能损失，其理论上限为 $2-\sqrt{2}$。这一差距量化了因预言机（oracle）无法预知未来元素需求而付出的代价。虽然特定条件下（如加权覆盖）可接近离线最优，但在一般多项式时间且低回溯的设定下，无法达到 $1-1/e$ 的保证。

Abstract: Consistent submodular maximization studies the tradeoff between solution quality and stability when elements arrive over time. For a monotone submodular objective, which models diminishing returns, an algorithm maintains a set of at most $k$ available elements and changes only $O(1)$ elements after each insertion. Dütting et al. [2025] established a tight $2/3$ approximation with unrestricted computation and a polynomial-time $0.51$ approximation. They left open at STOC 2025 whether efficient algorithms can match the offline $1-1/e$ guarantee. We resolve this problem by proving that the supremum approximation achievable with polynomially many value queries and worst-case constant recourse is \[ β=2-\sqrt2\approx0.5858<1-1/e. \] For every $\varepsilon>0$, our randomized algorithm attains $β-\varepsilon$ with $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ changes per insertion. Any fixed improvement requires exponentially many queries before one critical insertion or linear recourse of $Ω(k)$ changes at that insertion, even with unlimited queries afterwards. This gap quantifies the cost of consistency: the current oracle hides which elements will be needed after an arrival. We also determine the exact curvature-dependent threshold $1-(\sqrt2-1)\vartheta$, attain $1-1/e-\varepsilon$ for weighted coverage with $O(\varepsilon^{-1})$ recourse, and separate the existence of universal future-price certificates from their efficient computation. Our algorithm has a bounded-bit polynomial-time implementation for polynomial-bit rational oracle answers; the lower bound uses only logarithmic-bit rational answers.

</details>


### [169] [Minimum-makespan completion and vertex selection leave the Wang-Sitters constant at 11/6](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10004)
*Adam Y. Shavit*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明消除 Wang-Sitters 舍入方案第三步中的任意性并不能改善其 11/6 的最坏情况常数，揭示了该误差来源并非仅由第三步的自由度导致，并给出了相关的结构限制和反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究 Wang-Sitters 舍入方案中 11/6 最坏情况常数的根本来源，特别是验证该误差是否主要归因于第三步中允许任意有效槽匹配的自由度。

Method: 通过理论分析证明即使引入最小完工时间预言机（minimum-makespan completion oracle）或限制第一步仅针对松弛问题的顶点，最坏情况常数仍为 11/6；同时构造七机器实例反驳自然的两阶段修复猜想，并分析广义三路径族的结构特性。

Result: 1. 消除第三步自由度无法改善 11/6 常数；2. 最小完工时间预言机的最坏情况常数精确为 11/6；3. 关于该预言机的两个自然 7/4 猜想被证伪；4. 限制第一步无助于改进结果；5. 发现过载仅局限于两种形状的结构特征，并确立了广义三路径族的严格 7/4 上界。

Conclusion: Wang-Sitters 方案的 11/6 性能损失不能单纯归咎于第三步的自由度，该方案存在更深层的结构性限制，且特定的反例结构（如七机器实例）阻碍了自然修复策略的有效性。

Abstract: The 11/6 worst-case constant of the Wang-Sitters rounding scheme, which a companion note establishes, can naturally be attributed to the freedom in Step 3, where an arbitrary valid slot matching is permitted. We show that eliminating that freedom does not improve the constant. A minimum-makespan completion oracle still has worst-case constant exactly 11/6 against the optimum; both natural 7/4 statements about it are false; and restricting Step 1 to vertices of the relaxation does not help. The loss therefore cannot be attributed solely to the freedom in Step 3. We also record what structure survives: a reduction confining every overload to two shapes, a seven-machine instance defeating the natural two-phase repair, and a strict 7/4 bound on the generalized three-path family.

</details>


### [170] [Optimal Non-Adaptive Vantage Point Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10267)
*Jie Gao,Nicole Wein,Chang Wu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了“视点选择”问题，旨在通过查询顶点来揭示图中最短路径上的瓶颈边容量。在单查询设置下，确定了最佳竞争比为 $\tildeΘ(n^{2/3})$；在多查询设置下，推导出了非自适应算法的复杂竞争比边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题由 Ashvinkumar 等人提出，用于模拟互联网上瓶颈容量的估计问题。现有研究在单查询设置下的竞争比上下界存在较大差距（上界 $O(n)$，下界 $\tildeΩ(\sqrt{n})$），且缺乏对多查询设置下算法性能的精确理论分析。

Method: 1. 针对单查询设置，设计了一种新算法并构建了匹配的下界证明（忽略多对数因子）。
2. 将结果推广至通用设置，其中最优算法允许 $k$ 次查询，而本文算法允许 $αk$ 次查询（$α\geq 1$）。
3. 提出了一种随机非自适应算法，并证明了其匹配的下界。

Result: 1. 单查询设置：证明了最佳可能竞争比为 $\tildeΘ(n^{2/3})$，填补了原有上下界之间的空白。
2. 多查询设置：确定了非自适应算法的最佳期望竞争比为 $\tildeΘ\left( \min\left\{ \frac{n}{αk}, \max\left( \sqrt{\frac{n}α}, \frac{n^{2/3}}{αk^{1/3}} \right) \right\} \right)$。

Conclusion: 本文完全解决了单查询视点选择问题的竞争比复杂度，并给出了多查询设置下非自适应算法的精确竞争比边界，揭示了该问题在不同查询参数下的复杂渐近行为。

Abstract: We study the \emph{vantage point selection} problem, introduced by Ashvinkumar, Chowdhury, Gao, Goswami, Mitchell, and Polishchuk [WADS'25] to model the problem of estimating bottleneck capacities on the Internet. The input is a weighted undirected graph with unique shortest paths where every edge has a distinct unknown \emph{capacity}. When the algorithm \emph{queries} a vertex $v$, it reveals the minimum-capacity edge on the shortest path from $v$ to every other vertex reachable from $v$. The goal is to maximize the total number of revealed edges. The quality of an algorithm is measured by its competitive ratio against an optimal algorithm that knows all edge capacities a priori.
  We first consider the foundational single-query setting, where both the algorithm and the optimal algorithm are restricted to a single query. There is a trivial upper bound of $O(n)$ on the competitive ratio and the best known lower bound was $\tildeΩ(\sqrt{n})$. We provide an algorithm and matching lower bound (up to polylogarithmic factors) showing that the best possible competitive ratio is $\tildeΘ(n^{2/3})$.
  Furthermore, we extend our results to the general setting where the optimal algorithm is allowed $k$ queries and our algorithm is allowed $αk$ queries for $α\geq 1$. We present a randomized non-adaptive algorithm and matching lower bound (up to polylogarithmic factors) showing that the best possible expected competitive ratio for non-adaptive algorithms is the following surprisingly complex bound: $$ \tildeΘ\left( \min\left\{ \frac{n}{αk}, \max\left( \sqrt{\frac{n}α}, \frac{n^{2/3}}{αk^{1/3}} \right) \right\} \right). $$

</details>


### [171] [Testing the Binary Rank with Polynomial Query Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10496)
*Michal Parnas*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对0-1矩阵二元秩的自适应双边误差测试算法，其查询复杂度为 $O(d^3\log(d+1)/\varepsilon^2)$，解决了Parnas等人提出的关于二元秩测试复杂度是否多项式依赖于 $d$ 和 $1/\varepsilon$ 的开放性问题；此外，该算法还能在承诺二元秩不超过 $d$ 的前提下，以高概率找到矩阵的近似二元分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应Parnas, Ron和Shraibman提出的开放性问题，即是否存在查询复杂度在二元秩界限 $d$ 和距离参数 $1/\varepsilon$ 上均为多项式的二元秩测试算法。

Method: 设计了一种自适应的双边误差测试算法，用于测试0-1矩阵的二元秩，并在测试基础上扩展了寻找近似二元分解的功能。

Result: 实现了查询复杂度为 $O(d^3\log(d+1)/\varepsilon^2)$ 的测试算法；在额外 $d(n+m)$ 次查询下，能以至少 $5/6$ 的概率找到两个0-1矩阵 $A', B'$，使其乘积 $M'$ 与原矩阵 $M$ 的误差 entries 比例不超过 $O(\varepsilon)$。

Conclusion: 该研究不仅确立了二元秩测试的多项式查询复杂度可行性，还提供了在低秩承诺下高效构建近似二元分解的方法，推动了矩阵性质测试领域的发展。

Abstract: We provide an adaptive two-sided error testing algorithm for the binary rank of a $0,1$ matrix $M$ with query complexity $O(d^3\log(d+1)/ε^2)$, where $d$ is the tested binary rank bound and $ε$ is the distance parameter. This answers an open question posed by Parnas, Ron and Shraibman~\cite{parnas2021property}, who asked whether the binary rank can be tested with query complexity polynomial in $d$ and $1/ε$.
  Furthermore, our testing algorithm can be used to find an approximate binary decomposition of $M$ with an additional $d(n+m)$ queries. That is, under the promise that the binary rank of $M$ is at most $d$, we show how to find, with probability at least $5/6$, two $0,1$ matrices $A',B'$ such that $M' = A' \cdot B'$ is a $0,1$ matrix which differs from $M$ on at most an $O(ε)$ fraction of its entries.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [Reliability-Aware Hybrid-K Ensemble Selection for Cervical Cytology Classification: Integrating Discrimination, Calibration, and Selective Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09189)
*Nisreen Albzour,Sarah S. Lam*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本研究提出可靠性感知的 Hybrid-K 集成框架，用于宫颈细胞学分类，旨在优化校准置信度与不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床图像分析不仅需高准确率，更需校准后的置信度与可靠的不确定性估计以保障决策安全。

Method: 基于 SIPaKMeD 数据集评估 9 种架构，经温度缩放后，利用多指标复合评分排序构建 Hybrid-K 软投票集成，并通过 Dirichlet 采样及留一法验证鲁棒性。

Result: Hybrid-2 集成（Swin-Tiny+TinyViT-5M）较最佳单模型显著降低 AURC(43%)、NLL(17%) 及 WC-ECE(36%)，且在 96.8% 权重场景下稳定，但校正后单指标增益无统计学显著性。

Conclusion: 框架在内部验证下识别出对指标权重鲁棒的紧凑集成，改善了可靠性估计，但因缺乏独立校准集，结果具探索性。

Abstract: High classification accuracy alone is insufficient for clinical image analysis, where calibrated confidence and reliable uncertainty estimates are essential. This study proposes a reliability-aware Hybrid-K ensemble selection framework for multiclass cervical cytology classification using the SIPaKMeD dataset. Nine deep learning architectures were evaluated using a fixed stratified five-fold partition and three training seeds. After post-hoc temperature scaling, models were assessed using macro-F1, accuracy, AUROC, expected calibration error (ECE), worst-class ECE (WC-ECE), area under the risk-coverage curve (AURC), Brier score, and negative log-likelihood (NLL). Models were ranked using an equal-weight composite score, and Hybrid-K ensembles were formed from the top-ranked models using soft voting. Robustness was examined using 5,000 Dirichlet-sampled metric-weight vectors, leave-one-metric-out analysis, and corrected paired testing across 15 fold-by-seed evaluations. The final Hybrid-2 ensemble, comprising Swin-Tiny and TinyViT-5M, reduced AURC by 43%, NLL by 17%, and WC-ECE by 36% relative to the best individual model. It was selected in 96.8% of random weighting scenarios, remained unchanged across all leave-one-metric-out analyses, and improved the full composite score. However, per-metric gains were not statistically significant after Holm-Bonferroni correction (all adjusted p >= 0.168). Because post-hoc calibration did not use a fully independent calibration set, calibration-dependent results should be interpreted as exploratory internal estimates. Overall, the framework identified a compact ensemble robust to alternative metric weightings and improved reliability point estimates under internal validation on a single dataset.

</details>


### [173] [CHIMERA Challenge Task 2 and 3: Response Subtypes Classification and Progression Survival Prediction in Bladder Cancer Patients using Multimodal Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09510)
*Catherine Chia,Tongjie Wang,Robert Spaans,Maryam Mohammadlou,Farbod Khoraminia,J. Alberto Nakauma-González,Adam Kowalewski,Parandzem Khachatryan,Domingos Oliveira,Khrystyna Faryna,CHIMERA Challenge Consortium,Marlies Wakkee,Sita Vermeulen,Tahlita Zuiverloon,Nadieh Khalili*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: CHIMERA 挑战建立了高风险非肌层浸润性膀胱癌（HR-NMIBC）的多模态 AI 基准，评估了利用组织病理学、临床数据和 RNA 测序预测 BCG 反应亚型及疾病进展的性能。最佳模型在 BCG 反应预测（F1=0.73）和进展预测（C-index=0.68）上表现尚可，但分析揭示了模型在不同队列间的性能退化、对缺失数据的敏感性以及 T1 亚分期患者的预测难点，强调了多中心验证和缺失感知建模的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险非肌层浸润性膀胱癌（HR-NMIBC）具有高复发和进展风险，但现有的临床风险分层方法存在局限性，缺乏标准化的多模态 AI 预测基准来评估模型在真实场景下的鲁棒性和可迁移性。

Method: 发起名为 CHIMERA 的多模态 AI 挑战赛，构建包含 368 名患者的数据集（分为公开训练集和隐藏验证/测试集）。设立两个任务：Task BRS（基于组织病理学和结构化数据预测 RNA 定义的 BCG 反应亚型）和 Task Progression（结合组织病理学、结构化数据和 RNA 测序建模进展时间）。收集 159 份提交，筛选 13 个顶尖模型进行基准测试和误差分析。

Result: 最佳模型在 Task BRS 中达到加权 F1 分数 0.73，在 Task Progression 中达到 C-index 0.68。分析发现：1) 模态贡献具有任务依赖性（BRS 中组织病理学可部分补偿结构化变量，进展预测更依赖互补输入）；2) 存在队列依赖的性能退化；3) 模型对结构化数据缺失敏感；4) T1 亚分期患者是跨模型一致的预测难点。

Conclusion: CHIMERA 提供了膀胱癌领域的标准化多模态基准，不仅评估模型性能，还揭示了可迁移性障碍、信息充分性及患者级预测失败模式。研究强调了开发缺失感知模型和进行独立多机构验证对于提升临床 AI 鲁棒性的关键作用。

Abstract: High-risk non-muscle-invasive bladder cancer (HR-NMIBC) carries substantial risks of recurrence and progression, while current clinical risk stratification remains limited. CHIMERA was established as a multimodal AI challenge to benchmark prediction in HR-NMIBC under standardized evaluation. Task BRS predicts RNA-seq-defined BCG Response Subtypes from histopathology and structured clinicopathological data, whereas Task Progression models time-to-progression using histopathology, structured data, and RNA sequencing. A multimodal dataset of 368 patients was divided into public training and hidden validation and test sets. In total, 159 submissions were made, and 13 top-performing models were selected for benchmarking. The best models achieved a weighted F1 score of 0.73 for Task BRS and a C-index of 0.68 for Task Progression. Post-challenge analyses revealed task-dependent modality contributions, cohort-dependent performance degradation, and sensitivity to missing structured data. In Task BRS, histopathology partly compensated for pathology-derived structured variables, whereas progression models showed greater dependence on complementary inputs. Cross-model error analysis further identified patients that were consistently difficult across different architectures, with T1 substage associated with prediction difficulty. These findings highlight barriers to transportability and the importance of missingness-aware modeling and independent multi-institutional validation. CHIMERA provides a standardized multimodal benchmark for bladder cancer and a framework for studying not only model performance, but also robustness, information sufficiency, and patient-level prediction failure.

</details>


### [174] [Myocardial Strain Drift Correction in Deep Learning Based Ultrasound Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09577)
*Thierry Judge,Nicolas Duchateau,Andreas Østvik,Havard Dalen,Bjørnar Grenne,Pierre-Yves Courand,Lasse Lovstakken,Pierre-Marc Jodoin,Olivier Bernard*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合持久记忆令牌和师生微调策略的深度学习框架，旨在解决超声心动图心肌运动追踪中的时间漂移问题，从而提高心肌应变估算的准确性和临床可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度学习心肌运动追踪方法虽然性能强劲，但往往缺乏生理约束，导致在整个心动周期中出现时间漂移。这使得追踪点在周期结束时无法回到相对初始位置，造成应变估算不准确甚至发散。

Method: 扩展了最先进的TAS-Net方法，引入持久记忆令牌（persistent memory tokens）在滑动窗口间共享全心动周期的信息；并采用师生微调策略（teacher-student fine-tuning）在真实超声数据上强制实现生理一致性的循环运动，同时保持追踪精度。

Result: 实验表明，该方法显著减少了全局和区域应变的漂移，提高了与临床参考值的一致性，并改善了重测复现性（test-retest reproducibility）。

Conclusion: 该框架通过补偿追踪漂移，支持了更可靠的心肌应变估算，具有更高的临床应用价值。

Abstract: Myocardial strain from echocardiography is a key biomarker for cardiac function. Recent deep learning methods show strong performance for myocardial motion tracking but often lack physiological constraints, leading to temporal drift across the cardiac cycle. Consequently, tracked points may not return to their relative initial positions at the end of each cardiac cycle, producing inaccurate strain estimates and even divergence in some cases. We propose a deep learning framework that compensates for drift during myocardial tracking. We extend a state-of-the-art echocardiographic tracking method (TAS-Net) with persistent memory tokens that share information across sliding windows over full cardiac cycles. A teacher-student fine-tuning strategy on real echocardiographic data then enforces physiologically consistent cyclic motion while preserving tracking accuracy. Experiments show reduced global and regional strain drift, improved agreement with clinical references, and better test-retest reproducibility, supporting more reliable myocardial strain estimation in clinical practice.

</details>


### [175] [Morphological Decoupling-Based Skeletal Classification for Clinical Assessment of Malocclusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09801)
*Zhichun Jin,Zhicheng He,Hao Xu,Dongyang Li,Lin Wang,Hongliang Ren,Long Bai*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出TeethGNN，一种基于图神经网络的自动系统，通过融合CBCT图像特征与形态学信息，并引入协同校准策略，实现了高效准确的错颌畸形骨骼分级，性能优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的错颌畸形骨骼分级依赖人工测量CBCT重建的头影测量图，存在耗时、费力且受操作者间差异影响大的问题，亟需一种自动、可靠且高效的CBCT辅助诊断系统。

Method: 开发TeethGNN框架：1) 使用解耦可学习解码器直接从CBCT图像预测关键形态学指标；2) 利用图神经网络（GNN）融合图像特征与形态学特征以建模模态间关系；3) 引入协同校准策略，结合多尺度图对抗扰动（显式校准）和非线性拓扑图校准（隐式置信度调整）以增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在临床数据集上的实验表明，该系统准确率达到77.08%，AUC达到89.61%，显著优于对比的state-of-the-art方法。

Conclusion: 基于图的多模态融合与协同校准策略能有效提升错颌畸形分级性能，该系统为基于视觉的临床测量与诊断提供了准确可靠的解决方案，具有推动计算机辅助正畸诊断发展的潜力。

Abstract: Malocclusion skeletal grading is a fundamental task in orthodontics, critical for diagnosis and treatment planning. Traditionally, cone-beam computed tomography (CBCT) is used for visual measurement, and the reconstructed lateral cephalograms are handed over to expert dentists for diagnosis. However, manual review is time-consuming, labor-intensive, and subject to inter-operator variability. Therefore, an automatic CBCT-based system is needed for reliable malocclusion skeletal grading.
