In this case, we develop TeethGNN, a novel graph-based framework designed to combine CBCT image features with morphological information for accurate and efficient malocclusion grading. TeethGNN utilizes a decoupled learnable decoder to directly predict key morphological indicators from CBCT images, eliminating the need for manual measurements. These morphological features are then fused with image features using a graph neural network (GNN), which effectively models the relationships between the modalities. To further enhance robustness and calibration, we introduce a collaborative calibration strategy. This strategy combines multi-scale graph adversarial perturbation for explicit calibration and nonlinear topological graph calibration for implicit confidence adjustment. Extensive experiments and ablation studies on our collected clinical dataset demonstrate that our malocclusion measurement system achieves 77.08\% in accuracy and 89.61\% in AUC, outperforming the compared state-of-the-art methods. These results validate the effectiveness of graph-based multimodal fusion and collaborative calibration in improving malocclusion grading performance. Our system shows strong potential for advancing computer-aided orthodontic diagnosis, providing an accurate and reliable solution for vision-based clinical measurement and diagnosis.

</details>


### [176] [Cost-Aware Vision--Language Model Arbitration for Fabric Structure Recognition A Deployable Multi-Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10065)
*Chenwei Wang,Haochen Li,Shuk Ching Tang,Misbah Iqbal,Carman Lee,Elif Ozden-Yenigun*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多智能体系统，结合轻量级CNN级联和选择性视觉语言模型（VLM），在织物结构识别任务中实现了高精度与低成本的平衡，显著减少了VLM调用次数并提升了难分类别的识别率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯CNN分类器成本低但在视觉模糊类别上表现不佳，而视觉语言模型（VLM）泛化能力强但成本高且在专业领域不稳定；现有方法难以同时满足下游供应链系统对准确性、成本和推理速度的需求。

Method: 设计了一个多智能体系统：由蒸馏后的轻量级CNN级联处理大多数简单样本，仅当置信度低时触发VLM作为选择性仲裁器，并限制其输出为前3个符合织物分类体系的结果；利用织物分类体系作为全局约束提升准确性并减少VLM调用；CNN级联经过蒸馏以减小参数量，优化推理速度。

Result: 在14类新基准上，该方法top-1准确率达93.94%，最难四类准确率达94.50%（比基线提升17.6个百分点）；VLM触发率从60%降至10%以下，API成本降低约90%且无精度损失；无VLM调用时CPU推理时间≤93ms（蒸馏后仅9.3ms）；每次预测生成机器可读的推理记录。

Conclusion: 该方法通过层级化级联与分类体系约束，有效平衡了精度、成本与效率，为纺织供应链数字化提供了高精度、低延迟且可追溯的织物结构识别解决方案。

Abstract: Recognizing a fabric's structure is a prerequisite for translating textile-specific material information into structured digital form for downstream supply-chain systems. Pure CNN classifiers are cost-efficient but fail on visually ambiguous categories; vision--language models (VLMs) generalize more broadly but cost much more per image and are unstable on specialist domains. We present a multi-agent system in which a CNN cascade handles the easy majority and a VLM is invoked only as a selective arbiter, constrained to a top-3 taxonomy-consistent choice. The fabric taxonomy performs as a constraint for the whole recognition process to increase the accuracy and reduce the VLM calls. Meanwhile, the CNN cascade is distilled to a small parameter size to reduce the inference time and meet the needs of practical deployment. On a newly curated 14-class benchmark, a flat ConvNeXt-Tiny baseline reaches $90.45\,\%$ top-1 and $76.9\,\%$ on the four hardest classes; \method's hierarchical cascade reaches $93.94\,\%$ top-1 and $94.50\,\%$ hard ($+17.6$\,pp). Tightening the VLM trigger from $60\,\%$ to $<\!10\,\%$ cuts API cost by ${\sim}90\,\%$ with no measurable accuracy loss. CPU inference is $\le\!93$\,ms without a VLM call ($9.3$\,ms distilled). Each prediction carries a machine-readable reasoning record, offered as an entry point for future supply-chain documentation.

</details>


### [177] [Control Copy-Paste: Controllable Diffusion-Based Augmentation Method for Remote Sensing Few-Shot Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.21816)
*Yanxing Liu,Jiancheng Pan,Bingchen Zhang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“Control Copy-Paste”的可控扩散模型方法，通过将少样本新类别物体注入到具有多样化上下文的图像中，解决了光学遥感图像少样本目标检测（FSOD）中因上下文单一导致的过拟合问题，在DIOR数据集上平均提升了10.76%的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散模型的FSOD方法仅关注增加物体本身的多样性，忽略了周围上下文（context）的关键作用。当物体多样性足够时，检测器容易对单调的上下文产生过拟合，且有限的训练数据难以表征真实遥感场景的数据分布。

Method: 提出Control Copy-Paste方法：1. 利用条件扩散模型，将少样本新类别物体无缝注入到具有多样化上下文的图像中；2. 开发方向对齐策略（orientation alignment strategy），以缓解因实例长宽比变化引起的融合失真。

Result: 在公共DIOR数据集上的实验表明，该方法使检测性能平均提升了10.76%。

Conclusion: 通过利用多样化的上下文信息增强少样本实例，Control Copy-Paste有效缓解了FSOD中的过拟合问题，显著提升了光学遥感图像中的稀有物体检测性能。

Abstract: Few-shot object detection (FSOD) for optical remote sensing images aims to detect rare objects with only a few annotated bounding boxes. The limited training data makes it difficult to represent the data distribution of realistic remote sensing scenes, which results in the notorious overfitting problem. Current researchers have begun to enhance the diversity of few-shot novel instances by leveraging diffusion models to solve the overfitting problem. However, naively increasing the diversity of objects is insufficient, as surrounding contexts also play a crucial role in object detection, and in cases where the object diversity is sufficient, the detector tends to overfit to monotonous contexts. Accordingly, we propose Control Copy-Paste, a controllable diffusion-based method to enhance the performance of FSOD by leveraging diverse contextual information. Specifically, we seamlessly inject a few-shot novel objects into images with diverse contexts by a conditional diffusion model. We also develop an orientation alignment strategy to mitigate the integration distortion caused by varying aspect ratios of instances. Experiments on the public DIOR dataset demonstrate that our method can improve detection performance by an average of 10.76%.

</details>


### [178] [Diverse Instance Generation via Diffusion Models for Enhanced Few-Shot Object Detection in Remote Sensing Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18031)
*Yanxing Liu,Jiancheng Pan,Jianwei Yang,Tiancheng Chen,Peiling Zhou,Bingchen Zhang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用预训练扩散模型生成多样化遥感实例切片的新框架，通过切片到切片生成、类无关图像反演模块及语义对比损失，显著提升了遥感图像少样本目标检测（FSOD）的性能，平均提升4.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感图像少样本目标检测方法虽取得进展，但受限于训练样本实例多样性不足，难以应对濒危物种监测和灾害评估等应用中 novel 实例检测的挑战。

Method: 1. 利用在大规模自然图像上预训练的扩散模型，通过专用的“切片到切片”模块生成实例级遥感切片；2. 将生成的切片嵌入全尺寸图像进行数据增强；3. 开发类无关图像反演模块，将遥感实例切片反演至语义空间以适配遥感场景；4. 引入对比损失以实现合成图像与对应类别的语义对齐。

Result: 在多个数据集和不同方法上，该框架平均性能提升4.4%；消融实验证明，设计的反演模块能有效提升FSOD性能，语义对比损失可进一步boost性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架通过扩散模型生成多样化遥感实例并结合语义对齐策略，有效解决了遥感FSOD中实例多样性受限的问题，为相关应用提供了高性能解决方案。

Abstract: Few-shot object detection (FSOD) aims to detect novel instances with only a limited number of labeled training samples, presenting a challenge that is particularly prominent in numerous remote sensing applications such as endangered species monitoring and disaster assessment. Existing FSOD methods for remote sensing images (RSIs) have achieved promising progress but remain constrained by the limited diversity of instances. To address this issue, we propose a novel framework that can leverage a diffusion model pretrained on large-scale natural images to synthesize diverse remote sensing instances, thereby improving the performance of few-shot object detectors. Instead of directly synthesizing complete remote sensing images, we first generate instance-level slices via a specialized slice-to-slice module, and then embed these slices into full-scale imagery for enhanced data augmentation. To further adapt diffusion models for remote sensing scenarios, we develop a class-agnostic image inversion module that can invert remote sensing instance slices into semantic space. Additionally, we introduce contrastive loss to semantically align the synthesized images with their corresponding classes. Experimental results show that our method hasachieved an average performance improvement of 4.4% across multiple datasets and various approaches. Ablation experiments indicate that the elaborately designed inversion module can effectively enhance the performance of FSOD methods, and the semantic contrastive loss can further boost the performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [Stability of Fork-Join Systems with Redundancy and Heterogeneous Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09237)
*Chutong Gao,Seyed Iravani,Ohad Perry*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有冗余和异构服务器的叉 - 汇（FJR）系统在静态和动态容量分配策略下的稳定性问题，确定了最大稳定性区域，并给出了静态和动态策略下实现该区域的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决具有冗余机制的叉 - 汇系统在异构服务器环境下的稳定性分析问题，明确在不同容量分配策略下系统能够保持稳定的最大流量强度范围，为系统设计和资源调度提供理论依据。

Method: 通过将 (n,k) FJR 系统投影到无冗余的简化 (k,k) 系统，并结合基于广义 Schur-凸序的多维过程新型样本路径比较论证，分析静态和动态容量分配策略下的稳定性条件。

Result: 1. 确定了标称流量强度并刻画了最大稳定性区域；2. 对于静态分配策略，当最快服务器分配的总服务容量不超过 1/k 时，可实现最大稳定性区域；3. 对于动态分配策略，当分配给前 j 个最短队列的累积容量不超过总容量的 j/k（对所有 j=1,...,k-1）时，可实现最大稳定性区域。

Conclusion: 通过投影方法和样本路径比较论证，成功刻画了 FJR 系统在静态和动态策略下的最大稳定性区域，并给出了具体的容量分配条件，为冗余系统的稳定性控制提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We consider the stability problem of fork-join systems with redundancy (FJR) and heterogeneous servers under both static and dynamic capacity-allocation policies. In an $(n,k)$ FJR system, each arriving job is split into $n$ independent tasks, with one task assigned to each of $n$ parallel servers. Once $k \le n$ tasks have been processed, they are joined and the corresponding job departs the system; the remaining $n-k$ unprocessed tasks are then removed and are therefore termed redundant. We first identify the nominal traffic intensity and characterize the maximal stability region, defined as the set of traffic intensities for which there exists an admissible policy that stabilizes the system. We then establish conditions under which this maximal stability region is attained for two classes of policies: static and dynamic. Specifically, we show that for static allocation policies, in which service capacities remain fixed over time, maximality is achieved whenever the fastest server is allocated no more than $1/k$ of the total service capacity. For dynamic allocation policies, in which a fixed total service capacity may be repeatedly reallocated among the servers, we show that maximality is achieved whenever the cumulative capacity allocated to the $j$ shortest queues does not exceed $j/k$ of the total capacity for every $j=1,\ldots,k-1$. Our analysis is based on a projection of the $(n,k)$ FJR system onto a simpler $(k,k)$ system that has no redundancy, together with a novel sample-path comparison argument for multidimensional processes based on the generalized Schur-convex order.

</details>


### [180] [Optimality of Kløve Arrays within the Symmetric Kløve-Mossige Class](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09239)
*Lilin Yan,Hongwei Zhao*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文通过计算机辅助证明，确认了在固定传感器数量下，具有 contiguous sum co-arrays 的最小冗余对称 Kløve-Mossige 阵列的最优解均属于 Kløve 类，从而将优化搜索范围缩小至 Kløve 参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应 Rajamäki 和 Koivunen 提出的疑问：具有 contiguous sum co-arrays 的最小冗余对称 Kløve-Mossige 阵列是否总是 Kløve 阵列。

Method: 采用计算机辅助证明方法：首先分类生成器与其平移反射之间的重叠情况，进而对假设存在于 Kløve 类之外的最优解的孔径进行界限估计；将这些界限与经典的 Kløve 构造进行对比以解决传感器数量大于等于 330 的情况，并利用精确整数证书覆盖 2 至 329 的剩余情况。

Result: 证明了对于所有传感器数量，最大化传感器集合均属于 Kløve 类，且所有相等情况均对应于 Kløve 阵列；确立了在对称 Kløve-Mossige 类内的优化可简化为对 Kløve 参数的搜索。

Conclusion: 该定理证实了在特定的对称 Kløve-Mossige 类中，最优阵列结构必然落在 Kløve 类内，但该结论不适用于无限制的稀疏阵列或所有受限加法基。

Abstract: Rajam$\unicode{228}$ki and Koivunen asked whether minimum-redundancy symmetric Kl$\unicode{248}$ve-Mossige arrays with contiguous sum co-arrays are always Kl$\unicode{248}$ve arrays. We give a computer-assisted proof that, at every fixed sensor count, every maximizing sensor set belongs to the Kl$\unicode{248}$ve class. The argument classifies the overlaps between a generator and its shifted reflection, then bounds the aperture of a hypothetical optimizer outside the Kl$\unicode{248}$ve class. Comparing these bounds with a classical Kl$\unicode{248}$ve construction settles all sensor counts at least 330. An exact integer certificate covers the remaining counts from 2 through 329 and matches every equality case to a Kl$\unicode{248}$ve array as a complete set. Consequently, optimization within the full symmetric Kl$\unicode{248}$ve-Mossige class reduces to the previously known search over Kl$\unicode{248}$ve parameters. The theorem concerns this specified class, rather than unrestricted sparse arrays or all restricted additive bases.

</details>


### [181] [On the Sequential Test and Distributed Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09358)
*Earnest Akofor*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了停止时间的简单定义及其在集中式和分布式检测序列检验中的作用，推导了最优停止时间的上界，并将结果推广至任意非循环有向图形式的分布式检测网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为集中式和分布式检测中的序列检验提供一个简单且直接的框架，以获取最优决策规则，并解决最优停止时间的定性与定量分析问题。

Method: 通过定义停止时间，推导最优停止时间的数学上界，并进行数值模拟以验证其定性特征；随后将理论框架扩展至基于非循环有向图（DAG）的分布式检测网络拓扑。

Result: 获得了最优停止时间的上界，数值结果展示了其预期的定性特征；成功将该方法推广应用于任意非循环有向图结构的分布式检测网络。

Conclusion: 所提出的停止时间定义及推导过程为序列检测提供了获取最优决策规则的直接程序，且该理论具有广泛的适用性，可覆盖各类非循环分布式网络架构。

Abstract: We present a simple definition of stopping time and its role in the formulation of sequential tests for both centralized and distributed detection, providing a straightforward procedure for obtaining optimal decision rules. Upper bounds for optimal stopping time are derived and numerically shown to possess certain qualitative features expected of the optimal stopping time. The results are extended to any distributed detection network in the form of an acyclic directed graph.

</details>


### [182] [Degree Sequence Reconstruction from Subgraph Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09397)
*Venkata Gandikota,Arick Grootveld,Haodong Yang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种从顶点删除轨迹中重建未知图度序列的算法：一种基于拒绝采样和混合分布估计，需要 $\tilde O(n^{1/3})$ 个轨迹但解码器时间复杂度未知；另一种基于恢复度矩（degree moments），需要 $\tilde O(n^{1/2})$ 个轨迹并提供了亚指数时间解码器。此外，文章还给出了恢复边数的轨迹复杂度上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决从随机删除顶点的轨迹中恢复未知图的有序度向量（degree sequence）的问题，旨在降低重建所需的轨迹数量并提高解码效率，特别是寻找具有亚指数时间复杂度的解码方案。

Method: 提出了两种主要方法：1) 利用拒绝采样将问题转化为混合分布的估计问题；2) 通过恢复图的不变量（即度矩）来识别度序列，利用极值多项式界（extremal polynomial bounds）确定所需度矩的数量，并基于多项式机制构建解码器。

Result: 第一种算法实现了 $\tilde O(n^{1/3})$ 的轨迹复杂度，但缺乏已知的亚指数时间解码器；第二种算法证明了重构度序列需要且仅需 $\tilde \Theta(n^{1/2})$ 个度矩，实现了 $\tilde O(n^{1/2})$ 的轨迹复杂度，并给出了亚指数时间解码器。同时，确定了恢复边数的轨迹复杂度上界为 $O(n^3)$，下界为 $\Omega(n^2)$。

Conclusion: 通过度矩恢复的方法在轨迹复杂度和解码时间效率之间取得了更好的平衡，证明了 $\tilde \Theta(n^{1/2})$ 个度矩足以重构度序列，并确立了边数恢复问题的复杂度界限，为图重建理论提供了新的算法工具和理论边界。

Abstract: The goal of degree sequence reconstruction is to recover the ordered vector of degrees of an unknown graph from vertex deleted traces, where each vertex is deleted independently with probability $p$. We provide two algorithms for reconstruction; the first uses rejection sampling to reduce the problem to an estimation problem for a mixture distribution. Combined with prior trace reconstruction results, this gives a reconstruction algorithm using $\Exp{\tilde O (n^{1/3})}$ traces, although no sub-exponential time decoder is known. Our other approach involves recovering certain graph invariants, degree moments, that can identify a graphs degree sequence. Extremal polynomial bounds show that $\tilde Θ(n^{1/2})$ degree moments are necessary and sufficient to reconstruct the degree sequence, which leads to an algorithm with $\Exp{\tilde O(n^{1/2})}$ trace complexity. The same polynomial machinery yields a sub-exponential time decoder for the degree sequence from the moments. Additionally, we give an $O(n^{3})$ upper bound and a $Ω(n^2)$ lower bound for the trace complexity of recovering the number of edges.

</details>


### [183] [Characterizing Multi-Cell Pinching-Antenna Transmission: Revealing the Other Side of the Coin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09585)
*Zhiguo Ding*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出捏合天线（pinching antennas）不仅能增强用户与基站的连接，更能通过降低基站发射功率来抑制网络干扰，从而在链路质量不变的情况下提升用户数据速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注捏合天线增强用户与自身基站连接的直观优势，而本文旨在探索其较少被关注的非直观优势：从抑制干扰的角度出发，利用该技术降低全网基站的发射功率。

Method: 采用随机几何（stochastic geometric）研究方法，假设基站采用分数功率控制（fractional power control）策略，对捏合天线在干扰抑制方面的性能进行建模与分析。

Result: 分析结果表明，捏合天线使得基站能够以低功率（“耳语”而非“喊叫”）服务用户，显著降低了网络干扰；即使用户到自身基站的链路质量未变，其数据速率仍能因干扰减少而得到提升。

Conclusion: 捏合天线具备显著的干扰抑制能力，这种能力对于提升网络整体性能和用户数据速率具有重要影响，为该技术的应用提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Using pinching antennas to enhance a user's connection to its own base station (BS) is intuitive and has been well investigated in the literature. This letter focuses on a less intuitive advantage of pinching antennas from the interference-suppression perspective. In particular, using pinching antennas ensures that all BSs serve their users with low transmit power, i.e., BSs can ``whisper", rather than ``shout", to their users. As a result, by experiencing less interference, a user's data rate can still be improved with pinching antennas, even if its link quality to its own BS remains unchanged. A stochastic geometric study is carried out in the letter, where BSs employ fractional power control. Analytical and numerical results are presented to reveal the significant impact of this interference-suppression capability offered by pinching antennas.

</details>


### [184] [Fundamental Limits of Joint Target Detection and Parameter Estimation - Characterizing Mixed-State Sensing Limits via Posterior Entropy Volume](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09667)
*Dazhuan Xu,Nan Wang,Han Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的感知理论框架，将目标存在模式（离散）与物理参数（连续）建模为混合状态，并证明后验熵体积与联合互信息是同一极限的两种互补表达：熵体积具有物理单位且适用于工程尺度，互信息无量纲且满足可加性；核心结论表明，最小后验混合熵体积等于先验体积乘以 $2^{-I(Ξ;Y)}$，揭示了检测与估计在体积域相乘、在比特域相加的规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通感一体化（ISAC）的发展亟需一个统一的感知理论基础，以解决现有理论在离散目标检测与连续参数估计之间缺乏统一度量标准的问题，特别是需要一种既能反映物理尺度（如分辨率单元）又能适应目标数量变化的理论框架。

Method: 1. 在分支参考测度上将目标存在模式和连续物理参数建模为混合离散 - 连续状态 $Ξ$；2. 定义离散、连续及混合状态的熵数、熵体积和混合熵体积；3. 利用最大熵原理阐释指数熵尺度的测度论意义；4. 基于混合渐近等分性质和后验概率 - 体积不等式，通过条件典型集推导主要极限；5. 分析后验保持级联与中间压缩对信息损失的影响。

Result: 1. 证明了对于渐近可靠的高概率感知区域，最小可实现的一阶后验混合熵体积等于先验混合熵体积乘以 $2^{-I(Ξ;Y)}$，其中互信息 $I(Ξ;Y)$ 可分解为检测项 $I(V;Y)$ 与估计项 $I(X_V;Y\mid V)$ 之和；2. 确立了检测与估计贡献在体积域呈乘法关系、在比特域呈加法关系的机制，即每增加 1 比特感知信息，后验有效测度减半；3. 证明后验保持级联可达到直接推断极限，而任意中间压缩会导致精确的信息损失 $I(Ξ;Y\mid Z)$。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了感知理论的统一数学基础，通过熵体积与互信息的互补性，量化了检测与估计的联合性能极限，为通感一体化系统的设计提供了理论依据，表明优化感知系统需同时考虑离散检测与连续估计的信息增益，并避免非必要的中间信息压缩以减少信息损失。

Abstract: The development of integrated sensing and communication calls for a unified theoretical foundation for sensing. This paper models target-presence patterns and continuous physical parameters as a mixed discrete-continuous state $Ξ$ on a branched reference measure, and treats posterior entropy volume and joint mutual information as two complementary representations of the same limit. Entropy volume carries physical units, can be compared with engineering scales such as resolution cells, and remains meaningful when the number of active targets varies; mutual information is dimensionless, invariant to coordinates and units, and additive through the chain rule. We define entropy number, entropy volume, and mixed entropy volume for discrete, continuous, and mixed states, respectively. The maximum-entropy principle for the uniform distribution on a support of fixed measure explains the measure-theoretic meaning of the exponential entropy scale, while the main limit follows from a mixed asymptotic equipartition property and a posterior probability-volume inequality through conditional typical sets. For asymptotically reliable high-probability sensing regions, the minimum achievable first-order posterior mixed entropy volume equals the prior mixed entropy volume multiplied by $2^{-I(Ξ;Y)}$, where $I(Ξ;Y)=I(V;Y)+I(X_V;Y\mid V)$. Thus, detection and estimation contributions multiply in the volume domain and add in the bit domain, and one sensing bit halves the posterior effective measure. We further prove that posterior-preserving cascades attain the direct-inference limit, while arbitrary intermediate compression incurs the exact information loss $I(Ξ;Y\mid Z)$. Numerical results for a single-target presence-range model illustrate the information composition, posterior entropy-volume contraction, and cascade-interface loss.

</details>


### [185] [Conditions for Global Optimality in Quantum Arimoto-Blahut Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09731)
*Geng Liu,Masahito Hayashi*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文建立了广义 Arimoto--Blahut (AB) 算法在凸可微目标下达到全局最优的充要条件，提出了基于可行方向导数的后验最优性证书，并通过数值实验验证了该证书能有效区分全局最优解与次优稳定点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管广义 AB 算法在信息论和量子优化中广泛应用，且具有单调目标下降和数值稳定性，但这些特性并不能保证算法收敛至全局最优解，现有理论缺乏判断固定点全局最优性的结构化标准。

Method: 推导了线性约束下满秩 AB 固定点全局最优的充要条件（即 AB 更新方向与目标梯度之差属于约束法空间）；构建了基于可行方向导数的后验最优性证书及目标间隙的有限差分上界；通过去相位与去极化信道的相对熵最小化及振幅阻尼信道案例进行解析推导与数值验证。

Result: 确立了 AB 算法与镜像下降 (MD) 路径等价性及最优性的兼容性条件；在去相位与去极化信道案例中解析确定了唯一的全局最小化器为满秩 AB 固定点；在振幅阻尼案例中，检测到单调 AB 迭代稳定于次优固定点，且该次优性被有限差分证书成功识别。

Conclusion: 研究结果为评估量子 AB 算法的全局最优性提供了结构化的理论判据和可计算的后验验证工具，弥补了仅依赖单调收敛性无法保证全局最优的缺陷。

Abstract: Generalized Arimoto--Blahut (AB) algorithms are widely used in information theory and quantum optimization, but monotonic objective decrease and numerical stabilization do not guarantee global optimality. We establish necessary and sufficient conditions for the global optimality of full-rank AB fixed points for convex differentiable objectives under linear constraints. An AB fixed point is globally optimal if and only if the AB update direction and the objective gradient differ by an element of the constraint normal space at that point. The same compatibility condition characterizes agreement between individual AB and mirror-descent (MD) updates, whereas pathwise equivalence requires it along the entire common trajectory. Thus, an AB algorithm may follow a trajectory different from MD and still reach the global optimum. We also develop a posteriori optimality certificates based on feasible directional derivatives, including finite-difference upper bounds on the objective gap that require only objective evaluations. For channel relative entropy between dephasing and depolarizing channels, we analytically identify the global minimizer as the unique full-rank AB fixed point, although pathwise equivalence fails. Numerical experiments illustrate convergence of AB and MD along different trajectories and validate the certificates. By contrast, an amplitude-damping example shows that a monotone AB iteration can stabilize at a suboptimal fixed point, whose nonoptimality is detected by the finite-difference certificate. These results provide structural and computable criteria for assessing global optimality in quantum AB algorithms.

</details>


### [186] [Channel Estimation for OFDM via Delay-Doppler Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09782)
*Mingcheng Nie,Hao Chang,Xiaoqi Zhang,Junkai Liu,Wibowo Hardjawana,Branka Vucetic,Yonghui Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用延迟 - 多普勒（DD）域特性的新型OFDM信道估计算法，通过紧凑的时频域输入输出关系进行粗估计，再转换至DD域进行基于阈值的参数细化，最终获得高精度的时频域信道估计，性能显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统OFDM信道估计技术未能充分利用延迟 - 多普勒域信道的独特稀疏特性，导致在相同导频部署下估计精度和系统性能受限。

Method: 首先推导仅关注非零元素的紧凑时频域输入输出关系，利用线性最小均方误差（LMMSE）估计器进行粗估计；随后通过酉变换将估计结果转换至延迟 - 多普勒域，利用该域信道矩阵是多个不同相移响应聚合的特性，设计基于阈值的参数估计进行细化；最后将细化后的DD域参数重构为高精度的时频域信道估计。

Result: 数值结果表明，在相同的导频部署条件下，该方法相比传统OFDM信道估计技术取得了显著的性能增益。

Conclusion: 利用延迟 - 多普勒域的信道稀疏性和结构特性进行两阶段（粗估计加细化）估计，能有效提升OFDM系统的信道估计精度和整体性能。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a novel channel estimation (CE) algorithm for orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) systems that exploits the unique characteristics of the delay-Doppler (DD) domain channel. Specifically, the time-frequency (TF) domain input-output relationship (IOR) is derived in a compact form by focusing solely on the non-zero elements of the TF domain channel matrix. Based on this compact IOR, a coarse TF domain CE is first performed using a linear minimum mean square error estimator. Then, the resultant TF domain estimates are transformed to the DD domain through a unitary transformation for further refinement. We reveal that the effective DD domain channel matrix can be viewed as an aggregation of multiple DD domain channel responses with different phase shifts. This allows us to devise a threshold-based estimation for DD domain channel parameters with high accuracy. The estimated DD domain channel parameters are then applied to form a refined estimate of TF domain channel. Our numerical results demonstrate that the proposed method can achieve substantial performance gains over conventional OFDM channel estimation techniques under the same pilot deployment.

</details>


### [187] [Learning-Aided Short Code Design for ISAC based on MIMO-OFDM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09797)
*Mingcheng Nie,Shuangyang Li,Geng Wang,Peng Cheng,Shenghong Li,Chang Liu,Giuseppe Caire,Yonghui Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习的编码波形设计，用于集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统，通过在短块传输中灵活权衡通信可靠性与测距精度，显著优于传统方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在短块传输场景下，传统基于标准信道编码和调制的方案难以在通信可靠性（误码率）与感知精度（测距）之间实现灵活且优化的权衡问题。

Method: 基于实用的MIMO-OFDM架构，利用已知的信道状态信息和静态目标角度，设计了一种基于Transformer的发射机，将输入信息比特直接编码为ISAC发射波形，以联合优化误码率（BER）和延迟修正克拉美-罗界（MCRB）；同时采用对应的基于Transformer的接收机进行信息恢复。

Result: 分析了面向通信和面向感知的学习码字结构，发现平衡的ISAC波形自然呈现出介于两者极端之间的中间结构；数值结果证明该设计在权衡增益上显著优于传统方案。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于Transformer的深度学习编码波形设计能够有效实现通信与感知性能的灵活折衷，为短块传输下的ISAC系统提供了性能优越的新范式。

Abstract: This paper proposes a deep learning (DL)-based coded waveform design for integrated sensing and communications (ISAC), enabling flexible trade-offs between communication reliability and ranging accuracy in short-block transmissions. The proposed scheme is built upon a practical multiple-input multiple-output orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (MIMO-OFDM) architecture, where the communication channel state information and the angles of the static targets are assumed available at the transmitter. A transformer-based transmitter encodes input information bits directly into ISAC transmit waveforms to jointly optimize the bit error rate (BER) performance and the delay modified Cramer-Rao bound (MCRB). A corresponding transformer-based receiver is adopted at the communication side to recover the transmitted information bits. We further examine the learned codewords for communication-oriented and sensing-oriented designs, revealing that a balanced ISAC waveform naturally exhibits an intermediate structure between these two extremes. Numerical results illustrate these codeword structures and demonstrate that the proposed design provides substantial trade-off gains over conventional schemes based on standard channel coding and modulation.

</details>


### [188] [Proximity Gaps for Gabidulin Codes and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09838)
*Songsong Li,Chaoping Xing,Chen Yuan,Ruiqi Zhu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性秩度量码的邻近间隙（proximity gaps）问题，证明了其存在性并给出了具体的间隙界限，同时构建了首个基于秩度量纠错码的多项式承诺方案（PCS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 邻近间隙是交互式神谕证明（IOPPs）和多项式承诺方案（PCSs）可靠性的核心。尽管汉明度量码的邻近间隙已得到充分研究，但其在秩度量码中的对应问题虽有重要的编码理论和密码学应用前景，却尚未被充分探索。

Method: 1. 理论证明：证明了任意$[n,k,d]$线性秩度量码在特定$\delta$范围内存在邻近间隙，并针对Gabidulin码优化了间隙界限。
2. 紧性分析：通过构造无限族常数率Gabidulin码和仿射直线，证明了$(d-1)/(2n)$界限的紧性，并在$d/(3n)$处给出了误差下界的反例。
3. 应用构建：改编Ligero IOPP以适用于交错Gabidulin码，并调整基于Ligero的PCS以获得$q$-线性化多项式承诺方案。

Result: 1. 证明了线性秩度量码在$\delta\le(d-1)/(3n)$时存在邻近间隙，误差上限为$q^{e+1}/q^m$。
2. 针对Gabidulin码，将间隙改进至$(d-1)/(2n)$，误差为$10q^{n-1}/q^m$，分别匹配了通用线性汉明度量码和Reed-Solomon码的结果。
3. 证实了$(d-1)/(2n)$界限是紧的，并建立了误差的下界。
4. 成功构建了用于交错Gabidulin码的IOPP以及首个基于秩度量纠错码的$q$-线性化多项式承诺方案。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了秩度量码邻近间隙理论的空白，确立了其与汉明度量码相当的理论界限，并首次将秩度量码应用于多项式承诺方案框架，拓展了其在密码学中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Proximity gaps are central to the soundness of interactive oracle proofs of proximity (IOPPs) and polynomial commitment schemes (PCSs). An $[n,k,d]$ linear code $C\subseteq\mathbb F^n$ has a $δ$-proximity gap with error $ε$ if, for every $u_0,u_1\in\mathbb F^n$, either all points on $\ell_{u_0,u_1}=\{u_0+αu_1:α\in\mathbb F\}$ are $δ$-close to $C$, or at most an $ε$ fraction are. Although proximity gaps for Hamming-metric codes are well understood, their rank-metric counterparts remain largely unexplored despite their applications in coding theory and cryptography. In this work, we study proximity gaps for linear rank-metric codes and their cryptographic applications.
  First, we show that every $[n,k,d]$ linear rank-metric code $C$ over $\mathbb F_{q^m}$ admits a proximity gap for every $δ\le(d-1)/(3n)$, with error at most $q^{e+1}/q^m$, where $e=\lfloorδn\rfloor$. For Gabidulin codes, we improve the gap to $(d-1)/(2n)$ with error $10q^{n-1}/q^m$. These two proximity gaps match those for general linear Hamming-metric codes and Reed--Solomon (RS) codes, respectively. We prove the $(d-1)/(2n)$ bound is tight by constructing an infinite family of constant-rate Gabidulin codes and affine lines $\ell_{u_0,u_1}$ on which a $1-o(1)$ fraction of points are $d/(2n)$-close to the code, while $u_1$ is at least $3d/(4n)$-far from it. At the $d/(3n)$ gap, we also give a counterexample establishing a lower bound on $ε$.
  As applications, we construct an IOPP for interleaved Gabidulin codes by adapting the Ligero IOPP for interleaved RS codes. We then adapt the Ligero-based PCS for ordinary polynomials to obtain a $q$-linearized polynomial commitment scheme. To our knowledge, this is the first PCS framework based on rank-metric error-correcting codes.

</details>


### [189] [Fast Collision-Free Acquisition in 1-Persistent Age-Threshold Slotted ALOHA via Role-Protected Counters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09934)
*Plínio Santini Dester*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“角色保护计数器阈值时隙ALOHA”（RP-CTSA）的新协议，旨在解决1-pTSA协议在获取瞬态阶段耗时过长的问题。RP-CTSA通过将预约记忆与信息年龄（AoI）分离，在保持无冲突调度周期的同时，显著缩短了系统收敛时间，从而在有限时间范围内大幅降低了AoI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向目标的感知、估计和控制任务需要及时且规律的任务相关更新。虽然1-persistent age-threshold slotted ALOHA (1-pTSA) 能够自组织成周期性的无冲突调度，但其初始获取瞬态（acquisition transient）过程往往主导了有限时间范围内的系统性能，导致整体信息年龄（AoI）较高。

Method: 提出RP-CTSA协议，核心机制是将节点的“预约记忆”与“信息年龄”解耦。具体策略包括：1) 若已调度的单节点与活跃竞争者发生碰撞，该节点保留其相位（phase）；2) 若多个已调度节点发生碰撞，则立即释放它们；3) 仅在更新包被成功解码后才重置AoI。该协议仅需个体确认和二进制RELEASE/HOLD指示，无需集中式相位分配或碰撞节点识别。理论分析在逆人口访问缩放条件下，将获取动力学建模为精确的纯死亡过程（pure-death process）。

Result: 理论推导表明：当计数器阈值 $\Gamma_n=n$ 时，获取时间为 $O(n^2)$；当 $\Gamma_n\sim(1+\theta)n$ （$\theta>0$）时，获取时间优化为 $O(n\log n)$。仿真结果证实了这两种机制的存在，并显示相比于1-pTSA，RP-CTSA在保留所有无冲突调度特性的同时，在有限时间范围内实现了显著的AoI性能增益。

Conclusion: RP-CTSA协议通过巧妙的碰撞处理机制（区分单节点碰撞与多节点碰撞），有效解决了传统协议获取阶段过长的问题。它在无需复杂集中控制的前提下，实现了从 $O(n^2)$ 到 $O(n\log n)$ 的收敛速度提升，显著优化了面向时延敏感任务的有限时间信息年龄性能。

Abstract: Goal-oriented sensing, estimation, and control benefit from prompt, regular access to task-relevant updates. Although 1-persistent age-threshold slotted ALOHA (1-pTSA) can self-organize into a periodic collision-free schedule, its acquisition transient can dominate finite-horizon performance. We propose role-protected counter-threshold slotted ALOHA (RP--CTSA), which separates reservation memory from the age of information (AoI). A scheduled singleton retains its phase after colliding with active contenders, whereas a collision involving multiple scheduled nodes releases them immediately; AoI resets only after a decoded update. The protocol requires individual acknowledgments and a binary RELEASE/HOLD indication, but no centralized phase assignment or identification of colliders. Let $n$ denote the number of nodes and $Γ_n$ the counter threshold. Under inverse-population access scaling, the acquisition dynamics admit an exact pure-death representation. When $Γ_n=n$, the acquisition time is $O(n^2)$; when $Γ_n\sim(1+θ)n$, with $θ>0$, it is $O(n\log n)$. Simulations confirm both regimes and show substantial finite-horizon AoI gains over 1-pTSA while preserving every collision-free schedule.

</details>


### [190] [Quantum MDS codes from complements of unions of finite-field subsets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09943)
*Naihong Hu,Hong Ji*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文利用 $\mathbb F_{q^2}$ 子集并集的补集作为定位集，建立了 GRS 码 Hermitian 自正交的充分条件，构造了五类量子 MDS 码，且在特定参数下最小距离优于已知构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了获得最小距离更大的 $q$ 元量子 MDS 码，克服现有基于迹映射、线性变换和乘法子群陪集构造方法的局限性。

Method: 采用 $\mathbb F_{q^2}$ 中子集并集的补集作为定位集，结合乘法子群陪集及特定迹/范数值的集合，推导 Hermitian 自正交性条件。

Result: 构建了五类 Hermitian 自正交 GRS 码及对应的量子 MDS 码，部分码的最小距离超过 $q/2+1$。

Conclusion: 对比表明，新构造在无穷多个 $q$ 值下，同长度码的最小距离严格大于已知构造，验证了方法的有效性与改进性。

Abstract: Let $q$ be an odd prime power. We use complements of unions of subsets of $\mathbb F_{q^2}$ as locator sets and establish a sufficient condition under which a generalized Reed--Solomon (GRS) code is Hermitian self-orthogonal. Using cosets of multiplicative subgroups and sets with prescribed trace or norm values, we construct five families of Hermitian self-orthogonal GRS codes over $\mathbb F_{q^2}$. The Hermitian construction then yields five corresponding families of $q$-ary quantum maximum-distance-separable (MDS) codes. Under suitable parameter conditions, these quantum codes have minimum distances greater than $q/2+1$. By comparing codes of the same length, we give conditions under which our codes have strictly larger minimum distances than those obtainable from several previously known constructions based on trace maps, linear transformations, and cosets of multiplicative subgroups, either directly or via the propagation rule. We further show that such improvements occur for infinitely many values of $q$.

</details>


### [191] [The kernel-block rank profile and a complete classification of $\mathbb{Z}_2\mathbb{Z}_4\mathbb{Z}_8$-linear Hadamard codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09969)
*Dipak K. Bhunia*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的等价不变量——“核块秩剖面”（kernel-block rank profile），成功解决了$\mathbb{Z}_2\mathbb{Z}_4\mathbb{Z}_8$-线性Hadamard码族中仅凭长度、秩和核维数无法完全分类的问题，证明了该族中长度相同的码等价当且仅当其类型相同，并给出了长度为$2^t$时互不等价码的确切数量公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的$\mathbb{Z}_2\mathbb{Z}_4\mathbb{Z}_8$-线性Hadamard码分类不变量（长度、秩、核维数）不足以完全区分该码族，存在具有相同这些全局不变量但类型不同的无限对码族，此前仅能通过计算机等价测试在特定长度下区分，缺乏理论上的完备分类依据。

Method: 引入“核块秩剖面”作为新的等价不变量。该方法利用核（kernel）将二进制坐标划分为块，计算在这些块上截断的线性空间的维数多重集。通过计算整个码族的该剖面，分析其取值分布与参数$t_1, t_2, t_3$的依赖关系，从而反推码的类型参数。

Result: 计算表明，该码族存在$2^{t_1+t_2+t_3-1}$个块，核块秩剖面最多取两个值，其差值为$t_1-1$。该剖面为常数当且仅当$t_1=1$。因此，该剖面可唯一确定参数$t_1$，结合长度和核维数可确定$t_2$和$t_3$。最终得出结论：同长度的该类码等价当且仅当类型重合，且长度为$2^t$ ($t\geq3$) 时互不等价码的数量为$\lfloor(t^2+6)/12\rfloor$。

Conclusion: “核块秩剖面”是一个有效的分类不变量，它弥补了全局不变量的不足，实现了$\mathbb{Z}_2\mathbb{Z}_4\mathbb{Z}_8$-线性Hadamard码族的完全分类，确立了类型与等价类之间的一一对应关系，并提供了计算等价类数量的解析公式。

Abstract: The $\mathbb{Z}_2\mathbb{Z}_4\mathbb{Z}_8$-additive codes are subgroups of $\mathbb{Z}_2^{α_1}\times\mathbb{Z}_4^{α_2}\times\mathbb{Z}_8^{α_3}$, and a $\mathbb{Z}_2\mathbb{Z}_4\mathbb{Z}_8$-linear Hadamard code is the Gray map image of such a code. A recursive construction of $\mathbb{Z}_2\mathbb{Z}_4\mathbb{Z}_8$-additive Hadamard codes $\mathcal H^{t_1,t_2,t_3}$, with all $α_i\neq0$, $t_1\geq1$, $t_2\geq0$, and $t_3\geq1$, is known, as are the linearity, kernel dimension, and rank of the corresponding codes $H^{t_1,t_2,t_3}$ of length $2^t$, where $t+1=3t_1+2t_2+t_3$. Yet these invariants do not completely classify the family. Two infinite families of pairs of distinct types share the length, rank and kernel dimension, and for classified lengths $2^t$, $3\leq t\leq11$, such pairs were separated only by computer equivalence tests.
  In this paper, we introduce an equivalence invariant that resolves these cases. The kernel partitions the binary coordinates into blocks, two coordinates lying in the same block when every kernel word takes the same value in both; the \emph{kernel-block rank profile} is the multiset of the dimensions of the linear span punctured on these blocks. Unlike rank and kernel dimension, which are global, this invariant records how much of the span survives on each block. We compute it for the whole family: there are $2^{t_1+t_2+t_3-1}$ blocks, all of size $2^{2t_1+t_2}$, and the profile takes at most two values $t_2+\binom{t_1+2}{2}$ and $t_2+2+\binom{t_1+1}{2}$, whose difference is $t_1-1$; it is constant precisely when $t_1=1$. Hence, the profile recovers $t_1$, and the length and kernel dimension recover $t_2$ and $t_3$. Two codes of the family with the same length are therefore equivalent if and only if their types coincide, and the number of pairwise nonequivalent such codes of length $2^t$ is $\lfloor(t^2+6)/12\rfloor$ for every $t\geq3$.

</details>


### [192] [On the Evolution of the Capacity-Achieving Input Support for the Amplitude-Constrained AWGN Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10039)
*Luca Barletta,Alex Dytso*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了峰值幅度受限的加性高斯白噪声信道中，容量 Achieving 输入分布（CAID）支撑集随幅度约束变化的演化规律，证明了支撑集基数的局部变化仅发生在原点，且机制仅限于原点的出现/消失或分裂/合并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已知受限高斯信道的 CAID 是唯一、对称、离散且有限支撑的，但其支撑集结构随幅度约束变化的具体转换机制（transition mechanisms）长期以来尚未解决。

Method: 通过分析 KKT 函数的性质，证明原点是唯一的退化支撑点和非活跃接触点；建立最优分布与 KKT 函数的连续性；论证非零支撑点的局部稳定性（即随约束微小变化，其位置和概率质量连续变化且保持唯一）。

Result: 证明了在幅度约束发生局部变化时，支撑集基数要么保持不变，要么增加 1，且这种增加仅能发生在原点；所有局部支撑集基数的变化都局限于原点。

Conclusion: 局部范围内，支撑集结构的转换机制仅包括：原点的出现或消失，以及原点分裂为对称点对或对称点对合并为原点。

Abstract: We consider an additive white Gaussian noise channel subject to a peak-amplitude constraint and study how the support of the capacity-achieving input distribution (CAID) evolves as the amplitude constraint $A$ varies. Although the CAID is known to be unique, symmetric, discrete, and finitely supported, the structure of its support transitions has remained largely unresolved. We show that the origin is the only possible degenerate support point and the only possible inactive contact point of the KKT function. We then establish continuity properties of the optimal distribution and the KKT function and prove that every nonzero support point is locally stable: under small changes in $A$, it persists as a unique nearby atom whose location and probability mass vary continuously. Combining these results, we prove that, locally, the support cardinality at a nearby amplitude is either unchanged or larger by one and that any such increase can occur only at the origin. Consequently, all local changes in support cardinality are confined to the origin: locally, the only possible transition mechanisms are the appearance or disappearance of the point at the origin and the splitting or merging of the origin into a symmetric pair.

</details>


### [193] [Frame-Coded Legged Locomotion over Noisy Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10273)
*Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的多足机器人崎岖地形运动理论，将运动建模为带有擦除的量化有限帧展开，而非传统的噪声信道物质传输。该理论证明了机械平衡对应于后验均值，并推导出了信息 - 运动不等式及精确的增量冗余规则，为腿式运动建立了基本极限理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的开环多足运动模型将其视为噪声信道上的物质传输，存在局限性：无法提供正任务率，且缺乏随幸存接触集变化的解码器。作者旨在克服这些限制，通过引入量化有限帧展开理论来更准确地描述和解决崎岖地形下的运动控制问题。

Method: 将 d 维本体指令映射为 N>d 个异构局部接触指令，利用接触门控顺应形态物理实现加权活性子帧解码器。在线性高斯模型下，分析机械平衡、切向刚度及机械顺应性与后验统计量（均值、精度、协方差）的对应关系。通过解析等范数 Parseval 帧、谐波帧及随机高斯步态帧的性能，推导信息 - 运动不等式和增量冗余规则。

Result: 1. 机械平衡精确对应后验均值，切向刚度对应后验精度，机械顺应性对应后验协方差。2. 等范数 Parseval 帧对单个接触缺失具有极小极大最优性，两接触鲁棒性由帧相干性决定。3. 对于独立幸存概率 q，当模拟维速率 R<q 时可精确重构，R>q 则无法恢复任意指令。4. 残余接触噪声导致渐近每模放大率为 1/(q-R)，并在阈值处机械刚度裕度消失。5. 建立了信息 - 运动不等式及指导下一步态分量生成的精确增量冗余规则。

Conclusion: 研究建立了模拟帧编码定理，确定了有限的相对冗余及其逆定理，构成了腿式运动基本极限理论的核心部分。该理论为设计在崎岖地形上具有鲁棒性的多足机器人步态和形态提供了严格的数学基础和物理指导。

Abstract: Open-loop multilegged locomotion over rough terrain has been interpreted as matter transport over a noisy channel: leg-ground interactions are discrete basic active contacts, terrain deletes or perturbs those contacts, and spatial redundancy concentrates the resulting thrust and arrival time. That construction is repetition-like because every module carries the same scalar locomotion task. It consequently provides neither a positive task rate nor a decoder that changes with the surviving contact set. Here we formulate locomotion instead as a quantized finite-frame expansion with erasures. A d-dimensional body-level command is mapped into N>d heterogeneous local contact commands. Rough terrain erases or corrupts frame coefficients, while a contact-gated compliant morphology physically realizes the weighted active-subframe decoder. For a linear-Gaussian model, mechanical equilibrium is exactly the posterior mean, tangent stiffness is posterior precision, and mechanical compliance is posterior covariance. Equal-norm Parseval frames are shown to be minimax optimal against one missing contact, two-contact robustness is governed by frame coherence, and a harmonic frame gives a directly realizable gait family. For independently surviving contacts of probability q, random Gaussian gait frames admit exact reconstruction at every analog dimension rate R<q, with a binomial reliability exponent, whereas recovery of arbitrary commands is impossible for R>q. Residual contact noise yields an asymptotic per-mode amplification 1/(q-R) and a vanishing mechanical stiffness margin at the threshold. An information-locomotion inequality and an exact incremental-redundancy rule direct the next gait component toward the softest task-relevant unresolved mode. The resulting analog frame-coding theorem establishes a finite relative redundancy and converse as part of a fundamental limit theory of legged locomotion.

</details>


### [194] [Revisiting the Weight Spectrum of the Affine Grassmann Code $C^{\mathbb{A}}(2,m)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10274)
*Rohit Yadav*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种独立且更简化的推导方法，用于确定仿射格拉斯曼码 $C^{\mathbb{A}}(2,m)$ 的权重谱，该方法通过紧凑的矩阵形式和包含两个仿射子空间及其交集的单一闭式公式来计算汉明权重，适用于所有 $m\ge4$ 和素数幂 $q$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的研究（Piñero 和 Singh）通过关联交替矩阵的秩进行案例分析来确定权重谱，本文旨在提供一种更独立、更 streamlined（流线型/简化）的推导路径，以克服原有方法的复杂性或局限性。

Method: 将每个码字写成紧凑的矩阵形式，并通过一个涉及两个显式仿射子空间及其交集的单一闭式公式来表达其汉明权重。

Result: 成功推导出了适用于所有 $m\ge4$ 和任意素数幂 $q$ 的仿射格拉斯曼码 $C^{\mathbb{A}}(2,m)$ 的权重谱。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法不仅验证了现有结果，还通过统一的闭式公式和矩阵表示法，为理解仿射格拉斯曼码的权重分布提供了更通用且高效的数学框架。

Abstract: Affine Grassmann codes, introduced by Beelen, Ghorpade and Høholdt, are linear codes over $\mathbb{F}_q$ obtained by evaluating linear combinations of minors of a generic matrix. The weight spectrum of the affine Grassmann code $C^{\mathbb{A}}(2,m)$ was determined by Piñero and Singh via a case analysis on the rank of an associated alternating matrix. We give an independent and more streamlined derivation, valid for all $m\ge4$ and every prime power $q$, in which each codeword is written in a compact matrix form and its Hamming weight is expressed through a single closed formula involving two explicit affine subspaces and their intersection.

</details>


### [195] [Construction of Multi-sequences With High Nonlinear Complexity via Narrow Ray Class Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10369)
*Xiaofeng Liu,Jun Zhang,Fang-Wei Fu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于窄射线类域和Guruswami-Xing循环下降的统一框架，用于在任意亏格的函数域上构造具有高非线性复杂度的多序列，以增强密码学安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性复杂度是评估伪随机序列的基本准则，构造具有高非线性复杂度的多序列在密码学理论与实践中至关重要。本文旨在延续并统一此前在2014年和2017年IEEE Transactions on Information Theory上发表的相关构造工作。

Method: 利用窄射线类域（narrow ray class fields）理论，结合Guruswami和Xing提出的循环下降（cyclic descent）技术，构建了一个统一的数学框架。该框架适用于任意亏格（arbitrary genera）的函数域。

Result: 成功生成了新的多序列，这些序列在任意亏格的函数域上均表现出高非线性复杂度。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个通用的构造框架，扩展了高非线性复杂度多序列的生成范围，为密码学中的序列设计提供了更强的理论支持和实践工具。

Abstract: Nonlinear complexity is a fundamental criterion in the evaluation of pseudorandom sequences. The construction of multi-sequences with high nonlinear complexity is both theoretically and practically important in cryptography. Motivated by prior constructions of multi-sequences with high nonlinear complexity in [IEEE Trans. Inf. Theory, 60(10), 2014] and [IEEE Trans. Inf. Theory, 63(12), 2017], we provide a unified framework via narrow ray class fields, the cyclic descent due to Guruswami and Xing in [J. Combin. Theory Ser. A 129 (2015) ]. Then we can generate new multi-sequences with high nonlinear complexity over function fields with arbitrary genera.

</details>


### [196] [Mobility Information Capacity in the Sky: A Gaussian Channel Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10436)
*Weijie Yuan,Fan Liu,Shuangyang Li,Lin Zhou,Pingzhi Fan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“机动信息容量”的新度量，用于量化低空无线网络中轨迹观测所能揭示的关于意图机动输入的最大信息量，并推导出了类似香农公式的容量定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的空域容量指标主要量化占用率或流量，忽略了相同数量的航空器在不同运动替代方案下的差异，缺乏从信息论角度对机动能力的度量。

Method: 建立提升的线性高斯机动信道模型，推导其有限时域对数行列式容量；通过成本和不确定性白化获得时空机动本征模，并采用注水算法进行最优机动资源分配。

Result: 当非简并模态数量随时间线性增长且效率渐近对称时，推导出香农形式的定律 $R_M^{\rm G}=\frac{B_M}{2}\log_2(1+\mathrm{MNR})$，其中 MNR 为机动噪声比。

Conclusion: 提出的“机动信息容量”为天空提供了一种以运动为中心的容量视角，作为可区分性基线，区别于传统的受碰撞或几何约束的空域容量指标。

Abstract: Existing airspace capacity metrics mainly quantify occupancy or flow, although the same number of aerial vehicles may result in different motion alternatives. This letter establishes \emph{mobility information capacity} as an information-theoretic measure for low-altitude wireless networks. It quantifies the maximum information that trajectory observations reveal about intentional maneuver inputs under a given maneuver-resource budget and environmental uncertainty. For a common fixed feedback architecture, we formulate a lifted linear-Gaussian mobility channel and derive its finite-horizon log-determinant capacity. Cost and uncertainty whitening gives the spatiotemporal mobility eigenmodes, whose optimal maneuver-resource allocation follows water-filling. When the number of nondegenerate modes grows linearly with time and their efficiencies become asymptotically symmetric, we arrive at the Shannon-like law $R_M^{\rm G}=\frac{B_M}{2}\log_2(1+\mathrm{MNR})$, where MNR is the mobility-to-noise ratio. The proposed measure opens a motion-centric capacity perspective for the sky, while remaining a distinguishability baseline rather than a collision- or geometry-constrained airspace capacity.

</details>


### [197] [Risk-Averse Decision Making via Quantum Measurement Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10482)
*Meiyi Zhu,Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于优化确定性等价（OCE）的风险厌恶型量子测量设计方法，在适度牺牲平均效用的前提下，显著改善了效用分布的下尾部风险（如CVaR）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子测量优化仅关注平均效用最大化，忽略了在大型决策系统中作为行动输入时可能产生的糟糕结果风险，缺乏对风险厌恶目标的考量。

Method: 引入优化确定性等价（OCE）作为风险厌恶目标函数，涵盖平均效用和条件风险价值（CVaR）；针对分段线性增益函数，将问题转化为有限个半定规划（SDP）并推导其对偶形式；针对双态判别问题推导了类Helstrom测量的闭式解。

Result: 证明了该问题可归约为半定规划求解，获得了双态判别下的闭式解；数值结果显示优化后的测量显著改善了效用分布的下尾部表现。

Conclusion: 通过最大化OCE目标设计的量子测量能有效平衡平均效用与风险控制，为量子决策系统提供了更稳健的测量策略。

Abstract: Quantum measurements are conventionally optimized to maximize the average of a utility that depends on the true state and on the measurement outcome. However, when the outcome of the measurement is used as an action within a larger decision-making system, the average utility does not capture the risk of poor outcomes. This letter addresses the design of quantum measurements that maximize a risk-averse objective given by the optimized certainty equivalent (OCE), a family of criteria that includes the average utility and the conditional value at risk (CVaR) as special cases. For a piecewise linear gain function, defining the OCE, thus including the CVaR, the problem is shown to reduce to a finite number of semidefinite programs, for which a dual formulation is derived. For the discrimination of two states, a closed-form solution is obtained that takes the form of a Helstrom measurement. Numerical results show that the optimized measurement improves the lower tail of the utility distribution at a moderate cost in average utility.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [OpenDiscoveryTrace: Process Traces for Evaluating AI Scientist Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09203)
*Aayam Bansal,Keertan Balaji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了OpenDiscoveryTrace数据集，包含558个完整的AI科学代理推理轨迹，旨在解决现有基准仅评估最终输出而忽略推理过程的问题。研究发现，虽然前沿模型的最终成功率相近，但其错误类型和频率存在显著差异，证明了过程级评估的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自主AI科学家基准仅评估生成的代码、假设或论文等最终输出，丢弃了获取这些输出的推理过程。这导致无法审计科学方法、诊断失败模式，也无法区分系统性推理与幸运猜测。

Method: 构建了一个包含558个完整AI科学代理轨迹的公共数据集（OpenDiscoveryTrace），覆盖药物发现、材料科学、基因组学和文献分析等4个领域的124个科学任务。每个轨迹记录每步9个字段的结构化痕迹（包括思考、工具调用、观察、错误、修订触发点和自我报告置信度）。评估了7种模型（3个前沿模型和4个开源权重模型）及60个实时检索变体，并定义了五个基准任务及相应的基线模型（逻辑回归、随机森林、LSTM、Transformer）。

Result: 对363个由LLM评判的轨迹的初步分析显示，过程痕迹揭示了输出评估中不可见的行为差异：三个前沿模型的成功率相当（84%-89%），但Claude Opus 4.6的错误率是GPT-5.4的30倍（每轨迹2.5 vs 0.08），且错误 profile 不同（Claude主要为工具误用，GPT主要为推理错误）。

Conclusion: OpenDiscoveryTrace数据集、痕迹模式、代理框架及基准定义已公开，支持过程级评估、科学代理审计和AI治理研究，证明了捕捉推理过程对于全面评估AI科学能力至关重要。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for autonomous AI scientists evaluate only final outputs---generated code, hypotheses, or papers---yet discard the reasoning process by which those outputs were obtained. This makes it impossible to audit scientific methodology, diagnose failure modes, or distinguish systematic reasoning from fortunate guessing. We present \textbf{OpenDiscoveryTrace}, a public dataset of 558 complete AI scientific agent trajectories that captures how models reason, not just what they produce. Each trajectory records a structured 9-field-per-step trace---including thoughts, tool calls, observations, errors, revision triggers, and self-reported confidence---as models execute 124 scientific tasks spanning drug discovery, materials science, genomics, and scientific literature analysis. The dataset covers seven models: three frontier models (GPT-5.4, Claude Opus 4.6, and Gemini 3.1 Pro; 124 trajectories each, fully balanced across domains and difficulty levels) and four open-weight models (Qwen2.5-7B, Mistral-7B-v0.3, Phi-3.5-mini, and Qwen2.5-1.5B; 30 each), plus 60 live-retrieval variant trajectories. Pilot analysis on 363 LLM-judged trajectories reveals that process traces expose behavioral differences invisible to output-only evaluation: all three frontier models achieve comparable success rates (84--89%), yet Claude Opus 4.6 produces 30$\times$ more errors than GPT-5.4 (2.5 vs. 0.08 per trajectory, $p < 0.0001$, Cliff's $δ= 0.613$), with qualitatively different error profiles---66.7% tool misuse for Claude versus 83.6% reasoning errors for GPT-5.4. We define five benchmark tasks with baselines from logistic regression, random forests, LSTMs, and Transformer models. The dataset, trace schema, agent harness, and benchmark definitions are publicly available under CC BY 4.0 to support research on process-level evaluation, scientific agent auditing, and AI governance.

</details>


### [199] [Adaptive Entangled Game Modules in Artificial General Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09226)
*Haochen Li,Xinshuai Guo,Jingdong Ouyang,Wei Zhang,Leilei Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出概率波框架建模自适应主体集体行为，通过中国股市数据验证了刘-陈-敖（LCA）非局部纠缠神经纤维假说，发现89%的决策模式由适应性纠缠博弈模式解释，并主张将该机制融入AGI架构以构建更高效的人脑处理单元（HPU）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决新古典金融学基于独立理性主体假设的局限性，实证检验大脑非局部纠缠神经纤维（LCA假说）的存在性，并克服当前基于人工神经网络（ANN）的AI模型参数不透明及效率低下的问题，旨在推动具身智能和机器人技术发展。

Method: 构建广义行为智能（GBI）非局部概率波方程，推导可测试的本征模；利用中国日内股票交易数据进行实证分析，量化适应性纠缠博弈模式、新闻适应模式及独立模式在决策行为中的占比。

Result: 实证显示适应性纠缠博弈模式解释了82-94%（平均89%）的观测决策模式，仅不到5%为纯独立模式；2-12%的行为表现出对日内新闻和环境的双稳态适应及参考点突变；结果有力支持了LCA假说，表明交易行为反映了底层大脑机制。

Conclusion: 研究证实了行为心理学中内部智能决策的脑机制基础，强调必须在AGI架构中整合适应性纠缠博弈模块；通过结合ANN与概率波纠缠脑模拟，可开发紧凑、高效且鲁棒的人脑处理单元（HPU），从而增强AGI基础模型。

Abstract: We introduce a probability-wave framework for modeling the collective behavior of interacting adaptive agents, deriving testable eigenmodes through a generalized behavioral intelligence (GBI) nonlocal probability-wave equation. This framework captures a broad range of human intelligence behaviors with analytical mechanisms and offers an indirect method to examine the Liu-Chen-Ao (LCA) hypothesis of nonlocal entangled nerve fibers in the brain through collective trader behaviors. Our empirical analysis of Chinese intraday stock market data demonstrates that adaptive entangled game modes explain 82-94% (89% overall) of observed decision patterns, a sharp contrast to the predictions of neoclassical finance based on independent rational agents. Moreover, 2-12% of behaviors show adaption to intraday news, events, and environments, characterized by dual equilibrium states and abrupt reference point shifts, while purely independent modes occur in less than 5% of cases. These findings empirically support the LCA hypothesis, as observable trading behaviors reflect underlying brain mechanisms and internal intelligence decision-making in behavioral psychology. Our results highlight the necessity of incorporating adaptive entangled game modules into artificial general intelligence (AGI) architectures, addressing the limitations of conventional artificial neural network (ANN)-based AI, which relies on trillions of opaque parameters. By integrating ANN-based AI with probability-wave-based entangled-brain simulations, machine learning can enrich AGI foundation models (FMs) and facilitate the development of human-like processing units (HPUs) that leverage brain-inspired mechanisms. Such HPUs may ultimately create more compact, efficient, and robust AGI systems, particularly for embodied intelligence and robotics.

</details>


### [200] [Subagents vs Agent Skills: Executing Reusable Knowledge for Long-Horizon Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09233)
*Wasu Top Piriyakulkij,Rachel Lawrence,Alicia Curth,Sushrut Karmalkar,Niranjani Prasad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将可复用技能包作为“子代理”（subagents）而非仅加载到主上下文中的指令来执行，以解决长程任务中上下文窗口信息过载导致的推理质量下降问题，实验表明在技能具备清晰输入输出契约时，子代理模式优于传统技能执行模式，尽管存在额外的通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着任务 horizon 增长，将技能指令直接加载到代理上下文中的传统方法变得脆弱，因为上下文窗口中累积的信息过多会导致推理质量下降。

Method: 提出一种替代方案：将技能包作为子代理调用，即为每个子任务生成独立的上下文窗口，而非将其指令注入主代理上下文；通过对比子代理执行与传统技能指令执行在长程任务中的表现进行评估。

Result: 当技能包具有清晰的输入-输出契约且其指令编码了履行该契约所需的过程性知识时，子代理执行方式显著优于传统技能执行方式；但代价是需要额外 token 用于主代理与子代理间的协调，产生通信开销。

Conclusion: 可复用知识的效用不仅取决于其内容，还高度依赖于其组织形式与调用机制；子代理架构为长程任务中有效利用技能库提供了一种更鲁棒的范式。

Abstract: How can language model agents effectively leverage libraries of reusable knowledge to solve long-horizon tasks? Recent work has increasingly focused on agent skills: reusable capabilities represented as skill packages, i.e., multi-file bundles containing instructions, scripts, and other resources that help agents perform specific tasks. Agent skills are typically executed by loading their skill instructions into an agent's context and relying on the agent to follow them. As task horizons grow, however, this approach becomes increasingly brittle, because reasoning quality degrades as more information accumulates in the context window. We investigate an alternative approach in which skill packages are instead invoked as subagents. Rather than loading skill instructions into the main context, subagent execution spawns fresh context windows dedicated to solving individual subtasks. We show that subagent execution outperforms agent-skill execution when skill packages expose clear input-output contracts and their instructions encode the procedural knowledge needed to fulfill those contracts. The tradeoff is additional communication overhead, as extra tokens are required to coordinate between the main agent and its subagents. Our results show that the benefit of reusable knowledge depends not only on its content, but also on how it is organized and invoked.

</details>


### [201] [Gradland: On Phenomenal Experience, Differentiated Across Many Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09306)
*David Balduzzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出物理相互作用的一阶结构（梯度或雅可比矩阵）决定了现象体验的结构，并在理想化的神经网络世界“Gradland”中，通过引入基于基尔霍夫复杂性的“有效秩”和“凝聚力”两个指标，验证了该假设能解释体验持续时间、清晰度、纹理感知、新生儿感知混乱、概念区分、学习过程及丰富体验的功能等七个关键现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决意识科学中的核心难题，即物理过程如何产生主观的现象体验（感质），特别是试图从数学结构层面建立物理相互作用与体验特征之间的直接联系。

Method: 构建一个名为“Gradland”的理想化计算环境，其中物理规律已知且函数大多可微；提出并应用两个基于基尔霍夫复杂性的雅可比结构度量指标——有效秩（effective rank）和凝聚力（cohesion），通过一系列具体案例进行推演和验证。

Result: 研究结果表明，所提出的假设能够合理解释：(1) 体验持续数百毫秒的时长特性；(2) 生动与模糊体验的差异；(3) 纹理体验的产生；(4) 新生儿“繁花似锦的嗡嗡混乱”感知状态；(5) 清晰概念与混淆概念的区分；(6) 学习的主观感受；(7) 丰富密集体验的功能性意义。

Conclusion: 物理相互作用的一阶微分结构（雅可比矩阵）不仅是系统动态的描述，更是现象体验结构的本质特征，该框架为理解意识的多个维度提供了统一的数学解释路径。

Abstract: This paper investigates the hypothesis that the first-order structure of physical interactions, i.e. gradients or Jacobians, characterizes the structure of phenomenal experience. It does so in an idealized world inhabited by neural networks, Gradland, where the physics are known and the functions are (mostly) differentiable. The paper introduces two measures of Jacobian structure: effective rank and cohesion, based on Kirchhoff complexity. Applying the measures to a series of worked examples shows the hypothesis accounts for: (1) the duration of experience, that it can prolong over hundreds of milliseconds; (2) the difference between what is experienced vividly and obscurely; (3) the experience of texture; (4) the blooming buzzing confusion presumably experienced by newborns; (5) the difference between ideas that are held distinctly in mind and ideas that are confused; (6) what learning is like; and finally (7) the paper explains the function of rich, dense experience.

</details>


### [202] [An Autonomous GeoAI Agent for Arctic Eco-Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09374)
*Samira Alkaee Taleghan,Younghyun Koo,Farnoush Banaei-Kashani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种人机回环多代理 GeoAI 系统，用于整合生态与社区标准的北极生态导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 北极航运增长带来环境与社区风险，现有路由方法忽视生态敏感性及社区影响，仅优化效率与安全。

Method: 构建多代理系统协调数据获取、多目标路由生成及天际线决策支持，显式纳入关键栖息地等生态标准。

Result: 实现了统一路由框架，在保持人类控制价值判断的前提下，量化生态暴露与社区负担。

Conclusion: 该框架支持更安全、透明和社会负责任的北极导航，代码与项目页面已公开。

Abstract: Arctic maritime navigation is becoming increasingly important as changing sea-ice conditions expand seasonal accessibility while simultaneously introducing substantial operational, environmental, and community risks. Arctic route planning is inherently a multi-criteria problem: routes that improve vessel safety or efficiency may increase exposure to sea ice, sensitive ecosystems, or nearby communities. Existing routing methods prioritize travel time, fuel use, and navigational risk, often overlooking ecological and community impacts. We introduce a human-in-the-loop, multi-agent GeoAI system for Arctic eco-navigation that integrates operational, physical, ecological, and community-related criteria within a unified routing framework. Multiple specialized agents coordinate geospatial data acquisition and preparation, multi-objective route generation, and skyline-based decision support. The ecological criteria explicitly account for exposure to sensitive areas, including Essential Fish Habitat and seal critical habitat. By considering these ecosystem impacts and potential community burdens while keeping consequential value judgments under human control, the framework supports safer, more transparent, and socially responsible Arctic navigation. Project page and code are publicly available. https://samiraat.github.io/Arctic-Eco-Navigation-Agent/, https://github.com/samiraat/Arctic-Eco-Navigation-Agent

</details>


### [203] [The Menu Is an Execution Prior: State-Path Tool Menus for Online Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09395)
*Bo Yan,Weikai Lin,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“状态路径工具菜单”（State-Path Tool Menu），通过构建从请求状态到目标结果的预执行路径，优化智能体在大规模工具库中的工具选择顺序，显著提升了多步任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅按请求相关性对工具排序，往往能展示最终动作却遗漏或延迟了生成其输入的前置工具，导致智能体在面对包含数千个接口的大型工具库时，难以有效完成需要特定工具顺序的多步任务。

Method: 提出将菜单视为执行路径的先验分布。框架包含编码器（表征工具的可执行性、输出输入匹配度及训练路径中的顺序模式）、检索器（覆盖可执行入口、缺失输入的生产者及最终动作）和重排序器（确保生产者位于消费者之前），从而学习并生成有序的状态路径工具菜单。

Result: 在 ToolBench 基准上，该方法将在线成功率从 0.737 提升至 0.898，优于检索、重排序、生成和路由等基线方法；且在使用 32 个工具时比官方 128 个工具列表覆盖更完整的工具链，性能提升在不同模型容量的执行器家族中均保持一致。

Conclusion: 状态路径工具菜单通过显式建模工具间的依赖关系和执行顺序，有效解决了大规模工具库中多步任务规划难的问题，在不改变智能体架构的前提下显著增强了其执行能力。

Abstract: Language models act through tools, yet practical agents face libraries containing thousands of interfaces. We introduce the tool menu as the short, ordered subset of available tools shown to an agent before execution. The agent can call only tools in this menu. Multi-step tasks require the final action and the prerequisite tools that create its inputs in a usable order. Current constructors rank tools by request relevance, which can surface the final action while omitting or delaying less obvious producers. We introduce the state path, a pre-execution route from the observable request state to the desired outcome, and propose State-Path Tool Menu to learn it. Our framework treats the menu as an execution prior over these routes. Its encoder represents which tools can run from the current state, how their outputs satisfy later inputs, and which orders recur in training paths. A retriever covers an executable entry, the missing-input producers, and the final action. A reranker then places producers before consumers. On ToolBench, our menu raises online success from 0.737 to 0.898 and outperforms retrieval, reranking, generation, and routing baselines without changing the agent. The State-Path menu also covers more complete chains with 32 tools than the official list covers with 128, and its success gain persists across executor families with different model capacities. Our code is at https://github.com/Met2348/State-Path.

</details>


### [204] [Decision-Focused Active Learning for Scale-Aware Critical-Materials Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09413)
*Niranjan Srinivas,Debajyoti Ray,Elias Nakouzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于贝叶斯风险最小化的主动学习策略，用于优化关键元素回收的放大过程。通过分析CICERO工作流中的历史数据，该方法在钕铁硼（NdFeB）和钐钴（SmCo）磁体回收实验中，比非自适应空间填充方法以更少的实验次数（16-24次对比48次）达到了最佳富集效果，并提出了混合筛选策略以降低下游损失，最后规划了前瞻性验证测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决实验室结果与产品需求、过程成本及规模效应之间的脱节问题，旨在为关键元素回收过程的放大选择提供高效的决策框架，减少实验成本并优化回收效率。

Method: 利用太平洋西北国家实验室CICERO工作流的主动学习算法，基于先验结果选择实验；提出通过预期下游贝叶斯风险（Bayes risk）的最小化来选择批次；采用条件回顾性基准测试拟合模型，并对比非自适应空间填充方法；设计了混合筛选候选者的策略及两阶段重构方案。

Result: 在NdFeB磁体回收中，自适应策略在16至24个实验井中即达到记录的最大富集度，而非自适应方法需48个；SmCo磁体分析揭示了纯度与名义产率之间的权衡；混合筛选策略在模拟中显示出比联合搜索更低的估计损失；不同路线的富集效果及产出水排名受相态和稀释假设影响显著。

Conclusion: 基于预期贝叶斯风险最小化的主动学习策略能显著减少实验次数并优化回收过程决策；提出的混合策略在不确定性范围内表现稳健；未来需通过预注册的前瞻性测试，在统一的损失和记录标准下，结合明确的经济输入和规模验证来确认该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Choosing a recovery process for scale-up requires connecting laboratory results with product requirements, process costs, and scale effects. We analyze records from Pacific Northwest National Laboratory's Computer Intelligence for Critical Element Recovery and Optimization (CICERO) workflow for autonomous selective precipitation. Active learning uses prior results to choose experiments. In a conditional retrospective benchmark with fitted models and recycled neodymium-iron-boron (NdFeB) magnet records, active learning finds the best recorded result with fewer experiments than nonadaptive space filling. Enrichment is the selected rare-earth-to-iron ratio relative to that in the feed. Adaptive policies reach the recorded enrichment maximum by 16 to 24 wells (individual experiments), versus 48. Our two-stage reconstruction ties two adaptive alternatives at 16 wells. Conditional analyses of recycled samarium-cobalt (SmCo) magnets show a Round 2 tradeoff between purity and nominal yield, the recovery fraction calculated from an assumed starting amount - NdFeB Round 1 routes differ in enrichment. Rankings for produced water from oil and gas extraction depend on phase and dilution assumptions requiring confirmation.
  We propose choosing batches by their expected reduction in downstream Bayes risk: the minimum expected loss among available process decisions under current beliefs. In exploratory simulations, a hybrid that filters candidates has lower estimated loss than the implemented joint search across routes and conditions. Differences involving the synthetic two-stage policy are small relative to estimation uncertainty. We outline a pre-registered prospective test under a shared loss and logging standard, requiring clarified measurements and records, a defined process decision and relevant outputs, credible economic inputs, and validation at the intended scale.

</details>


### [205] [Valerant: An Automatic Navigable Game Map Generator via Action-Conditioned World Model Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09418)
*Yiran Qiao,Feng Wang,Jing Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Valerant，一个无需训练的框架，通过将预训练的动作条件世界模型与SLAM空间重建相结合，将2D视觉预测转化为持久的3D游戏地图，解决了游戏领域缺乏外部物理实体的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的游戏导向方法主要局限于2D视觉空间，无法实例化持久的3D几何结构。与自动驾驶和机器人领域不同，游戏没有独立的外部物理环境作为支撑，虚拟世界必须被显式构建，而传统的动作条件视频展开仅能提供视觉观测，缺乏支持移动和交互的空间表示。

Method: 提出Valerant框架，采用无训练（training-free）策略。该方法耦合了预测性视觉展开（predictive visual rollouts）、基于SLAM的空间重建（SLAM-based spatial reconstruction）以及探索驱动的动作选择（exploration-driven action selection），逐步将单张图像转化为持久的3D游戏地图。

Result: 成功将预训练的动作条件世界模型转化为适用于3D游戏地图探索与构建的世界动作模型（WAM），实现了从2D视觉模拟到具有显式几何结构的3D持久空间的跨越。

Conclusion: Valerant扩展了基于WAM的交互能力，使其超越2D视觉模拟，为减少3D游戏地图创建中的人工努力提供了一种新的技术途径。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) couple predictive world modeling with action generation, allowing anticipated future states to guide agent behavior. Although WAMs are rapidly advancing embodied AI, general-purpose counterparts remain largely unexplored in games. Existing game-oriented approaches often combine action-conditioned world models with external policies and reward functions to realize WAM-like decision-making, yet they operate mainly in 2D visual observation space and do not instantiate persistent 3D geometry. Extending this paradigm to 3D games introduces a distinct challenge. In autonomous driving and robotics, the physical environment exists independently of the model, providing a persistent 3D world in which selected actions can be executed. Games have no such external substrate; the virtual world itself must be instantiated. Most playable games require a persistent and navigable space, while 3D games additionally require explicit geometry that supports movement and interaction. Action-conditioned video rollouts provide visual observations but not this spatial representation. We present \textsc{Valerant}, a training-free framework that transforms a pretrained action-conditioned world model into a WAM for exploring and constructing 3D game maps. By coupling predictive visual rollouts with SLAM-based spatial reconstruction and exploration-driven action selection, \textsc{Valerant} progressively transforms a single image into a persistent 3D game map. This framework extends WAM-based interaction beyond 2D visual simulation and offers a new approach to reducing manual effort in 3D game-map creation.

</details>


### [206] [XAI-Arena: Can LLMs Assess the Quality of XAI Explanations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09428)
*Yanfei Hu Fleischhauer,Alona Zharova,Nadja Klein,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出XAI-Arena框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）作为评判者，解决可解释人工智能（XAI）评估中依赖主观人类判断导致的可复现性和可扩展性问题，并验证了LLM评分与人类评分的高度一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的XAI解释质量评估方法过度依赖主观的人类判断，导致研究之间缺乏可复现性、可扩展性及可比性，难以进行系统性的基准测试。

Method: 引入XAI-Arena框架，采用“LLM-as-a-judge”（大语言模型即评判者）范式，从感知简洁性、清晰度、任务适配度、信任校准、可操作性、透明度、忠实度及整体可解释性等多个维度，在不同数据集、机器学习模型及利益相关者角色下对XAI方法进行多维基准测试。

Result: 人类验证实验显示，LLM生成的评分与人类评分之间存在强烈的正相关关系（Spearman's rho=.693, p<.001），表明LLM能够有效捕捉XAI解释质量的系统性差异。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的评估方法能够提供一个可扩展、可复现且多维度的框架，用于XAI解释质量的比较评估，有效克服了传统人类评估的局限性。

Abstract: Evaluating the quality of explanations produced by explainable AI (XAI) methods remains challenging because existing approaches often rely on subjective human judgment, limiting reproducibility, scalability, and comparability between studies. We examine whether LLMs can serve as a reproducible and scalable mechanism to make comparative assessments of the quality of XAI explanations. We introduce XAI-Arena, an LLM-as-a-judge framework for scalable, reproducible, multidimensional, and stakeholder-sensitive evaluation of XAI explanation quality. XAI-Arena then allows us to compare XAI explanations along various dimensions, namely, perceived simplicity, clarity, task adequacy, trust calibration, actionability, transparency, faithfulness, and overall interpretability. We then benchmark XAI explanation methods across various datasets, machine learning models, and stakeholder personas. Human validation shows a strong positive association between LLM-generated and human ratings (Spearman's rho=.693, p<.001). Together, LLM-based evaluations can capture systematic differences in XAI explanation quality and provide a scalable and reproducible framework for comparative assessment of XAI explanations.

</details>


### [207] [Do Agents Know When They Succeed? Calibrating Agent Confidence from Internal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09448)
*Priyanka Mary Mammen,Emil Joswin,Srujananjali Medicherla*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ...


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ...

Method: ...

Result: ...

Conclusion: ...

Abstract: As agentic systems getting adopted rapidly in safety critical applications, it is vital to measure the confidence associated with the agentic actions. In comparison to the traditional machine learning systems, agentic workflows have complex failure modes with planning, tool invocation and dynamic environment interactions. In this paper, we investigate whether model's internal representations provide stronger signals of eventual task success in multi-turn agentic setups. We introduce two complementary methods: Latent Trajectory Dynamics (LTD), which summarizes changes in residual-stream representations across an an interaction trajectory, and the Action Representation Probe (ARP), which predicts success from representations formed at action decisions. Across three interactive benchmarks (Bash, SQL, Python) and three model families (Qwen14B, Qwen7B, DeepSeek6.7B), our methods consistently outperform surface level generation and sequence-based calibration baselines providing a zero-overhead reliability monitor that requires neither prompt alterations nor multi-sample rollouts.

</details>


### [208] [ContractEval: Query-Conditioned Execution Matching for Procedural Instruction Conformance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09458)
*Praphul Singh,Shanu Kumar,Akshat Agarwal,Ganesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CONTRACTEVAL框架，通过将过程指令转化为“查询活跃义务”并匹配证据，显式检测LLM代理在执行过程中的结构性失败（如遗漏、分支错误等），解决了仅靠输出或轨迹评估无法发现隐性过程违规的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM代理从单纯回答问题转向执行复杂过程，失败往往变得隐蔽：最终输出看似正确，但系统可能跳过了关键的检查、分支、依赖或不变量。现有的“仅输出评估”和“感知轨迹评估”均无法识别查询具体激活了哪些义务，导致结构性错误被遗漏。

Method: 引入CONTRACTEVAL诊断框架，将过程指令表示为查询活跃义务（query-active obligations），并将其与响应或轨迹证据进行匹配。该框架将遗漏、错误分支、顺序错误、多余动作、不变量违反及输出契约违反定义为 distinct conformance failures（独特的合规性失败），并在受控的审计过程契约套件上进行验证。

Result: 在包含注入结构性失败的受控测试中，传统的仅输出评估和感知轨迹的LLM评判器遗漏了大量错误；而在已知黄金预期图和观测图的情况下，ContractEval能检测并定位所有结构性失败。基于LLM的提取方法虽能保留大部分信号，但对校准较为敏感。

Conclusion: ContractEval并非合规性的绝对保证，但其核心价值在于使过程合规性变得可审计（auditable），而非隐式地依赖最终答案的质量，从而显式化了代理执行过程中的活跃义务。

Abstract: As LLM agents move from answering questions to carrying out procedures, failures can be unwarranted rather than visibly wrong: the final response looks acceptable even though the system skipped the check, branch, dependency, or invariant that made the answer justified. Output-only evaluation sees the answer, and trace-aware judging sees activity, but neither identifies which obligations were active for the query. We introduce CONTRACTEVAL, a diagnostic framework for making those active obligations explicit. It represents procedural instructions as query-active obligations and matches them against response or trace evidence, turning omissions, wrong branches, ordering errors, extra actions, invariant breaches, and output-contract violations into distinct conformance failures. On a controlled suite of audited procedural contracts, output-only and trace-aware LLM judges miss many injected structural failures; under gold expected and observed graphs, ContractEval detects and localizes all of them. LLM-backed extraction preserves much of this signal but remains calibration-sensitive. ContractEval is therefore not a compliance guarantee; it makes procedural conformance auditable rather than implicit in final-answer quality.

</details>


### [209] [Multi-Agent Agentic Graph Learning via Structural Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09565)
*Liang Qu,Jianxin Li,Hua Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAAGL框架，通过将图划分为社区并分配独立代理，利用结构签名和语义过滤解决现有代理图学习在异构图上的次优问题及上下文膨胀挑战，显著提升了推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理图学习（AGL）方法要么使用单代理，要么使用基于角色的多代理，但均在不同图区域共享推理策略，难以适应具有异构结构和语义模式的图；直接应用多代理协作面临图结构线性化破坏置换不变性以及采样邻域扩大导致上下文激增两大挑战。

Method: 提出多代理代理图学习（MAAGL）框架：1）将图划分为社区，为每个社区分配独立代理以实现区域专用化；2）分离表示结构与语义证据，结构证据通过动态更新的结构签名（置换不变且大小固定）总结，语义证据仅保留按相关性排序的前k个节点；3）基于具有相似签名的历史轨迹估计置信度，并在必要时触发辩论式协作。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，MAAGL的性能优于现有的最先进（SOTA）代理图学习方法。

Conclusion: MAAGL通过社区划分、独立代理专用化、结构签名及辩论式协作机制，有效解决了异构图推理中的策略共享局限及上下文管理难题，证明了多代理协作在复杂图推理任务中的优越性。

Abstract: Agentic graph learning (AGL) has recently achieved promising results on graph reasoning tasks, where an agent powered by a large language model (LLM) sequentially samples the graph as evidence to support its final prediction. Existing methods either employ a single agent or orchestrate multiple role-based agents to reason and learn over the entire graph, but both essentially rely on a shared reasoning policy across different graph regions, which can be suboptimal for graphs with heterogeneous structural and semantic patterns. Inspired by the progress of multi-agent collaboration on complex reasoning tasks, a natural remedy is to let multiple agents own different memory and collaborate; however, applying this paradigm to graphs directly faces two challenges. First, existing AGL methods typically verbalize graph structures into natural-language descriptions for LLM agents, making the reasoning process sensitive to the ordering of structural information and thereby breaking the permutation-invariant nature of graphs. Second, incorporating increasingly large sampled neighborhoods leads to rapidly growing contexts. To address these challenges, this paper introduces a multi-agent agentic graph learning (i.e., MAAGL) framework. MAAGL partitions the graph into communities and assigns an independent agent to each community for region-specific specialization. MAAGL represents structural and semantic evidence separately. Structural evidence is summarized by a dynamically updated structural signature that is permutation-invariant and fixed in size, while semantic evidence is filtered to the top-k nodes ranked by relevance. Based on historical trajectories with similar signatures, agents estimate their confidence and trigger debate-style collaboration when needed. Extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets show that MAAGL outperforms SOTA AGL methods.

</details>


### [210] [CityPlanner: A Sandbox Agent for Executable Urban Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09578)
*Wentao Zhang,Jingyuan Wang,Zetong Zhou,Yifan Yang,Wenrui Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CityPlanner，一个基于沙盒代理的可执行城市规划框架，通过统一文件环境UrbanSandbox和原子任务强化学习（分解为BuildPlan和ImprovePlan），在真实基准测试中优于现有启发式、特定任务RL及通用LLM代理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化和强化学习方法在处理城市规划等现实空间优化问题时，往往依赖特定任务的表示和约束处理，难以适应固定公式之外的灵活场景，缺乏可执行反馈机制。

Method: 提出CityPlanner框架，包含UrbanSandbox（统一基于文件的环境，支持代理检查任务文件、生成计划、运行评估器并基于可执行反馈修正决策）和原子任务强化学习（将长轨迹分解为初始构建的BuildPlan和基于反馈优化的ImprovePlan两个子任务）。

Result: 在真实世界基准测试中，CityPlanner一致优于启发式方法、特定任务强化学习及通用LLM代理基线；消融实验验证了UrbanSandbox、原子任务RL和迭代部署各组件的贡献。

Conclusion: CityPlanner通过沙盒环境与原子任务分解有效解决了城市规划中的可执行性与学习 tractability 问题，为复杂空间优化提供了通用且高效的代理框架。

Abstract: Urban planning is a real-world spatial optimization problem that requires selecting feasible actions from large candidate spaces under practical objectives such as cost and service quality. Existing optimization and reinforcement learning methods are effective for fixed formulations, but often depend on task-specific representations and constraint handling. We propose \emph{CityPlanner}, a sandbox-agent framework for executable urban planning. CityPlanner introduces \emph{UrbanSandbox}, a unified file-based environment where agents inspect task files, generate plans, run evaluators, and revise decisions based on executable feedback. To make learning tractable, we further propose atomic-task reinforcement learning, which decomposes long sandbox trajectories into \emph{BuildPlan} for initial construction and \emph{ImprovePlan} for feedback-based refinement. Experiments on a real-world benchmark show that CityPlanner consistently outperforms heuristic, task-specific RL, and general LLM-agent baselines. Ablations verify the contributions of UrbanSandbox, atomic-task RL, and iterative deployment. We release the code and dataset at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/co-agent-C1C8

</details>


### [211] [A Function-Space Approach to the Statistical Mechanics of Learning Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09589)
*Yizhou Zhang,Weichen Wu,Lun Du,Zhengjie Miao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种直接在函数空间中描述深度神经网络学习的统计力学框架，揭示了学习算子与统计算子之间的相互作用机制，证明了网络倾向于沿平滑且适应数据的方向快速松弛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深度神经网络在巨大的参数空间中表现出高度非线性的动力学，但它们却展现出规则的宏观行为。现有的参数空间分析难以直接解释这种宏观层面的稳定集体组织现象，因此需要建立一种直接在函数空间中描述学习过程的统计力学理论。

Method: 将参数配置视为微观实现，将函数及其动力学算子视为宏观变量。针对均方损失，推导由学习算子 $M=JJ^\ast$ 控制的精确误差动力学。结合条件随机动力学的动态玻尔兹曼权重与参数空间态密度（其局部曲率定义统计算子 $B$），通过对局部涨落积分，构建 fluctuation 势函数 $\Phi_{\mathrm{fluc}}$，并分析其在 ReLU 型函数空间下的性质。

Result: 推导出了涨落项公式 $\Phi_{\mathrm{fluc}}(M;B)$。研究发现，在固定谱下，当 $[M,B]=0$ 时该项旋转静止；通过将 $M$ 的大特征值与 $B$ 的小特征值配对可实现最小化，从而产生对抗旋转错配的局部恢复力。在 ReLU 网络中，低 $B$ 区域对应低结构曲率，表明学习过程偏好沿平滑且适应数据的方向进行更快的松弛。

Conclusion: 函数空间是研究学习过程中稳定集体组织的自然宏观层级。该框架成功地将微观参数动力学与宏观函数行为联系起来，解释了深度网络为何能表现出规则的学习轨迹和泛化能力。

Abstract: Deep neural networks exhibit regular macroscopic behavior despite highly nonlinear dynamics in vast parameter spaces. We develop a statistical-mechanical description of learning directly in function space, treating parameter configurations as microscopic realizations and functions with their dynamical operators as macroscopic variables. For mean-squared loss, the exact error dynamics are governed by the learning operator \(M=JJ^\ast\). Combining the dynamical Boltzmann weight of the conditional stochastic dynamics with the parameter-space density of states, whose local curvature defines a statistical operator \(B\), and integrating over local fluctuations yields
  $$ Φ_{\mathrm{fluc}}(M;B)=\frac{σ_ξ^2}{2}\log\det(M^{-1}+B)+\mathrm{const}. $$
  At fixed spectrum, this term is rotationally stationary when \([M,B]=0\), is minimized by pairing large eigenvalues of \(M\) with small eigenvalues of \(B\), and generates a local restoring contribution against rotational mismatch. For ReLU-type function spaces under mild stable statistical conditions, \(B=σ_ξ^2L^\ast\mathcal K L\), where \(L\) measures coarse-grained second-order structure. Thus the low-\(B\) sector corresponds, up to bounded anisotropy of \(\mathcal K\), to low structural curvature, implying a preference for faster relaxation along smooth, data-adaptive directions. These results identify function space as a natural macroscopic level for studying stable collective organization in learning.

</details>


### [212] [From State Synchronization to Cognitive Self-Evolution: An Operational Architecture for Cognitive Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09625)
*Haoran Gao,An Li,Zhen Li,Jun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对现有认知数字孪生（CDT）研究缺乏系统性架构整合的问题，提出了一种包含物理层、数字孪生层、认知层和任务层的四层CDT架构，构建了自进化闭环操作回路，并通过仿真验证了其在有限语义信息下的任务可行性与效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CDT研究多聚焦于学习模块、知识图谱和大语言模型等单一使能技术，缺乏关于如何将认知能力系统性集成到数字孪生架构中的深入见解，导致架构设计缺乏统一指导。

Method: 提出一种四层CDT架构（物理层、数字孪生层、认知层、任务层），建立跨越四层的自进化闭环操作回路：物理状态同步至数字表示，认知层利用知识、记忆和注意力构建任务特定模型，任务层在约束下生成决策，操作反馈进而优化认知经验并更新数字表示中的关系与标注。此外，定义了用户请求驱动和自驱动两种认知模式，并探讨了语义通信、知识查询等关键机制。

Result: 轻量级仿真研究表明，该框架在有限语义信息下能实现可靠的闭环任务执行，并通过积累的任务经验显著提高了操作效率。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为未来认知数字孪生系统的设计与开发提供了结构化的基础，解决了认知能力系统性集成的架构难题。
