Method: 提出Fortunate Recall (FR) 作为可组合的策略层。该方法首先将个人事实分类为包含10+1类别的行为本体，然后基于LLM提取的元数据，对每个类别应用确定性的特定生命周期策略（包括差异化时间衰减、槽位键覆盖、事件时间有效性及类别感知检索路由）。其实现版本FR-Bank不依赖特定基础设施。

Result: 1. 在新建的LifecycleBench基准（516题）上，FR-Bank通过率为76.9%，优于Mem0、A-MEM等竞品（61%-70.5%）。
2. 在LongMemEval-S上达到75.2%通过率，且未增加标准检索成本。
3. 消融实验表明，通用生命周期元数据保证了正确性，而行为本体主要提升校准能力，将下游幻觉率减半（12.0% vs 24.2%）。
4. 端到端测试显示，FR-Bank将Mem0的幻觉率从45.1%降至22.4%（已回答查询），同时正确回答率从18.6%提升至31.2%。
5. 在BEAM基准上，正确率达46.8%，显著优于Mem0的32.9%，尤其在矛盾解决方面表现突出。

Conclusion: 基于行为本体的细粒度生命周期管理能有效解决LLM记忆系统的存储膨胀与精度退化问题。通用生命周期元数据是正确性的关键，而行为本体分类则显著降低了幻觉并提升了矛盾解决能力。该研究证明了策略分解的有效性，并开源了相关本体、基准测试及代码。

Abstract: Current LLM memory systems treat all personal facts identically, so stores grow without bound while retrieval precision degrades. The core challenge is lifecycle management: which memories should persist, which should be replaced, and at what rate, conditioned on the behavioral type of each fact. Fortunate Recall (FR) is a composable policy layer that classifies personal facts into a 10+1 behavioral ontology and applies category-specific lifecycle policies (differential temporal decay, slot-key supersession, event-time validity, and category-aware retrieval routing) as deterministic functions over LLM-extracted metadata. FR-Bank, our infrastructure-independent implementation, reaches a 76.9% pass rate on LifecycleBench, a new 516-question temporal-disambiguation benchmark, ahead of Mem0, A-MEM, Memory-R1, and MemoryOS (61% to 70.5%), and 75.2% on the full LongMemEval-S under the canonical Wu et al. judge protocol, so lifecycle policies impose no measurable cost on standard retrieval. A pre-registered ablation locates the gains: replacing the typed layer with three generic lifecycle primitives leaves correctness statistically unchanged (-1.7pp, 95% CI [-6.0, +2.7]), so the generic lifecycle metadata carries the correctness advantage, while the behavioral ontology carries calibration, halving downstream confabulation (12.0% vs 24.2%, p<0.001). End-to-end, FR-Bank cuts confabulation from Mem0's 45.1% to 22.4% over answered queries and from 32.2% to 13.0% over all queries while answering more of them correctly (31.2% vs 18.6%); the ranking replicates on the open-weight Kimi K2.5. The decomposition transfers to BEAM, an independently built benchmark: 46.8% correct vs Mem0's 32.9% over 280 questions, with the ontology's benefit concentrated in contradiction resolution and saturating near seven policy clusters. The ontology, benchmark, and code are released.

</details>


### [247] [ConvMem: Convolutional Memory for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10441)
*Hongming Zhang,Zhaozhen Gu,Fengshuo Bai,Ming Hao,Qingyang Zhang,Yuanyuan Wang,Shiyang Tang,Yanna Wang,Bo Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ConvMem，一种无需训练、高度并行的框架，通过将长上下文推理重构为分层卷积来解决大语言模型处理超长文本时的局限，避免了现有序列方法的高延迟和过拟合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型受限于固定上下文长度，而处理长上下文的序列方法（如 MemAgent）存在高延迟、依赖昂贵的强化学习训练以及容易在特定数据集上过拟合的问题。

Method: 提出 ConvMem 框架，灵感源自卷积神经网络（CNN），将带查询提示的 LLM 视为卷积核。该方法通过分层卷积将线性推理路径缩短为对数树结构，并整合了可配置步长（Configurable Strides）、跳跃连接（Skip Connections）以确保证据的鲁棒捕获与传播，同时采用多核卷积（Multi-Kernel Convolution）将复杂查询分解为解耦的语义通道。

Result: 在 RULER-HotpotQA 和 RULER-2WikiMultiHopQA 数据集上的实验表明，ConvMem 的表现优于其他无需训练的基线模型，并避免了强化学习训练模型在分布外任务中常见的过拟合参数先验的风险。

Conclusion: ConvMem 通过分层卷积设计有效缓解了误差累积，实现了文本片段和推理线程的大规模并行化，为长上下文推理提供了一种高效、鲁棒且无需训练的解决方案。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities, they often struggle with extremely long contexts due to fixed context limits. To address this, sequential approaches like MemAgent extend the effective context by reading text in segments and iteratively updating a fixed-size memory. However, this sequential paradigm suffers from high latency and requires costly reinforcement learning (RL) training, which can lead to overfitting on specific datasets. To overcome these limitations, we propose ConvMem, a training-free, highly parallelizable framework that reformulates long-context reasoning as a hierarchical convolution. Inspired by CNNs, ConvMem treats an LLM prompted with a specific query as a convolutional kernel. This kernel summarizes text segments hierarchically, shortening the reasoning path from a linear chain into a logarithmic tree. Specifically, ConvMem integrates \textit{Configurable Strides} and \textit{Skip Connections} to ensure robust evidence capture and propagation, while employing \textit{Multi-Kernel Convolution} to decompose complex queries into disentangled semantic channels. This design not only mitigates error accumulation but also enables massive parallelization across both text segments and reasoning threads. Experiments on RULER-HotpotQA and RULER-2WikiMultiHopQA demonstrate that ConvMem outperforms training-free baselines and avoids the risk of overfitting to parametric priors often observed in RL-trained models on out-of-distribution tasks.

</details>


### [248] [JarvisGUI: Towards Cross-Device GUI Agents with Dynamic Task Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10451)
*Zixiang Chen,Yuheng Lu,Zihao Cheng,Zeming Liu,Jizeng Bai,Ziye Huang,Zhiyin Lin,Zihan Li,Yuhang Guo,Yunhong Wang,Haifeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了JarvisGUI，一个针对跨设备工作流的动态基准测试框架，旨在解决现有基准仅评估单设备静态任务的局限，揭示了当前顶尖GUI代理在跨平台协作和长程依赖管理上的显著能力缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的GUI使用常涉及跨越多设备和平台的工作流，需要状态共享和异构环境协调，但现有基准测试主要关注单设备静态任务，导致对代理在真实场景中就绪程度的评估过于乐观，缺乏对跨设备能力的考察。

Method: 引入JarvisGUI动态基准，将GUI任务形式化为轻量级类型系统下的输入输出转换，支持自动构建多步跨设备工作流；通过在涵盖Android、Windows和Ubuntu的虚拟环境中统一评估代理性能，测试其跨平台交互能力。

Result: 评估结果显示，当前最先进的开源GUI代理在状态转移感知、跨平台上下文推理以及长程依赖管理方面表现挣扎，无法有效应对真实世界工作流的复杂性。

Conclusion: JarvisGUI暴露了现有基准测试中不可见的关键能力缺口，表明当前GUI代理尚未准备好应对现实世界中复杂的跨设备异构环境协作任务，亟需提升相关核心能力。

Abstract: Real-world GUI usage frequently involves workflows that span multiple devices and platforms, requiring the transfer of intermediate results, maintenance of shared state, and coordination across heterogeneous environments. However, existing GUI benchmarks overwhelmingly evaluate agents on single-device, statically defined tasks, thus leaving such cross-device capabilities largely unexamined, resulting in an overly optimistic assessment of agents' readiness for real-world usage. We introduce JarvisGUI, a dynamic benchmark that evaluates GUI agents on cross-device workflows requiring coordinated interaction across heterogeneous platforms, including Android, Windows, and Ubuntu. Specifically, JarvisGUI formulates GUI tasks as input-output transformations under a lightweight type system, which allows us to automatically compose multi-step, cross-device workflows and dynamically evaluate agent performance within a unified framework. By evaluating agents in virtual environments spanning multiple operating systems, JarvisGUI reveals that state-of-the-art open-source GUI agents struggle with the state-transfer awareness, cross-platform contextual reasoning, and long-horizon dependency management required for real-world workflows, exposing a critical capability gap invisible to existing benchmarks.

</details>


### [249] [Less Is Personal: Learning Minimal Sufficient User Profiles for Personalized Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08180)
*Minghang Liu,Qiang Qiu,Yuanzhuo Wang,Huawei Shen,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ...


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ...

Method: ...

Result: ...

Conclusion: ...

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented personalization enables large language models to produce more accurate and preference-aligned outputs using relevant records retrieved from user histories. Personalized language models typically prepend a fixed number of retrieved user records, even when additional history is redundant, harmful, or unrelated to a user's distinctive behavior. We study minimal sufficient personalization: constructing the least costly ordered profile for each input while preserving the utility achievable from a retrieved candidate pool. We introduce ENOUGH, a method that iteratively appends behavioral records or emits STOP to construct profiles with adaptive lengths. Offline, bounded counterfactual search evaluates profile prefixes by jointly considering downstream gains, user specificity, and token costs. The resulting long-horizon targets are distilled into a multi-head value controller with explicit ranking and stopping supervision. At inference, the controller selects and orders records through lightweight decisions, and the frozen generator is invoked once after stopping. Extensive experiments on six personalized tasks demonstrate that ENOUGH consistently outperforms strong heuristic and retrieval-augmented baselines in both effectiveness and efficiency, achieving minimal sufficient profiles that preserve personalization utility while reducing unnecessary context costs.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [250] [Spectral origin of the topological gap exponent d + η: mechanism, kernel, decomposition, and scope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09159)
*Matthew Loftus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解析推导了拓扑间隙 $\Delta$ 的标度律 $\Delta\sim L^{d+\eta}$，将其分解为体积因子与反常维度因子的乘积，证明了该机制仅在 $d<2+\eta$（即 $d=2$）下由红外主导，并通过 Potts 模型数值模拟验证了谱积分与磁化强度的强相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在从解析角度理解临界点云拓扑间隙 $\Delta$（即 $H_1$ 总持久性超出密度匹配零模型的部分）的标度行为，解释其为何遵循 $\Delta\sim L^{d+\eta}$，并阐明维度 $d$ 与反常维度 $\eta$ 对谱积分红外/紫外主导性的影响机制。

Method: 通过解析推导谱积分 $I(\alpha) = \sum_{k\neq 0} S_{\mathrm{conn}}(k)\,|k|^\alpha$ 的标度行为，将其分解为体积项 $I_0$ 和形状项 $I_{\mathrm{shape}}$；结合 $q=4$ Potts 模型在 $L=32$--$256$ 范围内的 $\alpha$ 扫描数值模拟，分析 $I(-2\eta)$ 与磁化强度 $m$ 的协方差关系，并扩展 Divol--Polonik 启发式论证至非均匀泊松强度。

Result: 解析证明 $I(-2\eta) \sim L^{d+\eta}$ 且机制要求 $d < 2+\eta$（限制于物理系统的 $d=2$），$d=3$ 时转为紫外主导；数值发现 $q=4$ 的 $\alpha_{\mathrm{opt}} \in [-0.75, -0.5]$ 符合 Ising 模型而非 $q=4$ 理论值；$\Delta$ 与 $I(-0.5)$ 的每构型一致性主要由磁化强度相关驱动 ($R^2=0.91$)，剔除 $|M|$ 后相关性消失；形状因子 $|k|^{-2\eta}$ 仅通过系综 $L$ 标度确立。

Conclusion: 拓扑间隙的标度律源于体积因子（磁化驱动方差）与反常维度形状因子的解耦，该机制受维度限制仅在 $d=2$ 有效；观测到的拓扑持久性与谱积分的高度相关本质上是磁化强度的协方差效应，需更大尺度 ($L \geq 1024$) 模拟以确认 $q=4$ 模型的反常维度特性。

Abstract: The topological gap $Δ$ -- the excess $H_1$ total persistence of a critical point cloud over a density-matched null -- scales as $Δ\sim L^{d+η}$. We derive this analytically: the spectral integral $I(α) = \sum_{k\neq 0} S_{\mathrm{conn}}(k)\,|k|^α$ scales as $L^{2-α-η}$ when IR-dominated, giving $I(-2η) \sim L^{d+η}$. The decomposition $I(-2η) = I_0 \cdot I_{\mathrm{shape}}$ separates volume ($I_0 \propto N(1-m^2) \sim L^d$) from anomalous dimension ($I_{\mathrm{shape}} \sim L^η$); the volume factor accounts for the magnetization-driven per-configuration variance of $Δ$. We prove the mechanism requires $d < 2 + η$ (IR dominance), confining it to $d = 2$ for physical systems; in $d = 3$ the spectral integral is UV-dominated, explaining why density normalization is needed. An $α$-sweep for Potts $q = 4$ at $L = 32$--$256$ finds $α_{\mathrm{opt}}$ in $[-0.75, -0.5]$, consistent with $-2η_{\mathrm{Ising}}$ and inconsistent with $-2η_{q=4} = -1$; we flag this as tentative pending $L \geq 1024$ confirmation. The $\langle m^2 \cdot I(-2η)\rangle$ hyperscaling product is dominated by the correlation $r(m^2, I) \approx -0.98$ via the shared $I_0$ amplitude, so we report it as a covariance-correction analysis. Under a heuristic argument extending Divol--Polonik to inhomogeneous Poisson intensities, the bare PH kernel is flat; the effective kernel acquires $k$-dependence only at criticality. The per-configuration agreement between $Δ$ and $I(-0.5)$ is primarily a magnetization correlation: $R^2 = 0.91$ at $L = 256$ collapses to $R^2 \approx 0$ once $|M|$ is partialed out. Per-configuration evidence corroborates the $I_0$ Parseval identity but not the $|k|^{-2η}$ shape factor; the latter is established by ensemble $L$-scaling.

</details>


### [251] [Physics-informed neural networks by Gradient-Guided Gaussian Adaptive Sampling (3GAS-PINNs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09162)
*Yousen Wang,Wei Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出3GAS-PINNs方法，通过结合均匀分布与基于梯度高斯平滑的自适应采样策略，解决了传统PINNs在非线性问题中收敛慢、梯度不平衡及难以捕捉激波等局部间歇结构的问题，在三个基准测试中将数值模拟精度最高提升了14倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统物理信息神经网络（PINNs）在求解非线性偏微分方程时，因采用固定的损失权重和均匀的配点分布，导致收敛速度慢、梯度不平衡，且无法适应解场演化的复杂性，难以准确捕捉激波和孤立子等具有陡峭梯度的局部间歇结构。

Method: 提出梯度引导的高斯自适应采样物理信息神经网络（3GAS-PINNs），该方法融合了均匀概率分布与源自解空间梯度的高斯平滑概率分布。这种混合策略既保持了全局约束的满足，又能在高梯度区域集中配点，从而动态适应解场的复杂性。

Result: 在一维强制Burgers方程、Korteweg-de Vries (KdV) 方程和非线性薛定谔方程三个具有强非线性或陡峭梯度的基准问题上进行了评估。相比基线PINNs，3GAS-PINNs有效提升了间歇区域的物理一致性，数值模拟精度最高提升了14倍。

Conclusion: 3GAS-PINNs通过自适应采样机制成功克服了传统PINNs在处理非线性及局部间歇结构时的局限性，显著提高了对激波和孤立子等复杂物理现象的捕捉能力及整体求解精度。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) provide a mesh-free framework for solving partial differential equations, yet their performance in nonlinear problems is often limited by slow convergence, gradient imbalance, and insufficient resolution to capture localized intermittent structures such as shock waves[1]. These issues arise primarily from the use of fixed weights of loss and uniform collocation point distributions, which cannot adapt to the evolving complexity of the solution field during training. To address these challenges, Gradient-Guided Gaussian Adaptive Sampling Physics-Informed Neural Networks (3GAS-PINNs) is proposed in this paper, which combines uniform probability distribution and Gaussian-smoothed probability distribution derived from the spatial gradients of solution, to maintain global constraint satisfaction as well as concentrating collocation points in regions of high gradient. Thus, intermittency structures like shock wave and solitons can be accurately captured. The method is evaluated on three benchmark nonlinear problems, including one-dimensional forced Burgers equation, Korteweg-de Vries (KdV) equation and nonlinear Schrodinger equation, all of which exhibit steep gradients or strong nonlinearity. In comparison with baseline PINNs, 3GAS-PINNs can effectively promote the physical consistency in intermittent regions. The accuracy of the numerical simulation can be improved by a factor of up to 14.

</details>


### [252] [World-Time Compute with Verified Code World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09163)
*James Schwoebel,Ingrida Semenec,Jenia Rousseva,Marcos Ortiz,Collin Overbay,Christopher Klaus,Anderson Edmond,Manish Bhatt,Rome Thorstenson,Jessica Tsai,Martin G. Frasch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“世界时间计算”（world-time compute）方法，通过将领域动态编写为可执行代码来生成大量带精确标签的合成世界模型，用于微调小参数大语言模型（LLM），显著提升其在未见世界上的泛化能力，尤其在资源稀缺的小模型上效果显著，但在大模型或长链条推理任务中收益递减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大语言模型在缺乏大量真实标注数据的领域中泛化能力不足的问题，传统方法依赖真实数据或领域随机化，但存在标注成本高、标签不精确或任务多样性不足以驱动泛化等局限。

Method: 将领域动态编码为可验证的符号状态程序，每个程序实例化为一个独立的世界模型，生成无限量的精确标注轨迹；利用这些轨迹对LLM进行微调（称为“世界时间计算”），并在多个合成世界及真实基准（如ARC-AGI、List Functions、CLRS）上评估其跨世界泛化能力，同时通过 corrupted-label 对照实验验证标签精确性而非任务多样性是性能提升的关键。

Result: 在0.5B参数模型上获得+29分的显著提升；在List Functions任务中，基于128个独立世界训练的适配器在未见世界上达到40%准确率，远超corrupted-label对照组的6%（+34分）；合成世界的20步 rollout 标签100%精确，能准确回答10倍于训练分布的OOD探测；但增益随模型规模增大、推理链变长或涉及感知任务而饱和或减弱，跨任务迁移能力较弱。

Conclusion: “世界时间计算”是一种高效且可验证的训练范式，特别适用于符号状态领域和小模型，通过精确标签而非任务多样性驱动泛化；该方法由OpenWorld框架支持，但不适用于像素原生领域，且其增益具有饱和性，并非普适定律。

Abstract: LLMs generalize across a domain only after seeing many real, labeled examples, which most domains lack. We study a way to manufacture it cheaply. When a domain's dynamics can be written as code, one template instantiates into many world models: executable, verifiable programs over symbolic state, each an inexhaustible source of exactly-labeled trajectories. Fine-tuning an LLM on trajectories through many such worlds, which we call world-time compute, a training-time analogue of test-time compute, lifts generalization to held-out worlds it never trained on (synthesized world families). Gains are largest where capability is scarcest: +29 points at 0.5B; the largest model's lift is within noise, consistent with saturation. Labels can be trusted because the worlds are verified code: synthesized-then-checked dynamics are exact over 20-step rollouts and answer 10x out-of-distribution probes exactly (100%), whereas per-step LLM and MLP predictors compound error and collapse. Unlike domain randomization, each world is independently authored and verified; a corrupted-label control shows label exactness, not task variety, drives the gains. On real benchmarks (ARC-AGI grids, List Functions, CLRS) the same lever holds as per-world test-time training. On List Functions the harder cross-world form holds: one adapter trained on 128 disjoint worlds reaches 40% on held-out worlds versus 6% for a corrupted-label control (+34 points, CI [29, 39]). The gain is a saturating regularity, not a law: largest for few-step reasoning and small/weak models, fading for long chains, perception-induced tasks, and saturated tasks; cross-task transfer is weak without shared skill. Worlds are authored and served by OpenWorld, a zero-dependency framework (companion paper). Scope: symbolic state; pixel-native domains remain territory of learned models. All code, recipes, and this manuscript regenerate from one repository.

</details>


### [253] [Storage-Scalable Progressive Semantic Communication via Knowledge-Base Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10112)
*Heng Zhu,Ye Liu,Kun Zhu,Feifei Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SSKBQ的存储可扩展知识库复用量化方案，通过复用少量知识库解决多阶段量化中存储开销线性增长的问题，同时保持竞争力的重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的知识库辅助语义通信方案中，单知识库量化（SKBQ）存储开销小但量化能力受限，而多知识库残差量化（MKBQ）虽支持渐进式细化，但其知识库存储量随传输深度线性增长，导致存储可扩展性差。

Method: 提出存储可扩展知识库复用量化（SSKBQ）方案，核心在于跨多个残差细化阶段复用一组紧凑的知识库，从而解耦传输阶段数与维护的知识库数量；同时引入阶段感知残差监督机制，以正则化中间量化表示并促进渐进式细化。

Result: 实验结果表明，知识库复用策略有效解决了存储可扩展性问题，并且在保持竞争性渐进式重建性能的同时，显著降低了存储需求。

Conclusion: SSKBQ通过知识库复用机制成功平衡了存储效率与重建质量，为语义通信系统中的存储可扩展性提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Existing knowledge-base-assisted semantic communication schemes commonly adopt either single knowledge-base quantization (SKBQ) or multi-knowledge-base residual quantization (MKBQ). SKBQ incurs limited storage overhead but has restricted quantization capacity, whereas MKBQ supports progressive refinement by assigning an independent knowledge base (KB) to each stage, causing the KB storage to grow linearly with the transmission depth. To address this problem, we propose storage-scalable knowledge-base reuse quantization (SSKBQ), which reuses a compact set of KBs across multiple residual refinement stages and thereby decouples the number of transmission stages from the number of maintained KBs. A stage-aware residual supervision mechanism is further introduced to regularize intermediate quantized representations and encourage progressive refinement. Experimental results demonstrate that KB reuse provides an effective solution to the storage scalability problem while maintaining competitive progressive reconstruction performance.

</details>


### [254] [Scaling Post-Training Ternarisation to Qwen3-8B Capability Retention, Reproduction, Lossless Packing, and Packed Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09240)
*Anirudh Malik,M Sparsh Mehra,Poojith Devan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了将激进的后训练量化流程从 Qwen3-4B 扩展至 Qwen3-8B 的端到端特性，验证了模型规模提升能增强对低比特离散化的鲁棒性，并实现了无损打包存储与直接执行，尽管推理速度暂未超越 FP16。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决超低比特语言模型中标称比特数（如"1.58-bit"）未明确具体部署表示形式及执行成本的问题，填补从 4B 到 8B 模型在激进量化下的规模化扩展特性分析空白。

Method: 采用仅权重的 A16 配置，结合 KOTMS 旋转、E2M-ATQ 自适应三值化及 GPTQ 风格误差补偿算法；通过外部复现门控、4B/8B 能力匹配分析、跨语料困惑度评估、有效比特核算、无损晶格感知打包及直接打包执行进行端到端表征。

Result: 8B 模型在三语料上的困惑度比率为 1.361 倍；零样本任务平均准确率为 64.6%（FP16 为 72.4%），机会校正保留率为 78.5%；相比 4B 模型（保留率 69.6%），8B 具有 8.9 个百分点的优势。打包检查点大小为 8.24 GiB，直接执行速度为 15.52 tokens/s（显存占用 7.35 GiB），但当前 GEMV 内核仍慢于 FP16 cuBLAS。

Conclusion: 模型规模增大提升了对激进后训练离散化的鲁棒性，解决了实际序列化问题并验证了直接执行的可行性；但更广泛的种子选择、校准分布优化及内核加速仍是未来开放课题。

Abstract: Ultra-low-bit language models promise reductions in storage and memory traffic, but a nominal "1.58-bit" label does not specify the deployed representation or its execution cost. We study a scale-up of an aggressive post-training conversion pipeline from Qwen3-4B to Qwen3-8B.
  The conversion uses KOTMS rotation, E2M-ATQ adaptive ternarisation, and GPTQ-style error compensation in a weight-only A16 configuration. We do not claim these algorithms as new. Our contribution is the end-to-end scale-up characterisation: an external reproduction gate, matched 4B/8B capability analysis, cross-corpus perplexity, effective-bit accounting, lossless lattice-aware packing, and direct packed execution.
  The 8B model reaches a three-corpus perplexity ratio of 1.361x, with WikiText-2, C4, and PTB ratios of 1.318x, 1.393x, and 1.371x. On eight zero-shot tasks at n = 500, mean accuracy is 64.6% versus 72.4% for FP16, corresponding to 78.5% chance-corrected retention and a 7.8-point absolute cost. The matched 4B run retains 69.6%, yielding an 8.9-point 8B advantage.
  The packed checkpoint is 8.24 GiB and preserves the recorded perplexity to measurement precision. Direct packed execution reaches 15.52 tokens/s in 7.35 GiB, while a preliminary packed GEMV remains slower than FP16 cuBLAS. The result is a validated scale-up baseline: model size improves robustness to aggressive post-training discretisation, actual serialisation is solved for the measured artefact, and direct execution is feasible, while broader seeds, calibration distributions, and kernel optimisation remain open.

</details>


### [255] [Distribution-Consistent Inference for Dynamic Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09241)
*Dohyeon Kim,Bedionita Soro,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对混合专家模型（MoE）在推理时动态减少激活专家数量导致的分布偏移问题，提出了一种轻量级的层分布对齐（LDA）方法，该方法通过校准统计量修正表示失配，在保持稀疏推理效率的同时显著恢复了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MoE模型通常采用固定的top-k专家选择策略，未考虑不同token对专家数量的实际需求。虽然推理时动态调整top-k可以减少计算量，但会导致输出分布的RMS尺度和方差增加，引发表示失配（representation mismatch），从而造成下游任务性能下降，而现有方法往往忽视了这种由路由配置偏离引起的分布偏移。

Method: 提出层分布对齐（Layer-wise Distribution Alignment, LDA）技术，这是一种轻量级的推理时校正方法。该方法利用层级的校准统计量，将减少路由后的表示与默认配置下的表示进行对齐，以纠正因激活专家数量减少而产生的分布偏移。

Result: 在多种SMoE大语言模型、基准测试及路由策略上的实验表明，LDA能够有效恢复因减少路由引起的分布偏移所导致的性能损失，同时保持了稀疏推理的高效性，且带来的计算开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: LDA成功解决了MoE模型在动态减少专家激活数时的表示失配问题，证明了通过简单的统计量校准即可在不重新训练的情况下实现高效的动态推理，为平衡模型容量与推理效率提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures have emerged as a powerful paradigm for scaling model capacity while preserving efficient inference in large foundation models. However, most MoE models use a fixed top-$k$ expert selection policy, assigning the same expert budget to every token even when fewer experts may be sufficient. Inference-time dynamic top-$k$ routing can reduce computation without retraining, but existing methods often overlook the distributional shift caused by deviating from the training-time routing configuration. We show that reducing the number of activated experts consistently increases the RMS scale and variance of SMoE outputs, inducing a representation mismatch that contributes to downstream performance degradation in addition to the loss of expert capacity. To address this correctable component, we propose Layer-wise Distribution Alignment (LDA), a lightweight inference-time correction that uses layer-wise calibration statistics to align reduced-routing representations with the default configuration. Across multiple SMoE LLMs, benchmarks, and routing strategies, LDA recovers much of the performance lost induced by the distributional shift under reduced routing while preserving sparse-inference efficiency with negligible overhead.

</details>


### [256] [DiffLUT-Net: Differentiable Training of FPGA LUT Networks with Learnable Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09254)
*Jiaqi Ye,Xinrui Gong,Jingcun Wang,Olga Kondrateva,Bing Li,Grace Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DiffLUT-Net，一种专为FPGA设计的原生神经网络，通过从头训练六输入查找表（LUT）及其连接关系，实现了精度与资源消耗的良好平衡，并可直接导出为可综合的Verilog代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的FPGA神经网络部署流程通常仅加速乘累加运算或将预训练量化模型转换为查找表，缺乏针对FPGA原生架构（特别是LUT结构）进行从头端到端优化的方法。

Method: 提出DiffLUT-Net架构，利用可微分的LUT函数松弛技术和硬件源选择机制，联合学习每个六输入LUT的64个真值表条目及其六个输入端的信号来源。训练完成后，对真值表和连接进行离散化处理，剪枝未使用的逻辑，并直接导出为可综合的Verilog代码。

Result: 在五个基准测试中，DiffLUT-Net展现了优于现有方法的精度与资源权衡（accuracy-resource trade-offs）。

Conclusion: 联合学习LUT函数与稀疏连接对于构建紧凑、高效的FPGA原生推理网络是行之有效的策略。

Abstract: Field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs) enable efficient neural-network inference, but most deployment flows either accelerate multiply-accumulate operations or convert pretrained quantized models into lookup tables (LUTs). We present DiffLUT-Net, an FPGA-native network connected by six-input LUTs that are trained from scratch. We jointly learn the 64 truth-table entries of a LUT and the source to each of its six input ports using a differentiable LUT function relaxation and hardware source selection. After training, the truth tables and connections are discretized, unused logic can be pruned, and the network is exported directly as synthesizable Verilog. Across five benchmarks, DiffLUT-Net achieves favorable accuracy-resource trade-offs. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of jointly learning LUT functions and sparse connectivity for compact FPGA-native inference. The code is available at https://github.com/TUDa-HWAI/DiffLUT-Network.

</details>


### [257] [Accountable and uncertainty-aware evaluation of sensor-based AI under distribution shift: devices, subjects, and nearly three years underground](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09257)
*Benny Platte,Rico Thomanek,Christian Roschke,Marc Ritter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分阶段的、可问责的评估协议，用于解决传感器AI系统在部署环境（设备、人员、时间）变化下性能下降的问题，主张使用5%分位数而非均值作为部署决策依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的随机训练-测试拆分无法揭示因设备更新、人员变动及时间跨度导致的模型性能退化，且基于均值的评估指标会掩盖双峰分布等风险，导致部署决策失误。

Method: 提出包含四个累积泛化阶段（保留设备、主体、时间）的评估协议；引入量化不确定性、基于正确类别计数的机会参考基准、针对非现存类别的“现范围外率”指标，并制定基于5%分位数的显式决策规则。

Result: 在无基础设施的地磁定位任务中（两个地下矿井），对34个月后、新设备代际及新测量员数据进行的评估显示：模型现况条件精度的5%分位数为0.39（是机会水平的16.5倍），但不同位置类别间变异显著；均值评估会错误地通过某些5%分位数远低于机会水平的双峰配置。

Conclusion: 基于均值的评估具有误导性，采用考虑量化不确定性和低分位数阈值的分阶段评估协议，能更真实地反映部署风险，确保传感器AI系统在动态变化环境下的可靠性与可问责性。

Abstract: Sensor-based AI systems are rarely operated under the conditions under which they were trained: devices, personnel and recording epochs change, and each change degrades performance in ways a random train-test split cannot reveal. We propose a staged, accountable evaluation protocol that treats the evaluation of a deployed model as a measurement with declared reference levels and a quantified uncertainty. Four cumulative generalisation stages hold out devices, subjects and time. Each stage is judged on quantiles of repeated trainings against chance references with the correct class count, an out-of-present-scope rate exposes silent misdirection towards classes that are no longer present in deployment relative to training, and an explicit decision rule ties roll-out decisions not to means but to 5% quantiles. We demonstrate the protocol on infrastructure-free geomagnetic localisation with smartphone-based recurrent classifiers in two real underground mines, including a replication of the scheme's training stages at the second site. Unchanged models are re-evaluated on data recorded 34 months after the training campaign, on a device generation unknown at training time and with a held-out surveyor. The 5% quantile of their present-conditioned precision there is 0.39 over 299 repeated trainings, 16.5 times the chance level; across the composition of the 42 reachable location classes the figure varies by +/-0.08, several times the spread between repeated runs. Repeated trainings of a single configuration show why means mislead: a bimodal configuration passes a mean-based test decisively while its 5% quantile lies more than an order of magnitude below chance.

</details>


### [258] [Literati: Towards Anytime Optimal Shape Generalized Trees via AO*](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09299)
*Nakul Upadhya,Eldan Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Literati算法，这是首个用于最优形状广义树（SGT）归纳的算法，通过AND/OR图公式联合优化树结构和形状函数复杂度，在24个数据集上优于现有树方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的决策树归纳算法（无论是贪婪的最优决策树还是形状广义树SGT）存在局限性：最优决策树仅限于轴对齐阈值分割，表达力不足导致树结构复杂；而SGT虽提升了表达力，但现有归纳算法是贪婪的，缺乏最优性保证。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的AND/OR图问题公式，用于联合优化树结构和形状函数复杂度；开发了基于AO*的算法，并引入两项增强措施：用于OR节点选择的次要启发式方法和用于AND节点探索的轮询策略，以在保持最优性的同时提升即时性能。

Result: 在24个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，Literati在训练精度和测试精度上均优于最先进的树方法。

Conclusion: Literati成功实现了最优SGT归纳，通过全局优化解决了现有方法在表达力和最优性之间的权衡问题，显著提升了决策树模型的性能和紧凑性。

Abstract: Decision trees are prized for their interpretability and strong performance on tabular data, but popular greedy top-down induction algorithms can yield suboptimal and unnecessarily complex structures. Optimal decision tree methods address this through global optimization, yet remain restricted to axis-aligned threshold splits, which limit the expressivity of each node and often force deep, complex trees to capture non-linear feature effects. Shape Generalized Trees (SGTs) generalize threshold splits to learnable univariate shape functions, improving expressivity and enabling more compact trees. However, existing SGT induction algorithms are greedy and offer no optimality guarantees. In this work, we introduce Literati, the first algorithm for optimal SGT induction. We propose a novel AND/OR graph formulation of the problem that jointly optimizes tree structure and shape function complexity. To solve this AND/OR graph, we develop an AO*-based algorithm with two enhancements that improve anytime performance while preserving optimality: a secondary heuristic for OR-node selection and a round-robin policy for AND-node exploration. Across 24 real-world datasets, Literati achieves higher training and test accuracy than state-of-the-art tree approaches.

</details>


### [259] [Explaining f-Divergence-Based Regularization via Local Curvature and Sharpness-Aware Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09367)
*Nour Jamoussi,Marios Kountouris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了基于散度的正则化与Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) 在参数空间和输入空间扰动下的局部一致性，证明两者均通过曲率敏感惩罚提升泛化能力，并通过实验验证了最大曲率惩罚区域对应最佳模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于散度的正则化和SAM是深度学习中提升泛化能力的两种主要方法，均受扰动鲁棒性启发，但两者之间的理论联系此前尚未被深入探索。

Method: 基于$f$-散度的经典二阶展开，推导参数空间扰动下两种方法的局部一致性（Fisher加权二次型 vs. Hessian主导特征值惩罚）；将该几何视角扩展至输入空间扰动，定义基于输入变换的散度正则化并推导其回拉二次型；利用非对称$\alpha$-偏斜Jensen-Shannon散度族作为实验测试床，分析局部曲率系数与损失景观及模型性能的关系。

Result: 理论证明在指数族输出分布的负对数似然目标下，Fisher矩阵与Gauss-Newton矩阵重合，使两种方法的对应关系更加透明；实验显示更强的诱导曲率惩罚与更平坦的局部极小值相关，且在四个基准数据集上，最大曲率惩罚区域（$\alpha=0.5$） consistently 取得最佳的准确率和负对数似然。

Conclusion: 基于散度的正则化与SAM在局部几何视角下具有本质一致性，前者提供了比标准SAM更通用的扰动框架；最大化诱导曲率惩罚能有效引导模型收敛至更平坦的极小值，从而显著提升泛化性能。

Abstract: Divergence-based regularization and Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) are two prominent approaches for improving generalization in deep learning, both motivated by robustness to perturbations. However, their relationship has remained largely unexplored. Building on classical second-order expansions of $f$-divergences, we show that the two methods are locally consistent under parameter-space perturbations: both induce curvature-sensitive penalties, with divergence regularization yielding a Fisher-weighted quadratic form and SAM penalizing sharpness through the dominant Hessian eigenvalue. For negative log-likelihood objectives with exponential-family output distributions, this correspondence becomes especially transparent, since the Fisher and Gauss-Newton matrices coincide. We further show that the same local geometric perspective extends to input-space perturbations, where divergence-based regularization is defined through transformations of the input. In this setting, the regularizer induces a pullback quadratic form on the input space, providing a more general perturbation framework than standard SAM while preserving the same local sensitivity interpretation. To validate the analysis empirically, we use the asymmetric $α$-skew Jensen-Shannon divergence (JSD) family as a controlled testbed. Its local curvature coefficient scales as $α(1-α)$ and is maximized at the symmetric point $α=\tfrac12$, which recovers the standard JSD. Loss-landscape visualizations in the input-perturbation regime show that stronger induced curvature penalization is associated with flatter local minima. Experiments on four benchmark datasets further demonstrate that both accuracy and negative log-likelihood are consistently best near this regime of maximal curvature penalization.

</details>


### [260] [Constraint-Aware Discrete Black-Box Optimization Using Tensor Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09370)
*Keisuke Onoue,Ryosuke Kojima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于张量分解的代理模型方法，通过将先验逻辑约束直接融入离散黑盒优化过程，有效避开不可行区域，显著提升了采样效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的序列模型优化（SMBO）方法在处理具有已知逻辑约束的离散搜索空间时，其代理模型通常无法捕捉控制可行性的符号规则，导致在不可行区域浪费采样资源。

Method: 提出基于张量分解的代理模型以捕捉离散空间结构并整合可行性信息；将模型训练公式化为约束多项式优化问题，并利用源自T-范数的可微惩罚项求解其松弛形式。

Result: 在合成基准及真实世界任务（如压力容器设计）上的实验表明，该方法能有效引导搜索避开不可行区域，从而提高了采样效率。

Conclusion: 通过在代理模型中直接整合可行性信息，该方法解决了离散黑盒优化中约束处理不足的痛点，为复杂约束下的样本高效优化提供了新途径。

Abstract: Discrete black-box optimization is often addressed using approaches such as Sequential Model-Based Optimization (SMBO), which aims to improve sample efficiency by fitting surrogate models that approximate a costly objective function over a discrete search space. In many real-world problems, the set of feasible inputs is often given by logical constraints known in advance. However, existing surrogate modeling techniques generally fail to capture the symbolic rules governing feasibility in discrete input spaces. In this paper, we propose a surrogate modeling approach based on tensor decomposition that captures the structure of discrete search spaces while directly integrating feasibility information. To implement this approach, we formulate surrogate model training as a constrained polynomial optimization problem and solve a relaxed formulation using a differentiable penalty term derived from T-norms. Our experiments on both synthetic and real-world benchmarks, including a pressure vessel design task, demonstrate that the proposed method improves sample efficiency by effectively guiding the search away from infeasible regions.

</details>


### [261] [Online Inverse Integer Linear Optimization via Small-Gradient Skipping: Constant Regret and Finite Mistakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09809)
*Akira Kitaoka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出小梯度跳过（SGS）机制，通过跳过无错误的更新轮次，解决了在线逆线性优化中遗憾界存在的$\log T$间隙问题，在特定条件下实现了与$T$无关的错误次数上界，并优化了M-凸集下的计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在线逆线性优化通用设置中遗憾上界$O(d \log T)$与下界$\Omega(d)$之间未解决的$\log T$间隙；同时改进M-凸集场景下现有方法需每轮计算重心的高计算成本问题。

Method: 提出小梯度跳过（SGS）机制，在正确动作与其他候选项均匀分离且无预测错误时跳过更新步骤，并将该机制应用于在线梯度下降、在线牛顿步和MetaGrad三种算法。

Result: 1. 三种算法的错误次数上界均与总轮数$T$无关；2. 对于前向问题为整数线性规划的情况，在线牛顿步和MetaGrad结合SGS后的遗憾维度依赖性降为$O(d^2)$，消除了$\log T$因子；3. 在M-凸集下，无需计算重心即可高效获得遗憾上界。

Conclusion: SGS机制有效消除了在线逆线性优化中的$\log T$遗憾间隙，显著降低了维度依赖性，并避免了M-凸集场景下昂贵的重心计算，提升了算法的理论与计算效率。

Abstract: In online inverse linear optimization, the learner predicts a weight at each round, observes the optimal action of the agent, and updates its prediction. In the general setting, the gap of $\log T$ between the regret upper bound $O(d \log T)$ and the lower bound $Ω(d)$ is unresolved (here $T$ is the total number of rounds and $d$ is the dimension). When the action set is M-convex, the regret is known to be bounded by $O(d \log d)$, but the method attaining it computes a center of gravity at every round. This paper therefore proposes Small-Gradient Skipping (SGS), a mechanism that skips the update at rounds without a mistake in the case where the correct action is uniformly separated from the other candidates, and applies it to online gradient descent, the online Newton step, and MetaGrad. The number of mistakes is then bounded, for all three, by a quantity independent of $T$; and for the online Newton step and for MetaGrad with SGS, the dimension dependence of the regret becomes $O(d^2)$ when the forward problem is an integer linear program, that is, the factor $\log T$ is removed. Moreover, when the action set is M-convex, the regret is bounded efficiently without computing a center of gravity.

</details>


### [262] [XAI-Refine: An Automated Explanation-Knowledge Loop for Brain-Age Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09388)
*Yang Qiao,Junjie Wu,Deqiang Qiu,James J. Lah,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出XAI-Refine框架，通过自动化的“解释 - 知识”循环，将事后解释转化为可验证的神经生物学约束，在保持预测精度的同时，引导脑龄预测模型向符合神经生物学证据的方向优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的脑龄预测模型评估仅关注预测精度，忽略了模型机制的可重复性和神经生物学合理性；且现有的事后解释方法通常止步于诊断，缺乏将解释转化为具体修正目标的自动化流程。

Method: 提出XAI-Refine方法，构建自动化解释 - 知识循环：1) 整合多次训练的事后分析生成可靠解释；2) 将解释转化为神经生物学问题并检索验证文献，构建许可集；3) 计算当前解释在许可集上的最小投影作为修正目标；4) 将目标转化为可微约束，仅在多种子验证确认解释向目标移动、性能满足阈值且非目标漂移有界时更新模型。

Result: 在基于静息态功能连接的脑龄预测实验中，该方法有效评估了预测性能、解释可靠性、文献一致性及特定目标的模型修正能力，实现了从单纯的事后分析到证据引导模型优化的结构化转变。

Conclusion: XAI-Refine提供了一条结构化路径，能够利用已验证的神经生物学知识指导模型 refinement，确保模型不仅预测准确，其内部机制也具备神经生物学可解释性和可靠性。

Abstract: Brain-age prediction models are commonly evaluated by predictive accuracy, yet accurate predictions alone do not establish that a model relies on reproducible or neurobiologically supported mechanisms. Post-hoc explanation methods can expose these mechanisms, but existing workflows typically stop at diagnosis or require correction targets to be specified before model analysis. We propose XAI-Refine, an automated explanation-knowledge loop for brain-age prediction from resting-state functional connectivity. At each iteration, XAI-Refine consolidates complementary post-hoc analyses across repeated training runs into reliable, structured model explanations. It converts each reliable explanation into a neutral neurobiological question, retrieves and verifies relevant literature, and compiles the verified evidence into an admissible set in the same typed explanation space. The target for refinement is defined as the minimal projection of the current model explanation onto the admissible set induced by applicable verified knowledge. This revised explanation is then translated into a differentiable constraint while preserving the originating model variable, measurement operator, and applicable scope. Candidate updates are promoted only when multi-seed validation confirms target-directed explanatory movement, predictive performance remains within a prespecified guardrail, and non-target explanatory drift remains bounded. Experiments on functional-connectivity-based brain-age prediction evaluate predictive performance, explanation reliability, literature alignment, and target-specific model revision, illustrating a structured route from post-hoc analysis to evidence-guided model refinement.

</details>


### [263] [Applying foundation model embeddings towards urban livability evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09429)
*Ayush Khot,Wen Zhou,Shaowen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用基础模型嵌入（如AlphaEarth, AnySat, TerraMind）从高分辨率地理空间数据中提取社会经济指标的系统框架，旨在解决数据匮乏地区城市宜居性预测难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据匮乏地区难以准确测量社会经济指标，限制了政策干预和资源分配；而高分辨率地理空间数据广泛可用且蕴含丰富的宜居性统计信息，需挖掘其潜力。

Method: 分析不同地理空间数据类型对城市宜居性预测的影响，系统识别基础模型嵌入中编码的物理特征，构建优先选择高信息量特征的框架，并利用嵌入增强预测性能。

Result: 成功识别出最具预测性的地理空间指标，证明了利用基础模型嵌入可显著提升数据稀缺区域的城市宜居性预测效果。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种原则性的方法论，能够从卫星影像中提取可操作信息并处理复杂空间依赖性，有效应用于数据有限地区的城市宜居性预测。

Abstract: While accurate measurement of socioeconomic indicators remains challenging in data-scarce regions, which limits policy interventions and resource allocation, high-resolution geospatial data is widely available and can contain information on various livability statistics. We investigate which physical features are encoded within foundation model embeddings, such as AlphaEarth, AnySat, and TerraMind, and provide a systematic framework for identifying the most predictive geospatial indicators. By analyzing how different types of geospatial data influence urban livability predictions, our approach enables researchers to prioritize the most informative features for their specific applications. Additionally, we demonstrate how to leverage foundation model embeddings to enhance prediction performance for these outcomes. This work contributes a principled methodology for extracting actionable information from satellite imagery while accounting for complex spatial dependencies, with applications in predicting urban livability in regions with limited observation data.

</details>


### [264] [SCCM : Stream Cruise Control Method for Automated Drift Detection and Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09432)
*Mohammad Abu-Shaira,Weishi Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了流巡航控制方法（SCCM），一个用于在线回归的综合概念漂移检测与自适应框架。SCCM通过早期响应检测、漂移量量化、动态超参数调整和模型重校准，实现了自动化适应，并在18个合成数据集和8个真实数据集上证明了其优于现有基线方法的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界数据分布常随时间演变（概念漂移），忽略漂移会导致预测性能下降，而依赖固定超参数进一步限制了模型在变化条件下的适应性。现有的纯反应式方法通常只在性能下降后才启动适应，缺乏前瞻性。

Method: 提出流巡航控制方法（SCCM），包含早期响应及更新前漂移检测、漂移幅度量化、基于KPI窗口的阈值设定（以减少局部误报）、动态超参数调整和模型重校准。该方法采用内存设计以实现实时适应，并利用动态阈值保持对数据分布的不可知性，支持高维和大规模数据流的KPI监控。

Result: SCCM集成了四种在线回归模型，在涵盖突变、增量和交替渐进漂移的18个合成数据集以及8个真实数据集上进行了评估。使用R2和MSE指标与八个检测器 - 适配器基线进行对比，结果显示SCCM在各种在线回归设置中均具有更好的预测性能和有效的漂移处理能力。

Conclusion: SCCM框架通过 proactive（主动）而非 reactive（被动）的机制，成功解决了在线回归中的概念漂移问题，实现了自动化、实时的模型适应，显著提升了模型在动态数据环境下的鲁棒性和预测准确性。

Abstract: Real-world datasets often exhibit evolving distributions, known as concept drift. Ignoring drift degrades predictive performance, while reliance on fixed hyperparameters further limits model adaptability under changing conditions. Adaptive learning addresses this challenge by continuously updating models online, allowing them to incrementally adjust and remain effective as data distributions evolve. This paper presents the Stream Cruise Control Method (SCCM), a comprehensive framework for drift detection and adaptation in online regression. SCCM enables automated adaptation through early-response, pre-update drift detection, drift magnitude quantification, KPI-window-based thresholding for local false-alarm mitigation, dynamic hyperparameter tuning, and model recalibration. SCCM also adopts an in-memory design for real-time adaptability, unlike purely reactive methods that typically activate adaptation only after performance degradation is observed. By using dynamic thresholding and remaining agnostic to data distributions, SCCM supports KPI-based monitoring across varying data streams, including high-dimensional and large-scale settings. SCCM is integrated with four online regression models and evaluated on 18 synthetic datasets covering abrupt, incremental, and alternating gradual drift, together with eight real-world datasets. The evaluation uses both R2 and MSE and compares against eight detector--adaptation baselines. Results show improved predictive performance and effective drift handling across the evaluated online regression settings.

</details>


### [265] [Efficient Leakage-Free Neural Architecture Search under Leave-One-Subject-Out Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09433)
*Heinke Hihn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无泄露的基于块的LOSO-NAS方法，在BioVid数据集上以极少的参数量提升了分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决留一受试者交叉验证（LOSO）评估中神经架构搜索（NAS）计算成本过高的问题，传统嵌套实现需进行N次独立搜索，计算复杂度高达O(N^2)。

Method: 采用一种无数据泄露的、基于块的架构搜索策略，通过在受试者间共享NAS运行过程来优化计算效率。

Result: 在BioVid热痛数据集上，平均准确率从82.79%提升至83.39%，同时模型参数量最多减少了99.2%。

Conclusion: 该方法有效平衡了LOSO评估下的泛化性能与计算成本，实现了高精度与轻量级模型的统一。

Abstract: Leave-One-Subject-Out (LOSO) evaluation estimates generalisation performance for subject-based classification but makes Neural Architecture Search (NAS) computationally expensive because a fully nested implementation requires N independent architecture searches and, assuming approximately linear training cost, scales as O(N^2). We propose a leakage-free, block-based approach that shares NAS runs across subjects. On the BioVid Heat Pain dataset, our approach increased the mean accuracy from 82.79% to 83.39% while reducing the number of parameters by up to 99.2%.

</details>


### [266] [Tensor-Train Weak SINDy: Identifying High-Dimensional Nonlinear Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09434)
*Will Houser,Vanja Dukic,David M. Bortz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TT-WSINDy方法，结合MANDy与WSINDy技术并利用张量列车（TT）格式，解决了高维动力学系统数据驱动发现中计算与内存开销过大的问题，有效避免了维数灾难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来弱形式方法在动力学系统的数据驱动发现中取得显著进展，但在高维设置下，现有技术面临巨大的计算成本和内存消耗挑战。

Method: 提出TT-WSINDy算法，整合多维非线性动力学近似（MANDy）与弱形式稀疏非线性动力学识别（WSINDy）方法，并在张量列车（TT）格式下执行必要计算。

Result: 该方法能够在候选函数空间呈指数增长的情况下，执行弱形式变换、回归和稀疏化操作，且未受维数灾难影响。

Conclusion: TT-WSINDy成功实现了高维动力学系统的高效数据驱动发现，克服了传统方法在高维场景下的计算与内存瓶颈。

Abstract: In recent years, weak-form methods have made significant advances in data-driven discovery of dynamical systems. However, in high-dimensional settings, current techniques can prove expensive in both computation and memory. In this work, we introduce TT-WSINDy, which combines techniques of the Multidimensional Approximation of Nonlinear Dynamics (MANDy) and Weak Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics (WSINDy) methods, implementing requisite computations in the tensor-train (TT) format. We demonstrate that this method is able to search an exponentially-growing space of candidate functions -- performing weak-form transformation, regression, and sparsification -- without suffering from the curse of dimensionality.

</details>


### [267] [Uncertainty-Aware Sea-Ice Type Mapping with Multiple Ice Charts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09451)
*Samira Alkaee Taleghan,Younghyun Koo,Andrew P. Barrett,Farnoush Banaei-Kashani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文量化了海冰发展阶段（SoD）映射中的两种不确定性来源：多标注者间的不一致性（标注不确定性）和模型自身的预测不确定性。研究发现，采用软监督策略能提升两者相关性，尤其在冰缘区域相关性显著增强，且蒙特卡洛Dropout提供了校准度最佳的置信度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的海冰发展阶段（SoD）自动映射方法通常将操作冰图作为确定性标签进行训练，但这忽略了因分析师判断差异和观测限制导致的“多标注者标签不确定性”。将多源标注坍缩为单一确定性目标会丢失关键的变化信息，且缺乏对模型自身不确定性的量化及其与标注不确定性之间关系的评估。

Method: 研究量化了来自独立冰务图表差异的标注不确定性和来自学习预测模型的模型不确定性。通过测试模型不确定性是否在冰务部门存在分歧的区域更高来评估两者的关系。对比了不同的监督策略（包括软监督）以及不同的不确定性估计方法（如蒙特卡洛Dropout）。

Result: 引入多标注者信息的监督策略改善了不确定性的对应关系，其中软监督实现了最高的整体相关性（0.256）。在冰缘附近（0-10公里范围内），这种关系显著增强，模型预测不确定性与多标注者分歧的相关性达到0.704。在不确定性估计方法中，蒙特卡洛Dropout提供了校准度最好的置信度估计，期望校准误差（ECE）为0.050。

Conclusion: 在海冰遥感分析中，显式地量化和利用多标注者标签不确定性至关重要。软监督方法能有效捕捉标注变异，且模型不确定性在数据模糊区域（如冰缘）能准确反映专家分歧，蒙特卡洛Dropout是此类任务中可靠的置信度校准工具。

Abstract: Sea-ice stage of development (SoD) describes the age and associated thickness of sea ice and provides important information for navigation, and operational ice monitoring. SoD labels are obtained from operational ice charts, where trained analysts interpret satellite observations and assign standardized stage codes to regions with similar ice conditions. These codes often represent ranges of compatible ice thicknesses rather than exact physical values. Deep-learning methods can automate SoD mapping and commonly adopt operational ice charts as reference labels for training. These annotations are not exact, however; this is because chart interpretation relies on analyst judgement and on the observations available at the time, so different ice services may assign different SoD labels to the same conditions. We term this variation across independently produced expert annotations multi-annotator label uncertainty; collapsing the annotations into a single deterministic target discards this variation. A second source of uncertainty originates in the learned model itself. In this paper, we quantify both sources: annotation uncertainty from disagreement among independent ice-service charts and model uncertainty from the learned predictive models. We then evaluate their relationship by testing whether model uncertainty is higher where ice services disagree. We observe that supervision incorporating information from multiple annotators can improve this correspondence, with soft supervision achieving the highest overall correlation of 0.256. The relationship becomes substantially stronger near the ice edge, where model predictive uncertainty closely tracks multi-annotator disagreement, reaching a correlation of 0.704 within 0--10 km. Among the uncertainty-estimation approaches, Monte Carlo dropout provides the best-calibrated confidence estimates, with an expected calibration error of 0.050.

</details>


### [268] [Exact-Form Regret for Gradient Descent, Mirror Descent and Follow-the-Regularized-Leader](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09466)
*Ashkan Soleymani,Gabriele Farina,Patrick Jaillet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“精确性（exactness）”作为在线梯度下降、镜像下降及FTRL等一阶方法实现无遗憾学习的统一几何原则，揭示了不同算法控制的偏差类别取决于其内在几何结构，并据此定义了“保守相关均衡”以描述博弈中的学习结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的在线梯度下降研究多基于外部遗憾（与固定策略比较），而近期研究表明一阶方法能控制更丰富的依赖于动作的偏差。现有文献缺乏对这些偏差类别的统一几何刻画，无法解释不同算法为何能控制不同的偏差类及其在博弈均衡中的具体含义。

Method: 引入微分几何中的“精确性”概念（即位移场由标量势生成或关联的一形式在算法特定几何下是精确的），分别针对梯度下降（欧几里得几何）、镜像下降（正则化诱导几何）和FTRL（累积对偶状态几何）进行分析。通过检验正则性条件下的精确性与非零环量（circulation）对遗憾界的影响，构建统一的几何框架，并推导由此产生的均衡概念。

Result: 1. 确定了“精确性”是无遗憾的充要几何条件：满足精确性导致次线性遗憾，而非零环量则导致线性遗憾。
2. 揭示了不同一阶方法控制的是本质上不同的偏差类别，这取决于算法所采用的几何结构。
3. 提出了“保守相关均衡（conservative correlated equilibrium）”概念，刻画了由精确形式偏差诱导的均衡性质，并明确了其与传统相关均衡的重合或分离条件。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个统一的几何框架，超越固定比较器，从几何角度解释了一阶在线学习算法在无遗憾学习中所控制的偏差类别，并阐明了这些偏差类别对博弈论中均衡概念的直接影响，深化了对算法几何结构与学习动态之间关系的理解。

Abstract: Online gradient descent is usually studied through external regret, where the learner competes with fixed alternatives. Recent work shows that first-order methods control richer action-dependent deviations. We ask for a geometric characterization of the deviations with respect to which online gradient descent, mirror descent, and follow-the-regularized-leader (FTRL) achieve no regret. We identify exactness as the common principle. Exactness means that the relevant displacement field is generated by a scalar potential, or equivalently that the associated one-form is exact in the geometry used by the algorithm. This geometry depends on the algorithm. For gradient descent it is Euclidean geometry, for mirror descent it is the geometry induced by the regularizer, and for FTRL it is the cumulative dual state. Under mild regularity conditions, exactness yields sublinear regret, while nonzero circulation provides the complementary obstruction and leads to linear regret. This gives a unified geometric framework for understanding the deviation classes controlled by these algorithms and reveals that different first-order methods can control genuinely different classes of deviations. These deviation classes have direct consequences for learning, particularly in games. We study the equilibrium notions induced by exact-form deviations and introduce conservative correlated equilibrium, reflecting both the conservative geometry of the underlying displacement fields and the restricted family of deviations available to the players. We characterize its relation to correlated equilibrium, determine when the resulting equilibrium notions coincide and when they separate, and show how these relationships depend on the geometry and the learning algorithm. Overall, this work gives a unified geometric account of what first-order online learning algorithms are no-regret with respect to, beyond fixed comparators.

</details>


### [269] [Muon-C: Operator-Aligned Muon for Convolutional Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09676)
*Jiaxin Qing,Lexin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Muon-C，一种针对卷积神经网络设计的算子对齐优化器，通过将动量表示为频域通道转移矩阵并利用临界傅里叶网格，解决了现有方法几何依赖矩阵表示的问题，在图像生成和分类任务中显著提升了收敛速度和最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Muon优化器用近似正交的极方向替换矩阵动量，但其几何结构依赖于矩阵表示；对于卷积操作，标准的展开（unfolding）方法描述的是局部补丁映射而非卷积算子本身，导致优化几何不匹配。

Method: 提出Muon-C优化器：1) 将核动量表示为逐频率的通道转移矩阵块；2) 独立对这些块进行极化；3) 利用临界傅里叶网格将更新精确返回到原始有限核支持域；4) 结合块划分和傅里叶坐标构建新的优化几何，使其成为临界采样卷积范数下的线性最小化预言机。

Result: 在CIFAR-10流匹配任务中，Muon-C在40k迭代后达到9.87 FID，优于展开版Muon的22.26和Adam的51.31；达到同等质量仅需前者0.62倍和后者0.64倍的FLOPs；在同等调优预算下FID降至3.42。该优势在不同数据规模下持续存在，并迁移至多种卷积架构的分类任务。

Conclusion: Muon-C通过构建与卷积算子对齐的新几何结构，在最坏情况保证上优于或等于展开方法（对3x3核严格更强），显著提升了卷积网络在生成和分类任务中的训练效率与性能。

Abstract: Muon replaces matrix momentum with an approximately orthogonal polar direction, but its geometry depends on the matrix representation. For convolution, standard unfolding describes a local patch map rather than the convolution operator. We introduce Muon-C, an operator-aligned optimizer that represents kernel momentum as frequency-wise channel-transfer matrices, polarizes these blocks independently, and uses a critical Fourier grid to return updates exactly to the original finite kernel support. We show that the new geometry arises from combining the block partition and Fourier coordinates. The exact-polar direction is a linear minimization oracle under the critically sampled convolution norm. Its worst-case guarantee relative to the continuous convolution-operator norm is never weaker than unfolding and is strictly stronger for $3\times3$ kernels. On CIFAR-10 flow matching with matched applied-update RMS, Muon-C reaches 9.87 FID at 40k iterations, compared with 22.26 for unfolded Muon and 51.31 for Adam. It reaches their final quality using $0.62\times$ and $0.64\times$ their model FLOPs, respectively. Under equal tuning budgets, Muon-C achieves 3.42 FID. Gains persist across data scales and transfer to classification across convolutional architectures.

</details>


### [270] [Building the Harness Automatically: Self-Play in Code Distills a Text Harness for Black-Box Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09468)
*Yi Wu,Zheng Ren,Zhiyu Hu,Haochen Wang,Daryl Chang,Li Wei,Ting Wang,Zhen Li,Pooja Gupta,Nitin Jindal,Lukasz Heldt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过可执行实践学习数值搜索策略并将其蒸馏为文本提示（Harness A/B）的方法，显著提升了语言模型在低预算黑盒优化任务中的性能，且该策略具有跨模型和跨任务的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在无辅助情况下，语言模型在低预算黑盒优化（BBO）任务中表现远逊于经典优化器的问题，探索是否可以通过“可执行实践”让代理自主学习搜索策略，并利用语言作为可移植的部署媒介。

Method: 代理在开发阶段反复编写并评估优化器程序，随后将生成的程序和实践记录蒸馏成一段固定的文本提示（Harness A）。该方法在独立测试集、不同语言模型（Gemini, Claude）及密封的生产基准（YouTube奖励调整）上进行评估，并进行了端到端的复现以生成Harness B。

Result: Harness A使Gemini Flash的后悔值（regret）降低了48%（p<.001），在练习族上达到GP-BO性能范围，并在三个保留的BBOB景观上降低了平均后悔值。该文本策略成功迁移至其他Gemini执行器及Claude Sonnet（后悔值分别降低43%和49%）。复现产生的Harness B达到了同等性能水平，且该框架在YouTube基准上取得了最低后悔值。

Conclusion: 可执行实践是发现有效搜索策略的可行途径，而自然语言则是部署这些策略的可移植媒介，证明了通过实践学习并转化为文本提示能显著提升语言模型在优化任务中的表现。

Abstract: Can an agent learn a numerical search strategy through executable practice and then transfer that strategy as text? We study low-budget black-box optimization, where unaided language models remain well below strong classical optimizers. During development, an agent repeatedly writes and evaluates optimizer programs. It then distills the resulting program and practice record once into a 197-word primary Harness A, which is frozen before evaluation. Harness A reduces Gemini Flash regret by 48\% in an independent $N=30$ study ($p<.001$), enters the GP-BO performance range on the practice family, and lowers mean regret on all three held-out BBOB landscapes. The same text improves every tested Gemini executor and transfers to Claude Sonnet, reducing regret by 43\% and 49\% ($p\leq.005$). An independent end-to-end replication produces Harness B, a different program and text at the same performance tier. The same framework also attains the lowest regret on a sealed YouTube reward-tuning production benchmark. Executable practice is thus a viable way to discover a search policy, and language a portable medium for deploying it.

</details>


### [271] [Beyond Conventional Federated Learning via High-Order Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09904)
*Alireza Kabgani,Masoud Ahookhosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HiFedProx，用幂律正则化（指数$p\geq2$）替代FedProx的二次正则化，以更好控制联邦学习中客户端参数位移的幅度差异，实验表明在复合压力下$p=5$至$7$能显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FedProx的二次正则化随位移线性增长，难以有效控制普通客户端与异常大位移客户端之间的对比度，导致在客户端优化步数不一时参数位移幅度差异过大，影响模型收敛与稳定性。

Method: 提出HiFedProx，引入指数$p\geq2$的幂律正则化项，在参考位移$R$处保持正则化梯度幅值一致；$p>2$时在$R$以下响应更弱、以上更强。结合有限预算的随机客户端优化与同小批量Armijo回溯线搜索，在冻结的60写手FEMNIST子集上进行多种子实验，测试$p\in\{2,3,4,5,6,7,8\}$的表现。

Result: 在清洁训练下各$p$值表现相近，但在复合压力下，$p=7$和$p=6$分别在中度和重度压力下比$p=2$损失降低11.44%和23.16%；位移尾部比率随$p$增大持续下降，但预测性能在中间范围（$p=5$–$7$）达到峰值，且Armijo试错成本随$p$增加而上升。

Conclusion: 正则化指数$p$不应盲目最大化，而需校准；实验表明$p=5$至$7$为最优范围，能在压缩位移差异的同时平衡预测性能与计算成本，显著提升联邦学习在压力场景下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Federated clients that perform several local optimization steps can return parameter displacements with widely different magnitudes. The quadratic regularization of FedProx grows linearly with displacement and therefore offers limited control over the contrast between ordinary and unusually large client movements. We here introduce HiFedProx, which replaces the quadratic penalty with a scale-matched power-type regularizer indexed by $p\geq2$. All powers have the same regularization-gradient magnitude at a reference displacement $R$, while every $p>2$ gives a weaker response below $R$ and a stronger response above it. An exact affine reference calculation shows that increasing $p$ compresses relative displacement disparities, although very large powers approach fixed-radius behavior and increase local curvature. HiFedProx combines this geometry with finite-budget stochastic client optimization and same-minibatch Armijo backtracking. In paired five-seed experiments on a frozen 60-writer FEMNIST subset, a common-parameter study over $p\in\{2,3,4,5,6,7,8\}$ shows similar clean-training performance but substantial gains under composite stress. The lowest moderate- and severe-stress losses occur at $p=7$ and $p=6$, improving over $p=2$ by $11.44\%$ and $23.16\%$, respectively. Although displacement-tail ratios continue to decrease through $p=8$, predictive performance peaks in an intermediate range and Armijo trial cost increases with $p$. These results indicate that the exponent should be calibrated rather than maximized. In our experiments, $p=5$--$7$ provides the most useful range.

</details>


### [272] [From Fixed Keys to Readable Schemas: Small Language Models for Vehicle Agent Function Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09476)
*Hamed Jafarzadeh Asl,Yuanhao Yu,Vahid Partovi Nia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比了车载小语言模型（SLM）中两种函数表示方法：功能令牌（FT）与提示中嵌入模式（SIP）。研究发现，FT推理快但无法泛化至未见函数且易错误调用；SIP虽开销大，但能泛化至新函数并更可靠地拒绝无关请求。实验表明，函数表示方式比模型规模更能决定系统能力与失效模式，最佳性能出现在0.6B模型配合SIP时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载助手需在严格内存与延迟约束下，将自然语言请求准确转化为车辆函数调用。小语言模型（SLM）适合端侧部署，但其函数接口的表示方式（专用功能令牌 vs. 提示中直接提供函数模式）对泛化性、推理效率及安全性有决定性影响，亟需系统评估以指导实际部署。

Method: 构建包含9,822个单轮样本的基准数据集，覆盖79个源自Android Automotive的车辆函数，包含持出函数（held-out functions）和需拒绝的域外请求。在四种参数量（270M至1.7B）的SLM上，对功能令牌（FT）和提示中嵌入模式（SIP）两种方法进行匹配的微调实验，并辅以理论分析解释SIP的泛化机制与推理成本来源。

Result: 在训练所见函数上，模型扩展收益有限，270M模型性能可媲美1.7B模型，整体最优表现出现在0.6B模型；在持出函数上，FT准确率为零，而SIP随模型规模提升显著泛化；在域外请求上，FT易错误调用已训练函数，SIP则能基于当前提供函数可靠拒绝。SIP的灵活性以更高内存占用和延迟为代价。

Conclusion: 对于基于SLM的车载函数调用系统，函数表面的表示方式（FT vs. SIP）而非单纯模型规模，是决定其泛化能力、安全拒绝机制及失效模式的关键因素。实际部署需在推理效率与功能灵活性之间权衡，SIP更适合需支持动态函数扩展的场景。

Abstract: In-vehicle assistants must translate natural-language requests into accurate vehicle function calls under strict memory and latency constraints, making small language models (SLMs) attractive for on-device deployment. For such models, a key design choice is how the available function surface is presented. Two approaches are to represent each function with a dedicated Functional Token (FT) or provide function schemas directly in the prompt. FTs enable compact inference but are restricted to functions learned during training, whereas Schema-in-Prompt (SIP) can generalize to unseen functions at the cost of longer prompts and higher inference overhead. We introduce a benchmark of 9,822 single-turn examples spanning 79 vehicle functions derived from Android Automotive, including held-out functions and requests requiring refusal. We compare both approaches under matched fine-tuning across four SLMs from 270M to 1.7B parameters. On functions seen during training, scaling provides limited benefit: the 270M model can match the 1.7B model, while the strongest overall performance occurs at 0.6B. On held-out functions, FT achieves zero accuracy by construction, whereas SIP generalizes and improves substantially with scale. On out-of-scope requests, FT can invoke an unavailable function it was trained to emit, while SIP more reliably refuses based on the functions offered. This flexibility comes with higher memory use and latency. Our theoretical analysis explains how SIP enables generalization and why longer schema contexts increase inference cost. Overall, function-surface representation, rather than model scale alone, determines the capabilities and failure modes of SLM-based vehicle function calling.

</details>


### [273] [Adversarial Training for Tabular Credit Scoring: A Multi-Attack Robustness Evaluation in P2P Lending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09945)
*Gijs A. F. Niewzwaag,Marijn G. S. Veth,Manuele Massei,Marcos R. Machado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过系统性基准测试发现，针对单一攻击的对抗训练在P2P信贷评分中泛化能力有限，而混合攻击训练能显著提升模型面对异构攻击时的鲁棒性，同时保持正常测试性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗鲁棒性研究多集中于图像和文本领域，且通常仅评估单一攻击与对应防御的匹配效果，缺乏针对表格型信贷数据中防御措施跨攻击类型泛化能力的指导，难以应对借款人策略性篡改自报输入以获取有利决策的现实风险。

Method: 在大型Lending Club子集上构建系统的训练 - 测试鲁棒性基准，涵盖三种模型家族（逻辑回归、前馈神经网络、表格数据Transformer）和四种针对申请人可变特征的攻击（FGSM、PGD、盐胡椒噪声、DeepFool）及混合攻击机制，采用分层交叉验证进行全网格评估。

Result: 对抗训练显著提升了对训练时所用攻击的鲁棒性，并在基于梯度的攻击家族内具有良好的迁移性，但对非梯度腐蚀攻击的迁移效果较弱；单一攻击防御高估了实际场景中的鲁棒性，而混合训练在异构攻击下实现了最均衡的鲁棒性，且未牺牲干净测试集上的性能。

Conclusion: 在信贷模型治理中应推行多攻击压力测试，采用混合攻击训练策略以构建更具实际韧性的机器学习信贷评分系统，避免单一防御策略带来的虚假安全感。

Abstract: Machine learning-based credit scoring is increasingly central to Peer-to-Peer (P2P) lending, yet its resilience to adversarial manipulation, where applicants strategically alter self-reported inputs to secure favourable decisions, remains poorly understood. Most adversarial-robustness evidence comes from image and text domains and evaluates a single attack against a matching defence, offering little guidance on how defences generalise across attack types in tabular credit data. We address this with a systematic train-test robustness benchmark on a large Lending Club subset, spanning three model families (logistic regression, a feed-forward neural network, and a transformer for tabular data) and four attacks confined to applicant-mutable features: Fast Gradient Sign Method (FGSM), Projected Gradient Descent (PGD), Salt-and-Pepper (S&P) noise, and DeepFool, plus a mixed-attack regime. Across a full grid evaluated with stratified cross-validation, adversarial training sharply improves robustness against the attack it is trained on and transfers well within the gradient-based family, but transfers weakly to non-gradient corruption, so single-attack defences overstate real-world resilience. Mixed training delivers the most balanced robustness across heterogeneous attacks while preserving clean-test performance, supporting multi-attack stress testing in credit-model governance.

</details>


### [274] [Unthrottling the Tanh Jacobian in SAC: A Negative Result on Bang-Bang Control and MetaDrive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09478)
*Faiq Shamass*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了SAC算法中tanh激活函数导致的梯度消失问题，并提出了一种最小干预的修复方案，但在多个任务上的实验表明，该方案不仅未能提升性能，反而导致策略崩溃或性能下降，证明该“梯度消失”现象并非需要修复的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAC使用tanh将无界高斯策略映射到有界动作空间，其雅可比行列式在动作趋近边界时趋于零。这引发了一个担忧：在需要极端动作（如全刹车或全油门）的最优解处，这种梯度消失会阻断演员（Actor）获取评论家（Critic）的信号。

Method: 提出一种最小干预方案：在演员损失函数中增加一项，该项对tanh前均值的梯度为分离（detached）的Q值动作梯度，且无增益参数。实验在最小时间双积分器（bang-bang控制最优）和MetaDrive微调任务上进行，测试了无门控旁路和门控旁路两种变体。

Result: 在双积分器任务中，原始SAC已接近最优回报（-31.6 vs -30.3）；无门控旁路导致策略饱和（99%步骤动作绝对值≥0.9）且回报崩溃至-195.5；门控旁路同样失败。在MetaDrive中，旁路未提升回报，碰撞率降低往往以增加驶出道路为代价。此外，自动调节的熵系数对抗旁路，推动策略向尾部探索。

Conclusion: tanh导致的雅可比效应确实存在，但将其视为需要修复的缺陷是错误的。在该研究的任务中，修复该效应不仅无益反而有害。达到动作边界并不等同于解决了最优解位于边界的问题。

Abstract: Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) represents a continuous policy as an unbounded Gaussian that is squashed by tanh. The Jacobian of that map is $\partial a/\partial u = 1-a^2$, which vanishes as $|a|\to 1$. A natural concern is that this throttle starves the actor of critic signal exactly where extreme actions (full brake, full throttle) are optimal. We test a minimal intervention that restores the missing signal: one extra term in the actor loss whose gradient on the pre-tanh mean is the detached action-gradient of $Q$, with no gain parameter.
  On a minimum-time double integrator whose optimum is bang-bang at the action bounds, vanilla SAC already reaches near-optimal return ($-31.6$ vs. a calibrated optimum of $-30.3$) across ten paired seeds. An ungated bypass does saturate the policy (99% of eval steps with $|a|\ge 0.9$) and collapses return to $-195.5$. A gated bypass that fires only on the flat shoulder $|a|\in[0.9,0.999]$ also fails, and does so without leaving a saturated policy. Warm-started MetaDrive fine-tuning shows the same pattern: the bypass does not improve return, and where collision rate falls it is typically traded for out-of-road departures. Auto-tuned entropy coefficient rises against the bypass, which is a push toward the tails.
  The Jacobian effect is real. Treating it as a bug to be undone is not free, and on the tasks studied here it is not helpful. Saturating a bound is not the same as solving a problem whose optimum lives on that bound.

</details>


### [275] [An Exponential Deterministic--Randomized Gap in ERM-Oracle Complexity for Thresholds on an Unknown Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10196)
*Xuan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在特定一致性ERM预言机（如最小前缀规则）下，随机化算法能显著减少在线学习中所需的预言机调用次数，而确定性算法则需线性次调用；但在其他预言机规则（如可行中位数规则）下，确定性算法同样高效，表明性能差异源于预言机选择规则而非问题类别本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应 Attias 和 Ramaswami (NeurIPS 2025) 提出的关于随机化是否能在大类仅通过预言机访问时减少在线学习预言机调用的问题，具体研究未知全序上阈值的传递性在线学习场景。

Method: 通过分析不同一致性ERM预言机规则（最小/最大前缀规则、可行中位数规则、全局中位数规则）下确定性学习器与随机化学习器的表现，构建硬分布实例，推导错误次数与调用次数的下界与上界，并对比弱一致性预言机接口。

Result: 1. 在最小/最大前缀规则下，确定性学习器错误数M与调用数Q满足$M+Q\ge T-\varepsilon$，导致$O(\log T)$错误需$T-\varepsilon-O(\log T)$次调用；而随机化学习器仅需$O(\log T)$期望调用和错误。
2. 随机化顺序是最优的：在该规则下，任何学习器若要实现多项式对数级错误，必须至少进行$\Omega(\log T)$次期望调用。
3. 分离现象由预言机选择规则决定：可行中位数规则下确定性学习器可达$O(\log T)$复杂度，而全局中位数规则再次导致线性成本。
4. 若仅使用返回可实现性比特的弱一致性预言机，确定性和随机化学习器均需$\Theta(T)$次调用。

Conclusion: 随机化在在线学习中减少预言机调用的优势并非普适，而是高度依赖于预言机的具体选择规则；对于某些自然规则，随机化能实现指数级加速，而对于其他规则或弱接口，确定性算法已足够或两者均受限。

Abstract: Attias, Hanneke and Ramaswami (NeurIPS 2025) asked whether randomization provably reduces the oracle calls needed for online learning when the class is accessible only through an oracle. We study the instance they singled out: transductive online learning of thresholds on an unknown total order of T instances, with a consistency-type ERM oracle that returns a full concept consistent with a queried labeled set (or reports non-realizability). Our main result is a separation for a fixed natural oracle. When the oracle is the minimal-prefix rule (or the maximal-prefix rule), every deterministic learner makes M mistakes and Q calls with $M+Q\ge T-\varepsilon$ on some instance ($\varepsilon\in\{0,1\}$, according to whether the empty prefix is a concept), and the constant is exact; hence $O(\log T)$ mistakes cost $T-\varepsilon-O(\log T)$ calls, whereas that paper's randomized learner achieves $O(\log T)$ expected calls and mistakes under the same rule. The randomized order is optimal: on an explicit hard distribution under the minimal-prefix rule, every learner has expected mistakes at least $((T+1-\varepsilon)\,128^{-\mathbb{E}[Q]}-1)/2$, so $Ω(\log T)$ expected calls are necessary for polylogarithmic mistakes. The separation is governed by the oracle's selection rule, not by the class alone: for a legal feasible-median ERM rule a deterministic learner achieves $O(\log T)$ calls and mistakes, while a global-median rule again forces linear total cost. The same linear bound holds when the oracle's answers are chosen adversarially and then frozen into a memoryless oracle. We add partial tradeoff results for fixed query budgets (the middle regime is open) and an interface contrast: with only a weak consistency oracle, returning a realizability bit, both deterministic and randomized learners need $Θ(T)$ calls.

</details>


### [276] [Efficient Fairness Auditing Across Guidance Scales in Text-to-Image Diffusion Models via Causal Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09486)
*Nabila Tasfiha Rahman,Rajatsubhra Chakraborty,Depeng Xu,Lu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于因果抽象的审计工具，通过构建高层结构因果模型并利用概率Transformer预测目标特征分布，从而在不生成大量图像的情况下，高效评估文本到图像扩散模型在不同分类器自由引导尺度下的公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本到图像扩散模型公平性审计通常需要跨多种采样配置生成大量图像，导致计算成本极其高昂，难以进行全面的评估。

Method: 将扩散过程表示为低层结构因果模型，并构建对应于抽象去噪状态的高层模型；刻画投影因果结构，确立公平性相关干预查询的可识别性，并提供高层模型保留该查询的充分条件；利用概率Transformer实现高层模型，作为跨引导尺度的目标特征分布的摊销预测器。

Result: 实验评估了分布保真度、公平性查询准确性和计算效率，并通过对标准Stable Diffusion 1.5和公平性增强模型StayFair的审计演示，验证了该方法在不同引导尺度下分析模型行为的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过因果抽象和摊销预测，显著降低了公平性审计的计算开销，同时保持了对干预查询的准确评估能力，为扩散模型的公平性分析提供了高效工具。

Abstract: Fairness auditing of text-to-image diffusion models often requires generating large numbers of images across sampling configurations, making comprehensive evaluation computationally expensive. We propose a causal-abstraction-based audit instrument for efficiently evaluating fairness under interventions on the classifier-free guidance scale. Given a fixed prompt and a target feature function, we represent the diffusion process as a low-level structural causal model and construct a corresponding high-level model over abstract denoising states. We characterize the projected causal structure, establish identifiability of the fairness-relevant interventional query, and provide sufficient conditions under which the high-level model preserves this query. A probabilistic transformer implements the high-level model as an amortized predictor of target-feature distributions across guidance scales. Experiments evaluate distributional fidelity, fairness-query accuracy, and computational efficiency. We present two auditing demonstrations: one using standard Stable Diffusion 1.5 and another using StayFair, a fairness-enhanced Stable Diffusion model, to examine their behavior across guidance scales.

</details>


### [277] [A positive resolution of the gap-entropy conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10529)
*P. M. Aronow,Nathan Kallus,Patrick Lopatto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在固定置信度下，针对独立单位方差高斯臂的最佳臂识别问题，其样本复杂度下界与 $H(\log(1/\delta) + \mathrm{Ent}(I))$ 成正比，并提出了达到该界限的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决最佳臂识别（Best-Arm Identification）中关于“间隙熵猜想”（gap-entropy conjecture）的遗留问题，旨在精确刻画在高斯分布假设下，识别唯一最优臂所需的最小期望样本数与臂间差距分布（即间隙熵）之间的理论关系。

Method: 通过定义间隙 $\Delta_i$ 和复杂度参数 $H=\sum \Delta_i^{-2}$，引入基于间隙分布的熵项 $\mathrm{Ent}(I)$ 来量化难度。研究者在所有臂标签排列平均的意义下，推导了满足置信度 $1-\delta$ 的算法的期望样本数下界，并构造了一种不依赖于具体实例的通用算法来验证该上界。

Result: 证明了最优期望样本数在绝对常数因子内等于 $H(\log(1/\delta)+\mathrm{Ent}(I))$。同时，提出了一种通用算法，其期望样本数由上述主项加上一个依赖于最小间隙 $g$ 的低阶项 $g^{-2}\log\log(e^e/g)$ 所界定。

Conclusion: 该研究正式确立了固定置信度最佳臂识别问题的间隙熵猜想，表明样本复杂度不仅取决于传统的 $H$ 参数，还显著受限于臂间隙分布的熵，为理解该问题的内在难度提供了精确的理论界限。

Abstract: We prove the gap-entropy conjecture for fixed-confidence best-arm identification with independent unit-variance Gaussian arms, means in $[0,1]$, and a unique optimal arm. For each suboptimal arm $i$, let $Δ_i=μ_*-μ_i$ be its gap from the optimal mean, and write $H=\sum_{i\ne *}Δ_i^{-2}$. Let $p_r$ be the fraction of $H$ contributed by arms with $2^{-(r+1)}<Δ_i\le2^{-r}$, and let $\mathrm{Ent}(I)=\sum_{r:p_r>0} p_r\log(1/p_r)$. Among all algorithms that identify the optimal arm with probability at least $1-δ$ on every Gaussian instance, the optimal expected number of samples on a given instance, averaged over all permutations of the arm labels, is within absolute constant factors of $H(\log(1/δ)+\mathrm{Ent}(I))$. Moreover, there is an algorithm, independent of the instance, whose expected number of samples is bounded by a constant multiple of this quantity plus $g^{-2}\log\log(e^e/g)$, where $g=\min_{i\ne *}Δ_i$ is the gap to the closest competitor.

</details>


### [278] [A Statistical Approach to Estimating Sample Size of Machine Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09547)
*Dat Phan-Trong,Sunil Gupta,Svetha Venkatesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新框架，通过将非线性机器学习模型近似为局部线性表示，并在这些局部区域评估统计功效，从而解决了非线性机器学习模型样本量确定的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的功效分析通常要求预先指定预测变量与结果之间的关系及效应结构，这对于学习复杂预测曲面且无法进行直接解析功效计算的非线性机器学习模型来说，使得样本量确定极具挑战性。

Method: 提出一种框架，利用局部线性表示来近似非线性机器学习模型，并通过评估这些局部区域的统计功效来估算所需的样本量。

Result: 该框架能够为非线性机器学习模型提供样本量需求的估算，克服了传统方法无法直接应用于复杂非线性模型的局限。

Conclusion: 通过局部线性近似和区域功效评估，该方法为非线性机器学习预测模型的样本量确定提供了一种可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Sample size determination for machine learning (ML) prediction models is challenging because conventional power analysis typically requires the predictor-outcome relationship and effect structure to be specified a priori. Nonlinear ML models learn complex prediction surfaces that do not admit straightforward analytical power calculations. We propose a framework that approximates nonlinear ML models with localized linear representations and estimates sample size requirements by evaluating statistical power across these local regions.

</details>


### [279] [Robust Industrial Cyber Physical Classification Using Neuromorphic Temporal Embeddings and Hybrid SNN XGBoost Under Machine Unlearning Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09564)
*Ammar Kamoona,Sajad Koushkbaghi,Mahdi Jalili,Peter McTaggart,Xinghuo Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合脉冲神经网络（SNN）与XGBoost的架构，用于电力分配网络的入侵检测。该方法利用SNN作为固定特征提取器，仅重训练XGBoost分类器，在实现高精度检测（Synchrophasor数据集99.9%，MSU/ORNL数据集95.0%）的同时，显著提升了针对数据投毒和机器遗忘攻击的结构韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力分配网络的数字化增加了网络攻击风险，但现有的入侵检测系统（IDS）依赖计算昂贵的深度学习模型，难以在边缘端部署。此外，周期性重训练使这些系统易受机器遗忘攻击影响，导致检测性能下降。

Method: 提出一种混合SNN与XGBoost架构：SNN在干净数据上一次性训练并作为固定的时间编码特征提取器，仅轻量级的XGBoost分类器在模型更新时进行重训练。该方法结合了高效的时间编码与结构韧性。

Result: 在两个真实世界电力系统数据集上表现优异：Synchrophasor数据集准确率达99.9%（F1-macro 0.999），MSU/ORNL数据集达95.0%（F1-macro 0.943），优于独立基线模型。在10%标签翻转攻击下，F1-macro仅损失0.9%；并将目标类崩溃的阈值从60%推迟至70%。

Conclusion: 神经形态时间编码不仅能提供高精度的网络攻击检测，还能显著增强 cyber-physical 系统对数据投毒攻击的韧性，解决了边缘部署困难和模型更新脆弱性的问题。

Abstract: The digitalisation of electrical distribution networks has increased the exposure of power-grid infrastructure to cyber attacks. Existing intrusion detection systems (IDSs), however, often rely on computationally expensive deep learning models that are difficult to deploy at the edge. Periodic retraining also exposes these systems to machine unlearning attacks, where selective data removal can degrade detection performance. We propose a hybrid Spiking Neural Network (SNN) and XGBoost architecture that combines efficient temporal encoding with a lightweight classifier and provides structural resilience to such attacks. The SNN is trained once on clean data and used as a fixed feature extractor, while only the XGBoost classifier is retrained during model updates. Evaluated on two real-world public power-system datasets, the proposed method achieves 99.9\% accuracy (F1-macro 0.999) on the Synchrophasor dataset and 95.0\% accuracy (F1-macro 0.943) on the MSU/ORNL dataset, outperforming standalone baselines. Under selective label-flipping attacks, the hybrid model loses only 0.9\% F1-macro at 10\% poisoning and delays target-class collapse from 60\% to 70\% poisoning compared with raw models. These results demonstrate that neuromorphic temporal encoding can provide both accurate cyber-attack detection and improved resilience to data poisoning in cyber-physical systems.

</details>


### [280] [Positional task conditioning for scalable defect detection across product families in large product catalogs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09567)
*Soham Satyadharma,Gabriel Roccabruna,Suleiman A. Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将大型产品目录中的错误检测任务分解为聚焦子任务的方法，并引入了位置任务条件（PTC）技术，将大模型的能力蒸馏到小模型中，在大幅降低成本的同时显著提升了检测准确率（F1从52%提升至87%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型产品目录中存在重复和单位不匹配等不一致问题，严重影响客户体验。由于产品列表冗长且错误类型多样，直接利用大语言模型（LLM）进行分类会受限于长上下文能力，导致分类质量下降。

Method: 1. 任务分解：将检测过程分解为聚焦的子任务，以减少上下文长度并隔离错误类型。
2. 位置任务条件（PTC）：一种新的蒸馏技术，通过在结构提示边界强化任务身份，将上述能力蒸馏到单个较小的模型中，以实现可扩展的部署。

Result: 1. 任务分解使F1分数从52%提升至87%。
2. PTC在五种模型和两个架构家族上的表现优于基于原理的蒸馏方法。
3. PTC模型的性能与最先进模型仅相差1.79% F1，但成本降低了高达98%。
4. 该系统已在多个国家部署，处理超过1000万个产品家族。

Conclusion: 通过任务分解和位置任务条件（PTC）蒸馏技术，有效解决了长上下文导致的LLM性能下降问题，实现了高精度、低成本且可扩展的产品不一致性检测系统。

Abstract: Product families in large product catalogs suffer from inconsistencies such as duplicates and unit mismatches that degrade customer experience. Detecting these requires reasoning over multiple error types across lengthy product listings, where LLM classification quality degrades due to long-context limitations. We address this by decomposing detection into focused sub-tasks that reduce context and isolate error types, improving F1 from 52\% to 87\%. For scalable deployment, we introduce Positional Task Conditioning (PTC), which distills this capability into a single smaller model by reinforcing task identity at structural prompt boundaries. PTC outperforms rationale-based distillation across five models and two architecture families, achieving within 1.79\% F1 of the frontier at upto 98\% lower cost. Our system is deployed across multiple countries processing 10+ million product families.

</details>


### [281] [Teacher Geometry Shapes Learnability in Teacher-Student Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09595)
*Kai J. Sandbrink,Flavio Martinelli,Alexander van Meegen,Wulfram Gerstner,Johanni Brea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出教师 - 学生系统中常被忽视的教师网络结构对可学习性的显著影响，通过定义“可学习性”为收敛至全局最小值的成功率，揭示了教师节点相似性分布（最大差异 vs 最小差异）如何导致损失景观中次优局部极小值（界内 vs 界外）分布的差异，并提出通过差异化调整读出头与内层偏置的学习率来提升收敛成功率的优化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常假设教师网络参数为随机正态分布，忽略了教师结构本身对可学习性的巨大影响，导致理论分析与实际结构化函数之间存在差距。

Method: 1. 形式化定义可学习性为收敛成功率，关联过参数化、算法、初始化分布及教师几何结构；2. 构造最大化与最小化节点差异的两种教师分布；3. 在无限数据与快速读出头假设下，将小网络损失景观解析降维至二维，分析教师结构对吸引域的影响；4. 在大网络中验证不同教师结构诱导的局部极小值类型差异；5. 提出差异化学习率调整策略（提高读出头学习率，降低内层偏置学习率）。

Result: 1. 最大节点差异的教师分布显著降低界外（OOB）局部极小值比例，提升收敛成功率；2. 最小节点差异的教师分布诱导更多 OOB 极小值，导致收敛困难；3. 损失景观分析表明教师结构直接改变界内极小值的吸引域范围；4. 差异化学习率策略在多种激活函数与设置下均有效提升收敛成功率。

Conclusion: 教师网络的几何结构是决定可学习性的关键因素，通过理解其对损失景观的影响并设计针对性优化策略（如差异化学习率），可显著缩小理论模型与实际应用结构化函数之间的差距，为深度学习理论提供更贴近实践的洞察。

Abstract: Teacher-student systems, in which a teacher neural network generates training labels so that a student neural network can learn to implement the same function, are widely used as an abstract setting to study learning. However, the structure of the teachers is often overlooked by assuming randomly-generated, normally-distributed parameters. This hides substantial variation in how learnable different teachers are. We formalize learnability as the success rate of converging to the global minimum, as a function of overparameterization, learning algorithm, student initialization distribution, and teacher geometry. We both identify an easy distribution that maximizes node dissimilarity and a hard distribution that minimizes it, and show that these two distributions induce markedly different success rates across a large range of settings and for different activation functions. To explain the gap, we study the loss landscape of small neural networks that contain two distinct kinds of suboptimal local minima, out-of-bounds (OOB) minima at the edge of the data distribution and interior minima within. Assuming infinite data and a fast readout layer, we analytically reduce the loss landscape of small networks to two dimensions, showing that the region of attraction of interior minima changes as a function of teacher structure. In larger networks, maximally dissimilar teachers induce more interior minima, while minimally dissimilar teachers induce more OOB minima. Motivated by these analyses, we show that differentially increasing the learning rate of the readout layer and decreasing the learning rate of the inner biases increases success rates. These findings provide an important step in narrowing the gap between the study of teacher-student networks and more structured functions that arise in practice.

</details>
